/**
 * Fake wrist/finger pulse device: one sample per second, with eight seconds of
 * lost contact every minute. It only posts what a sensor could observe; the
 * server decides quality.
 */
const API_URL = process.env.API_URL ?? "http://127.0.0.1:4000";
const DEVICE_KEY = process.env.DEVICE_KEY ?? "device-demo-key";
const DEVICE_ID = process.env.DEVICE_ID ?? "pulse-01";
const CREW_ID = process.env.CREW_ID ?? "alex-morgan";

const started = Date.now();
let contactLostUntil = 0;
let nextLossAt = started + 60_000;

function sample(): { value: number; contact: "good" | "none" } {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now < contactLostUntil) {
    return { value: 0, contact: "none" };
  }
  // Simulated contact loss: 8 s once a minute.
  if (now >= nextLossAt) {
    contactLostUntil = now + 8000;
    nextLossAt = now + 68_000;
    console.log("simulating 8 s of contact loss");
    return { value: 0, contact: "none" };
  }
  const elapsed = (now - started) / 1000;
  const drift = Math.sin(elapsed / 7) * 4;
  const value = Math.round(72 + drift + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4);
  return { value, contact: "good" };
}

async function postHeartRate() {
  const next = sample();
  const body = {
    deviceId: DEVICE_ID,
    crewId: CREW_ID,
    metric: "heart_rate",
    value: next.value,
    observedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    contact: next.contact,
  };
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/v1/ingest/sample`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        authorization: `Bearer ${DEVICE_KEY}`,
        "content-type": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    if (!response.ok) {
      const text = await response.text();
      console.error(`sample rejected ${response.status}: ${text}`);
    } else if (next.contact === "none") {
      console.log("contact lost sample sent");
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`cannot reach backend at ${API_URL}`, error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
  }
}

console.log(`fake device ${DEVICE_ID} -> ${API_URL} as ${CREW_ID}`);
setInterval(() => {
  void postHeartRate();
}, 1000);

import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import type { Db } from "./db.js";

/**
 * Append-only audit chain. Each row hashes the previous hash plus the row's
 * canonical JSON, so editing any earlier row breaks every later hash.
 */
export type AuditRow = {
  id: number;
  at: string;
  actor: string;
  action: string;
  ref: string;
  detail_json: string;
  prev_hash: string;
  hash: string;
};

export function canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalJson).join(",")}]`;
  const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
    .filter(([, item]) => item !== undefined)
    .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
  return `{${entries.map(([key, item]) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalJson(item)}`).join(",")}}`;
}

function hashFor(prevHash: string, row: Omit<AuditRow, "id" | "hash" | "prev_hash">): string {
  return createHash("sha256")
    .update(prevHash + canonicalJson(row))
    .digest("hex");
}

export function appendAudit(
  db: Db,
  actor: string,
  action: string,
  ref: string,
  detail: unknown = {},
  at: string = new Date().toISOString(),
): void {
  const last = db
    .prepare("SELECT hash FROM audit_log ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1")
    .get() as { hash: string } | undefined;
  const prevHash = last?.hash ?? "";
  const row = {
    at,
    actor,
    action,
    ref,
    detail_json: canonicalJson(detail),
  };
  const hash = hashFor(prevHash, row);
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO audit_log (at, actor, action, ref, detail_json, prev_hash, hash)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
  ).run(row.at, row.actor, row.action, row.ref, row.detail_json, prevHash, hash);
}

export type VerifyResult = {
  ok: boolean;
  checked: number;
  firstBrokenId: number | null;
  expected: string | null;
  actual: string | null;
};

export function verifyAudit(db: Db): VerifyResult {
  const rows = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM audit_log ORDER BY id ASC").all() as AuditRow[];
  let prev = "";
  for (const row of rows) {
    const expected = hashFor(prev, {
      at: row.at,
      actor: row.actor,
      action: row.action,
      ref: row.ref,
      detail_json: row.detail_json,
    });
    if (row.prev_hash !== prev || row.hash !== expected) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        checked: rows.length,
        firstBrokenId: row.id,
        expected,
        actual: row.hash,
      };
    }
    prev = row.hash;
  }
  return { ok: true, checked: rows.length, firstBrokenId: null, expected: null, actual: null };
}

import type { FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from "fastify";
import { ApiError } from "./errors.js";
import type { Ctx } from "./context.js";
import type { Role } from "./types.js";

/**
 * Three shared keys map to three roles. Anything else is 401.
 * The SSE route accepts the key as a query token only because EventSource
 * cannot set headers.
 */
export function roleFor(ctx: Ctx, request: FastifyRequest): Role | null {
  const header = request.headers.authorization;
  const bearer = header?.startsWith("Bearer ") ? header.slice(7) : null;
  // EventSource cannot set headers, so the stream route alone accepts ?token=.
  const query = request.url.startsWith("/v1/stream")
    ? ((request.query as { token?: string } | undefined)?.token ?? null)
    : null;
  const token = bearer ?? query;
  if (!token) return null;
  if (token === ctx.config.crewKey) return "crew";
  if (token === ctx.config.groundKey) return "ground";
  if (token === ctx.config.deviceKey) return "device";
  return null;
}

export function requireRole(
  ctx: Ctx,
  request: FastifyRequest,
  allowed: Role[],
): Role {
  const role = roleFor(ctx, request);
  if (!role) {
    throw new ApiError(401, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Send Authorization: Bearer <key> with a valid key.");
  }
  if (!allowed.includes(role)) {
    throw new ApiError(403, "FORBIDDEN", "This key is not allowed to use this route.");
  }
  return role;
}

export function unauthorized(reply: FastifyReply) {
  return reply
    .status(401)
    .send({ error: { code: "UNAUTHORIZED", message: "A valid API key is required." } });
}

import { z } from "zod";

export type Config = {
  port: number;
  host: string;
  dbPath: string;
  corsOrigin: string;
  crewKey: string;
  groundKey: string;
  deviceKey: string;
  delayScale: number;
};

const envSchema = z.object({
  PORT: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(65_535).default(4000),
  HOST: z.string().default("127.0.0.1"),
  DB_PATH: z.string().default("./data/crewsignal.db"),
  CORS_ORIGIN: z.string().default("http://localhost:3000"),
  CREW_KEY: z.string().min(8).default("crew-demo-key"),
  GROUND_KEY: z.string().min(8).default("ground-demo-key"),
  DEVICE_KEY: z.string().min(8).default("device-demo-key"),
  DELAY_SCALE: z.coerce.number().positive().default(1),
});

export function loadConfig(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): Config {
  const parsed = envSchema.safeParse(env);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const issues = parsed.error.issues
      .map((issue) => `${issue.path.join(".") || "env"}: ${issue.message}`)
      .join("; ");
    throw new Error(`Invalid backend configuration: ${issues}`);
  }
  const value = parsed.data;
  return {
    port: value.PORT,
    host: value.HOST,
    dbPath: value.DB_PATH,
    corsOrigin: value.CORS_ORIGIN,
    crewKey: value.CREW_KEY,
    groundKey: value.GROUND_KEY,
    deviceKey: value.DEVICE_KEY,
    delayScale: value.DELAY_SCALE,
  };
}

 FILE: backend/src/context.ts
 lines: 45

import type { Db } from "./db.js";
import type { Config } from "./config.js";
import type { Role } from "./types.js";

export type SseEvent =
  | { type: "link.changed"; data: unknown }
  | { type: "packet.delivered"; data: unknown }
  | { type: "packet.acknowledged"; data: unknown }
  | { type: "sample"; data: unknown };

export type Listener = (event: SseEvent) => void;

export function createBus() {
  const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
  return {
    subscribe(listener: Listener) {
      listeners.add(listener);
      return () => listeners.delete(listener);
    },
    emit(event: SseEvent) {
      for (const listener of listeners) {
        try {
          listener(event);
        } catch {
          listeners.delete(listener);
        }
      }
    },
    size() {
      return listeners.size;
    },
  };
}

export type Bus = ReturnType<typeof createBus>;

export type Ctx = {
  db: Db;
  config: Config;
  bus: Bus;
  /** Injectable clock so tests can move time without sleeping. */
  now: () => Date;
};

export type RoleSet = Role[];

 FILE: backend/src/db.ts
 lines: 141

import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import fs from "node:fs";
import nodePath from "node:path";

/**
 * SQLite storage. Timestamps are ISO UTC strings; ordering uses them directly
 * because they are lexicographically sortable in that form.
 *
 * Nothing here decides clinical meaning. The server only stores, routes,
 * delays and audits.
 */
export type Db = Database.Database;

const schema = `
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS packets (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  crew_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  private INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  include_private INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  state TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  sent_at TEXT,
  visible_at TEXT,
  delivered_at TEXT,
  ack_at TEXT,
  ack_visible_at TEXT,
  delay_seconds REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS packet_events (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  packet_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  state TEXT NOT NULL,
  at TEXT NOT NULL,
  note TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS packet_events_packet ON packet_events (packet_id, at);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS observations (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  crew_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  metric TEXT NOT NULL,
  value REAL,
  unit TEXT NOT NULL,
  observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  entered_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  lane TEXT NOT NULL,
  method TEXT NOT NULL,
  context TEXT NOT NULL,
  quality TEXT NOT NULL,
  quality_reason TEXT,
  environment TEXT NOT NULL,
  procedure_version TEXT NOT NULL,
  raw_value TEXT,
  updated_seq INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS observations_seq ON observations (updated_seq);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS observations_crew ON observations (crew_id, observed_at);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS checkins (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  crew_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  json TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_seq INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS encounters (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  crew_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  json TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_seq INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS link_state (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
  state TEXT NOT NULL,
  environment TEXT NOT NULL,
  delay_scale REAL NOT NULL,
  changed_at TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_log (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  at TEXT NOT NULL,
  actor TEXT NOT NULL,
  action TEXT NOT NULL,
  ref TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  detail_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  hash TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sync_seq (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
  value INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS devices (
  device_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  last_seen_ms INTEGER NOT NULL
);
`;

export function openDatabase(path: string, delayScale = 1): Db {
  if (path !== ":memory:") {
    fs.mkdirSync(nodePath.dirname(path), { recursive: true });
  }
  const db = new Database(path);
  db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
  db.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");
  db.pragma("busy_timeout = 5000");
  db.exec(schema);

  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO link_state (id, state, environment, delay_scale, changed_at)
     VALUES (1, 'live', 'mars_transit', ?, ?)`,
  ).run(delayScale, now);
  db.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_seq (id, value) VALUES (1, 0)`).run();
  return db;
}

export function nextSeq(db: Db): number {
  db.prepare("UPDATE sync_seq SET value = value + 1 WHERE id = 1").run();
  const row = db.prepare("SELECT value FROM sync_seq WHERE id = 1").get() as {
    value: number;
  };
  return row.value;
}

export function currentSeq(db: Db): number {
  const row = db.prepare("SELECT value FROM sync_seq WHERE id = 1").get() as {
    value: number;
  };
  return row.value;
}

 FILE: backend/src/errors.ts

import type { z } from "zod";

/** One error shape for every failure: { error: { code, message } }. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  code: string;

  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
  }
}

export function parseOrThrow<T extends z.ZodType>(schema: T, value: unknown): z.infer<T> {
  const parsed = schema.safeParse(value ?? {});
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const message = parsed.error.issues
      .map((issue) => `${issue.path.join(".") || "body"}: ${issue.message}`)
      .join("; ");
    throw new ApiError(400, "VALIDATION", message);
  }
  return parsed.data;
}

import type { Db } from "./db.js";
import type { EnvironmentKey, LinkState } from "./types.js";

/**
 * Scenario one-way delays in seconds. These are demo link estimates, not
 * physics: DELAY_SCALE compresses them for a walkthrough.
 */
export const SCENARIO_DELAYS: Record<EnvironmentKey, number> = {
  mars_transit: 1200,
  station: 1,
  lunar_base: 1.3,
  rover: 2,
  remote_clinic: 0,
};

export const environmentKeys: EnvironmentKey[] = [
  "mars_transit",
  "station",
  "lunar_base",
  "rover",
  "remote_clinic",
];

export type LinkRow = {
  state: LinkState;
  environment: EnvironmentKey;
  delayScale: number;
  changedAt: string;
};

export type LinkView = LinkRow & {
  scenarioDelaySeconds: number;
  effectiveDelaySeconds: number;
};

export function effectiveDelayMs(
  state: LinkState,
  environment: EnvironmentKey,
  delayScale: number,
): number {
  if (state !== "delayed") return 0;
  return SCENARIO_DELAYS[environment] * 1000 * delayScale;
}

export function linkView(row: LinkRow): LinkView {
  return {
    ...row,
    scenarioDelaySeconds: SCENARIO_DELAYS[row.environment],
    effectiveDelaySeconds: effectiveDelayMs(row.state, row.environment, row.delayScale) / 1000,
  };
}

export function readLink(db: Db): LinkView {
  const row = db
    .prepare("SELECT state, environment, delay_scale, changed_at FROM link_state WHERE id = 1")
    .get() as {
    state: LinkState;
    environment: EnvironmentKey;
    delay_scale: number;
    changed_at: string;
  };
  return linkView({
    state: row.state,
    environment: row.environment,
    delayScale: row.delay_scale,
    changedAt: row.changed_at,
  });
}

export function writeLink(
  db: Db,
  state: LinkState,
  environment: EnvironmentKey,
  at: string = new Date().toISOString(),
): LinkView {
  db.prepare("UPDATE link_state SET state = ?, environment = ?, changed_at = ? WHERE id = 1").run(
    state,
    environment,
    at,
  );
  return readLink(db);
}

import type { Db } from "./db.js";
import type { OutboxState } from "./types.js";

export type PacketRow = {
  id: string;
  crew_id: string;
  kind: string;
  title: string;
  payload_json: string;
  private: number;
  include_private: number;
  state: OutboxState;
  created_at: string;
  sent_at: string | null;
  visible_at: string | null;
  delivered_at: string | null;
  ack_at: string | null;
  ack_visible_at: string | null;
  delay_seconds: number;
};

export type PacketEvent = {
  state: OutboxState;
  at: string;
  note: string;
};

export type PacketView = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  kind: string;
  title: string;
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  private: boolean;
  includePrivate: boolean;
  state: OutboxState;
  createdAt: string;
  sentAt: string | null;
  visibleAt: string | null;
  deliveredAt: string | null;
  ackAt: string | null;
  ackVisibleAt: string | null;
  delaySeconds: number;
  history: PacketEvent[];
};

const privateMetrics = new Set(["mood", "stress", "rested", "connected"]);
const privateKeys = new Set(["mood", "stress", "rested", "connected", "details"]);

/**
 * Defense in depth: the client may claim the private block is excluded, but
 * the server removes those fields anyway before storing.
 */
export function stripPrivate(payload: Record<string, unknown>): {
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  removed: string[];
} {
  const removed: string[] = [];
  const clone = structuredClone(payload) as Record<string, unknown>;

  for (const key of Object.keys(clone)) {
    if (privateKeys.has(key)) {
      delete clone[key];
      removed.push(key);
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(clone.observations)) {
    const kept = (clone.observations as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).filter((entry) => {
      if (typeof entry.metric === "string" && privateMetrics.has(entry.metric)) {
        removed.push(`observations.${String(entry.metric)}`);
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
    clone.observations = kept;
  }

  if (Array.isArray(clone.trends)) {
    const kept = (clone.trends as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).filter((entry) => {
      if (typeof entry.metric === "string" && privateMetrics.has(entry.metric)) {
        removed.push(`trends.${String(entry.metric)}`);
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
    clone.trends = kept;
  }

  for (const nested of ["checkIn", "checkin"]) {
    const value = clone[nested];
    if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
      const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
      for (const key of Object.keys(record)) {
        if (privateKeys.has(key)) {
          delete record[key];
          removed.push(`${nested}.${key}`);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return { payload: clone, removed };
}

export function recordEvent(
  db: Db,
  packetId: string,
  state: OutboxState,
  at: string,
  note: string,
): void {
  db.prepare("INSERT INTO packet_events (packet_id, state, at, note) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)").run(
    packetId,
    state,
    at,
    note,
  );
}

export function getPacketRow(db: Db, id: string): PacketRow | undefined {
  return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM packets WHERE id = ?").get(id) as PacketRow | undefined;
}

export function toPacketView(db: Db, row: PacketRow): PacketView {
  const events = db
    .prepare("SELECT state, at, note FROM packet_events WHERE packet_id = ? ORDER BY id ASC")
    .all(row.id) as PacketEvent[];
  return {
    id: row.id,
    crewId: row.crew_id,
    kind: row.kind,
    title: row.title,
    payload: JSON.parse(row.payload_json) as Record<string, unknown>,
    private: row.private === 1,
    includePrivate: row.include_private === 1,
    state: row.state,
    createdAt: row.created_at,
    sentAt: row.sent_at,
    visibleAt: row.visible_at,
    deliveredAt: row.delivered_at,
    ackAt: row.ack_at,
    ackVisibleAt: row.ack_visible_at,
    delaySeconds: row.delay_seconds,
    history: events,
  };
}

export function listPacketRows(db: Db, crewId: string): PacketRow[] {
  return db
    .prepare("SELECT * FROM packets WHERE crew_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC")
    .all(crewId) as PacketRow[];
}

 FILE: backend/src/quality/gate.ts

import { rules } from "./rules.js";
import type { Metric, Observation, Quality } from "../types.js";

/**
 * Quality gate. Copied from frontend/lib/quality/gate.ts, unchanged in logic.
 * Pure: same inputs, same output.
 *
 * Plausibility ranges are sensor-sanity limits from the demo rules, never
 * clinical limits. A flagged observation stays in the log but must not enter
 * charts, baselines or comparisons.
 */
export type GateResult = {
  quality: Quality;
  qualityReason: string | null;
  accepted: boolean;
};

export const STALE_AFTER_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;

const ranges: Partial<Record<Metric, { low: number; high: number }>> = {};
for (const rule of rules) {
  ranges[rule.metric as Metric] = { low: rule.low, high: rule.high };
}
for (const metric of ["stress", "rested", "connected"] as Metric[]) {
  ranges[metric] = ranges.mood;
}

export function plausibilityRange(metric: Metric) {
  return ranges[metric] ?? null;
}

const plain: Record<Quality, string> = {
  ok: "Sensor reported a usable value.",
  no_contact: "The sensor was not touching the skin, so the value is not usable.",
  stale: "This value is older than 60 minutes. It is history, not a current reading.",
  out_of_range: "The value is outside the plausible range for this sensor. Repeat the measurement.",
  motion: "Movement during the measurement made this value unreliable.",
  not_measured: "Nothing was measured for this reading.",
};

export function gate(
  observation: Observation,
  now: Date = new Date(),
  options: { ignoreStaleness?: boolean } = {},
): GateResult {
  const rejected = (quality: Quality, reason?: string): GateResult => ({
    quality,
    qualityReason: reason ?? plain[quality],
    accepted: false,
  });

  if (observation.quality === "not_measured" || observation.value === null) {
    return rejected("not_measured");
  }

  if (observation.artifact === "motion") {
    return rejected("motion");
  }

  if (observation.quality === "no_contact") {
    return rejected("no_contact");
  }

  const range = plausibilityRange(observation.metric);
  if (range && (observation.value < range.low || observation.value > range.high)) {
    return rejected(
      "out_of_range",
      `Value is outside the plausible sensor range (${range.low}–${range.high}). Repeat the measurement.`,
    );
  }

  // Staleness applies only to manual and live lanes. Replay and synthetic data
  // are historical by definition, so they are never "stale".
  if (
    !options.ignoreStaleness &&
    (observation.lane === "manual" || observation.lane === "live")
  ) {
    const observed = new Date(observation.observedAt).getTime();
    if (Number.isFinite(observed) && now.getTime() - observed > STALE_AFTER_MS) {
      return {
        quality: "stale",
        qualityReason: plain.stale,
        accepted: false,
      };
    }
  }

  return { quality: "ok", qualityReason: null, accepted: true };
}

export function isLateEntry(observation: Observation): boolean {
  if (observation.lane !== "manual") return false;
  const observed = new Date(observation.observedAt).getTime();
  const entered = new Date(observation.enteredAt).getTime();
  if (!Number.isFinite(observed) || !Number.isFinite(entered)) return false;
  return entered - observed > 5 * 60 * 1000;
}

/** 0 = usable, higher = stricter, so a disagreement keeps the stricter flag. */
export const qualityRank: Record<Quality, number> = {
  ok: 0,
  stale: 1,
  motion: 2,
  no_contact: 3,
  out_of_range: 4,
  not_measured: 5,
};

================================================================
 FILE: backend/src/routes/ingest.ts
 lines: 110
================================================================
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { ApiError, parseOrThrow } from "../errors.js";
import type { Ctx } from "../context.js";
import { requireRole } from "../auth.js";
import { sampleSchema } from "../schemas.js";
import { gate } from "../quality/gate.js";
import { nextSeq } from "../db.js";
import { readLink } from "../link.js";
import type { Observation, Quality } from "../types.js";

const MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SECOND = 20;

export function registerIngest(app: FastifyInstance, ctx: Ctx) {
  const buckets = new Map<string, { windowStart: number; count: number }>();

  app.post("/v1/ingest/sample", async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(ctx, request, ["device"]);
    const body = parseOrThrow(sampleSchema, request.body);

    const nowMs = ctx.now().getTime();
    const bucket = buckets.get(body.deviceId) ?? { windowStart: nowMs, count: 0 };
    if (nowMs - bucket.windowStart >= 1000) {
      bucket.windowStart = nowMs;
      bucket.count = 0;
    }
    bucket.count += 1;
    buckets.set(body.deviceId, bucket);
    if (bucket.count > MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SECOND) {
      throw new ApiError(429, "RATE_LIMIT", "Too many samples from this device.");
    }

    // Contact state maps to the same quality vocabulary the UI uses.
    let quality: Quality = "ok";
    let artifact: Observation["artifact"] = null;
    if (body.contact === "none") {
      quality = "no_contact";
    } else if (body.contact === "poor") {
      quality = (body.motion ?? 0) > 0.5 ? "motion" : "no_contact";
      if (quality === "motion") artifact = "motion";
    }

    const link = readLink(ctx.db);
    const observation: Observation = {
      id: `dev-${body.deviceId}-${randomUUID()}`,
      crewId: body.crewId,
      metric: body.metric,
      value: body.value,
      unit: body.metric === "heart_rate" ? "bpm" : "",
      observedAt: body.observedAt,
      enteredAt: ctx.now().toISOString(),
      lane: "live",
      method: "ppg_finger",
      context: "rest",
      quality,
      qualityReason: null,
      environment: link.environment,
      procedureVersion: "demo-procedures-1",
    };
    if (artifact) observation.artifact = artifact;

    const result = gate(observation, ctx.now());
    const storedQuality = result.quality;
    const reason = result.qualityReason;
    const seq = nextSeq(ctx.db);

    ctx.db
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO observations (
          id, crew_id, metric, value, unit, observed_at, entered_at, lane, method,
          context, quality, quality_reason, environment, procedure_version, raw_value, updated_seq
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      )
      .run(
        observation.id,
        observation.crewId,
        observation.metric,
        observation.value,
        observation.unit,
        observation.observedAt,
        observation.enteredAt,
        observation.lane,
        observation.method,
        observation.context,
        storedQuality,
        reason,
        observation.environment,
        observation.procedureVersion,
        null,
        seq,
      );

    ctx.db
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO devices (device_id, last_seen_ms) VALUES (?, ?)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET last_seen_ms = excluded.last_seen_ms`,
      )
      .run(body.deviceId, nowMs);

    const stored: Observation = { ...observation, quality: storedQuality, qualityReason: reason };
    ctx.bus.emit({ type: "sample", data: stored });

    return reply.status(202).send({
      accepted: result.accepted,
      observation: stored,
      cursor: seq,
      serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
    });
  });
}

================================================================
 FILE: backend/src/routes/link.ts
 lines: 41

import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import type { Ctx } from "../context.js";
import { requireRole } from "../auth.js";
import { parseOrThrow } from "../errors.js";
import { linkPutSchema } from "../schemas.js";
import { readLink, writeLink } from "../link.js";
import { appendAudit } from "../audit.js";

export function registerLink(app: FastifyInstance, ctx: Ctx) {
  app.get("/v1/link", async (request) => {
    requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew", "ground", "device"]);
    return {
      link: readLink(ctx.db),
      serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
    };
  });

  app.put("/v1/link", async (request) => {
    const role = requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew", "ground"]);
    const body = parseOrThrow(linkPutSchema, request.body);
    const previous = readLink(ctx.db);
    const link = writeLink(ctx.db, body.state, body.environment, ctx.now().toISOString());

    appendAudit(
      ctx.db,
      role,
      "link.changed",
      "link",
      {
        from: { state: previous.state, environment: previous.environment },
        to: { state: link.state, environment: link.environment },
        scenarioDelaySeconds: link.scenarioDelaySeconds,
        effectiveDelaySeconds: link.effectiveDelaySeconds,
      },
      ctx.now().toISOString(),
    );

    ctx.bus.emit({ type: "link.changed", data: link });
    return { link, serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString() };
  });
}

================================================================
 FILE: backend/src/routes/packets.ts
 lines: 141
================================================================
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import type { Ctx } from "../context.js";
import { requireRole } from "../auth.js";
import { ApiError, parseOrThrow } from "../errors.js";
import { outboxItemSchema } from "../schemas.js";
import { appendAudit } from "../audit.js";
import { readLink } from "../link.js";
import {
  getPacketRow,
  listPacketRows,
  recordEvent,
  stripPrivate,
  toPacketView,
} from "../packets.js";
import { runDueTransitions } from "../scheduler.js";

export function registerPackets(app: FastifyInstance, ctx: Ctx) {
  app.post("/v1/packets", async (request, reply) => {
    const role = requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew"]);
    if (role !== "crew") throw new ApiError(403, "FORBIDDEN", "Crew key required.");
    const body = parseOrThrow(outboxItemSchema, request.body);

    const existing = getPacketRow(ctx.db, body.id);
    if (existing) {
      return reply.status(200).send({
        packet: toPacketView(ctx.db, existing),
        serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
        created: false,
      });
    }

    const link = readLink(ctx.db);
    if (link.state === "blackout") {
      appendAudit(
        ctx.db,
        "crew",
        "packet.rejected",
        body.id,
        { code: "LINK_BLACKOUT", crewId: body.crewId },
        ctx.now().toISOString(),
      );
      throw new ApiError(
        503,
        "LINK_BLACKOUT",
        "The link is in blackout. Nothing was stored. The packet stays queued on the device.",
      );
    }

    const now = ctx.now();
    const nowIso = now.toISOString();
    const delayMs = link.effectiveDelaySeconds * 1000;
    const visibleAt = new Date(now.getTime() + delayMs).toISOString();

    const stored = body.includePrivate
      ? { payload: body.payload, removed: [] as string[] }
      : stripPrivate(body.payload);

    ctx.db
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO packets (
          id, crew_id, kind, title, payload_json, private, include_private, state,
          created_at, sent_at, visible_at, delay_seconds
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'sent', ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      )
      .run(
        body.id,
        body.crewId,
        body.kind,
        body.title,
        JSON.stringify(stored.payload),
        body.private ? 1 : 0,
        body.includePrivate ? 1 : 0,
        nowIso,
        nowIso,
        visibleAt,
        delayMs / 1000,
      );

    recordEvent(
      ctx.db,
      body.id,
      "sent",
      nowIso,
      link.state === "live"
        ? "Link is live. Server accepted the packet."
        : `Server accepted the packet. Delivery visible in ${Math.round(delayMs / 1000)} s.`,
    );
    appendAudit(
      ctx.db,
      "crew",
      "packet.sent",
      body.id,
      {
        crewId: body.crewId,
        kind: body.kind,
        delaySeconds: delayMs / 1000,
        stripped: stored.removed,
        includePrivate: body.includePrivate,
      },
      nowIso,
    );

    const row = getPacketRow(ctx.db, body.id)!;
    return reply
      .status(201)
      .send({ packet: toPacketView(ctx.db, row), serverTime: nowIso, created: true });
  });

  // The crew key is shared by the whole crew, so scoping uses the crewId the
  // client names. A packet belonging to someone else answers 404, not 403.
  app.get<{ Params: { id: string }; Querystring: { crewId?: string } }>(
    "/v1/packets/:id",
    async (request) => {
      requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew"]);
      runDueTransitions(ctx);
      const row = getPacketRow(ctx.db, request.params.id);
      const crewId = request.query.crewId;
      if (!row || (crewId && row.crew_id !== crewId)) {
        throw new ApiError(404, "NOT_FOUND", "No such packet for this crew member.");
      }
      return { packet: toPacketView(ctx.db, row), serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString() };
    },
  );

  app.get<{ Querystring: { crewId?: string } }>("/v1/packets", async (request) => {
    requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew"]);
    runDueTransitions(ctx);
    const crewId = request.query.crewId;
    if (!crewId) {
      throw new ApiError(
        400,
        "CREW_ID_REQUIRED",
        "Pass ?crewId= to list only that crew member's packets.",
      );
    }
    return {
      packets: listPacketRows(ctx.db, crewId).map((row) => toPacketView(ctx.db, row)),
      serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
    };
  });
}

================================================================
 FILE: backend/src/routes/stream.ts
 lines: 43
================================================================
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import type { Ctx, SseEvent } from "../context.js";
import { requireRole } from "../auth.js";

export function registerStream(app: FastifyInstance, ctx: Ctx) {
  app.get("/v1/stream", async (request, reply) => {
    requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew", "ground"]);

    reply.hijack();
    reply.raw.writeHead(200, {
      "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",
      "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
      Connection: "keep-alive",
      "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
    });
    reply.raw.write(": connected\n\n");

    const send = (event: SseEvent) => {
      if (reply.raw.writableEnded) return;
      reply.raw.write(`event: ${event.type}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(event.data)}\n\n`);
    };

    const unsubscribe = ctx.bus.subscribe(send);
    const heartbeat = setInterval(() => {
      if (reply.raw.writableEnded) return;
      reply.raw.write(": heartbeat\n\n");
    }, 15_000);

    let closed = false;
    const cleanup = () => {
      if (closed) return;
      closed = true;
      clearInterval(heartbeat);
      unsubscribe();
    };

    request.raw.on("close", cleanup);
    request.raw.on("error", cleanup);
    reply.raw.on("close", cleanup);
    reply.raw.on("error", cleanup);
    app.addHook("onClose", async () => cleanup());
  });
}

================================================================
 FILE: backend/src/routes/surgeon.ts
 lines: 77
================================================================
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import type { Ctx } from "../context.js";
import { requireRole } from "../auth.js";
import { ApiError } from "../errors.js";
import { readLink } from "../link.js";
import { appendAudit } from "../audit.js";
import { getPacketRow, toPacketView, type PacketRow } from "../packets.js";
import { runDueTransitions } from "../scheduler.js";

export function registerSurgeon(app: FastifyInstance, ctx: Ctx) {
  app.get("/v1/surgeon/inbox", async (request) => {
    requireRole(ctx, request, ["ground"]);
    const rows = ctx.db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT * FROM packets WHERE state IN ('delivered','acknowledged') ORDER BY delivered_at DESC, created_at DESC",
      )
      .all() as PacketRow[];
    return {
      packets: rows.map((row) => toPacketView(ctx.db, row)),
      serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
    };
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>("/v1/surgeon/packets/:id/ack", async (request) => {
    requireRole(ctx, request, ["ground"]);
    const row = getPacketRow(ctx.db, request.params.id);
    if (!row) throw new ApiError(404, "NOT_FOUND", "No such packet.");

    if (row.ack_at) {
      // Double acknowledgement is idempotent: the first timestamp wins.
      return {
        packet: toPacketView(ctx.db, row),
        serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
        alreadyAcknowledged: true,
      };
    }

    if (row.state !== "delivered") {
      throw new ApiError(
        409,
        "NOT_DELIVERED",
        "Only a delivered packet can be acknowledged.",
      );
    }

    const now = ctx.now();
    const nowIso = now.toISOString();
    const link = readLink(ctx.db);
    const returnDelayMs = link.effectiveDelaySeconds * 1000;
    const ackVisibleAt = new Date(now.getTime() + returnDelayMs).toISOString();

    ctx.db
      .prepare(
        "UPDATE packets SET ack_at = ?, ack_visible_at = ? WHERE id = ? AND ack_at IS NULL",
      )
      .run(nowIso, ackVisibleAt, row.id);

    // The acknowledgement event is written by the scheduler when the return
    // delay actually elapses, so the crew's history never shows it early.
    appendAudit(
      ctx.db,
      "ground",
      "packet.ack",
      row.id,
      { returnDelaySeconds: returnDelayMs / 1000, crewId: row.crew_id },
      nowIso,
    );

    runDueTransitions(ctx, now);
    const updated = getPacketRow(ctx.db, row.id)!;
    return {
      packet: toPacketView(ctx.db, updated),
      serverTime: nowIso,
      alreadyAcknowledged: false,
    };
  });
}

================================================================
 FILE: backend/src/routes/sync.ts
 lines: 200
================================================================
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { ApiError, parseOrThrow } from "../errors.js";
import type { Ctx } from "../context.js";
import { requireRole } from "../auth.js";
import {
  checkInSyncSchema,
  encounterSyncSchema,
  observationSyncSchema,
  syncedCollections,
  type SyncCollection,
} from "../schemas.js";
import { currentSeq, nextSeq } from "../db.js";
import { appendAudit } from "../audit.js";
import { reconcileObservation } from "../sync.js";
import { plausibilityRange } from "../quality/gate.js";
import type { Observation, Quality } from "../types.js";

type CollectionParams = { collection: string };

function assertCollection(value: string): SyncCollection {
  if (!(syncedCollections as readonly string[]).includes(value)) {
    throw new ApiError(
      404,
      "UNKNOWN_COLLECTION",
      `Collection must be one of ${syncedCollections.join(", ")}.`,
    );
  }
  return value as SyncCollection;
}

type ObservationRow = {
  id: string;
  crew_id: string;
  metric: string;
  value: number | null;
  unit: string;
  observed_at: string;
  entered_at: string;
  lane: string;
  method: string;
  context: string;
  quality: Quality;
  quality_reason: string | null;
  environment: string;
  procedure_version: string;
  raw_value: string | null;
  updated_seq: number;
};

function rowToObservation(row: ObservationRow): Observation {
  const observation: Observation = {
    id: row.id,
    crewId: row.crew_id,
    metric: row.metric as Observation["metric"],
    value: row.value,
    unit: row.unit,
    observedAt: row.observed_at,
    enteredAt: row.entered_at,
    lane: row.lane as Observation["lane"],
    method: row.method,
    context: row.context as Observation["context"],
    quality: row.quality,
    qualityReason: row.quality_reason,
    environment: row.environment,
    procedureVersion: row.procedure_version,
  };
  if (row.raw_value !== null) observation.rawValue = row.raw_value;
  return observation;
}

export function registerSync(app: FastifyInstance, ctx: Ctx) {
  app.post<{ Params: CollectionParams }>("/v1/sync/:collection", async (request) => {
    const role = requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew"]);
    const collection = assertCollection(request.params.collection);
    const now = ctx.now();
    const nowIso = now.toISOString();
    let applied = 0;

    if (collection === "observations") {
      const items = parseOrThrow(observationSyncSchema, request.body);
      const incoming = items as Observation[];
      const insert = ctx.db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO observations (
          id, crew_id, metric, value, unit, observed_at, entered_at, lane, method,
          context, quality, quality_reason, environment, procedure_version, raw_value, updated_seq
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      );
      const updateQuality = ctx.db.prepare(
        `UPDATE observations
         SET quality = ?, quality_reason = ?, raw_value = ?, updated_seq = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
      );

      for (const item of incoming) {
        const existing = ctx.db
          .prepare("SELECT id FROM observations WHERE id = ?")
          .get(item.id) as { id: string } | undefined;

        // Gate parity: the stored quality can only become stricter.
        const reconciled = reconcileObservation(item, now);
        const parityQuality: Quality = reconciled.quality;

        if (existing) {
          // Observations are immutable except the quality fields.
          const seq = nextSeq(ctx.db);
          updateQuality.run(
            parityQuality,
            reconciled.qualityReason,
            item.rawValue ?? null,
            seq,
            item.id,
          );
        } else {
          const seq = nextSeq(ctx.db);
          insert.run(
            item.id,
            item.crewId,
            item.metric,
            item.value,
            item.unit,
            item.observedAt,
            item.enteredAt,
            item.lane,
            item.method,
            item.context,
            parityQuality,
            reconciled.qualityReason,
            item.environment,
            item.procedureVersion,
            item.rawValue ?? null,
            seq,
          );
        }
        applied += 1;
      }
    } else {
      const schema = collection === "checkins" ? checkInSyncSchema : encounterSyncSchema;
      const items = parseOrThrow(schema, request.body) as Array<{ id: string; crewId: string }>;
      const upsert = ctx.db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO ${collection} (id, crew_id, json, updated_seq) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
         ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET crew_id = excluded.crew_id, json = excluded.json,
           updated_seq = excluded.updated_seq`,
      );
      for (const item of items) {
        upsert.run(item.id, item.crewId, JSON.stringify(item), nextSeq(ctx.db));
        applied += 1;
      }
    }

    const cursor = currentSeq(ctx.db);
    appendAudit(ctx.db, role, "sync.push", collection, { applied, cursor }, nowIso);
    return { applied, cursor, serverTime: nowIso };
  });

  app.get<{ Params: CollectionParams; Querystring: { since?: string; crewId?: string } }>(
    "/v1/sync/:collection",
    async (request) => {
      const role = requireRole(ctx, request, ["crew"]);
      const collection = assertCollection(request.params.collection);
      const since = Number(request.query.since ?? 0);
      if (!Number.isFinite(since) || since < 0) {
        throw new ApiError(400, "BAD_CURSOR", "since must be a non-negative number.");
      }
      const crewId = request.query.crewId;

      if (collection === "observations") {
        const rows = ctx.db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT * FROM observations
             WHERE updated_seq > ? AND (? IS NULL OR crew_id = ?)
             ORDER BY updated_seq ASC LIMIT 1000`,
          )
          .all(since, crewId ?? null, crewId ?? null) as ObservationRow[];
        const items = rows.map(rowToObservation).map((item) => ({
          ...item,
          plausibleRange: plausibilityRange(item.metric),
        }));
        return {
          items,
          cursor: currentSeq(ctx.db),
          serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
          gate: "server-side, demo rules",
        };
      }

      const rows = ctx.db
        .prepare(
          `SELECT json FROM ${collection}
           WHERE updated_seq > ? AND (? IS NULL OR crew_id = ?)
           ORDER BY updated_seq ASC LIMIT 1000`,
        )
        .all(since, crewId ?? null, crewId ?? null) as Array<{ json: string }>;
      return {
        items: rows.map((row) => JSON.parse(row.json) as unknown),
        cursor: currentSeq(ctx.db),
        serverTime: ctx.now().toISOString(),
      };
    },
  );
}

================================================================
 FILE: backend/src/scheduler.ts
 lines: 105

import type { Ctx } from "./context.js";
import { appendAudit } from "./audit.js";
import { getPacketRow, recordEvent, toPacketView, type PacketRow } from "./packets.js";

/**
 * All transitions are computed from stored timestamps and guarded by the
 * previous state, so a restart neither loses a delivery nor repeats one.
 */
export type TickResult = {
  delivered: string[];
  acknowledged: string[];
};

export function runDueTransitions(ctx: Ctx, now: Date = ctx.now()): TickResult {
  const nowIso = now.toISOString();
  const delivered: string[] = [];
  const acknowledged: string[] = [];

  const dueDeliveries = ctx.db
    .prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM packets WHERE state = 'sent' AND visible_at IS NOT NULL AND visible_at <= ? ORDER BY visible_at ASC",
    )
    .all(nowIso) as PacketRow[];

  for (const row of dueDeliveries) {
    const update = ctx.db
      .prepare(
        "UPDATE packets SET state = 'delivered', delivered_at = ? WHERE id = ? AND state = 'sent'",
      )
      .run(nowIso, row.id);
    if (update.changes !== 1) continue;

    recordEvent(
      ctx.db,
      row.id,
      "delivered",
      nowIso,
      "Ground station received the packet.",
    );
    appendAudit(
      ctx.db,
      "system",
      "packet.delivered",
      row.id,
      { crewId: row.crew_id, delaySeconds: row.delay_seconds },
      nowIso,
    );
    delivered.push(row.id);
    const updated = getPacketRow(ctx.db, row.id);
    if (updated) ctx.bus.emit({ type: "packet.delivered", data: toPacketView(ctx.db, updated) });
  }

  const dueAcks = ctx.db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT * FROM packets
       WHERE state = 'delivered' AND ack_at IS NOT NULL AND ack_visible_at IS NOT NULL
         AND ack_visible_at <= ? ORDER BY ack_visible_at ASC`,
    )
    .all(nowIso) as PacketRow[];

  for (const row of dueAcks) {
    const update = ctx.db
      .prepare(
        `UPDATE packets SET state = 'acknowledged'
         WHERE id = ? AND state = 'delivered' AND ack_visible_at <= ?`,
      )
      .run(row.id, nowIso);
    if (update.changes !== 1) continue;

    recordEvent(
      ctx.db,
      row.id,
      "acknowledged",
      nowIso,
      "The return delay elapsed. The crew can now see the acknowledgement.",
    );
    appendAudit(
      ctx.db,
      "system",
      "packet.acknowledged",
      row.id,
      { crewId: row.crew_id },
      nowIso,
    );
    acknowledged.push(row.id);
    const updated = getPacketRow(ctx.db, row.id);
    if (updated) {
      ctx.bus.emit({ type: "packet.acknowledged", data: toPacketView(ctx.db, updated) });
    }
  }

  return { delivered, acknowledged };
}

export function startScheduler(ctx: Ctx, intervalMs = 1000): () => void {
  runDueTransitions(ctx);
  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    try {
      runDueTransitions(ctx);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Scheduler tick failed", error);
    }
  }, intervalMs);
  return () => clearInterval(timer);
}

import { z } from "zod";

/** Zod mirrors of frontend/lib/types.ts. No clinical interpretation here. */

const iso = z
  .string()
  .refine((value) => Number.isFinite(new Date(value).getTime()), "must be an ISO timestamp");

export const metricSchema = z.enum([
  "heart_rate",
  "skin_temp",
  "bp_systolic",
  "bp_diastolic",
  "glucose",
  "sleep_minutes",
  "mood",
  "stress",
  "rested",
  "connected",
  "motion",
]);

export const laneSchema = z.enum(["manual", "live", "replay", "synthetic"]);
export const qualitySchema = z.enum([
  "ok",
  "no_contact",
  "stale",
  "out_of_range",
  "motion",
  "not_measured",
]);
export const contextSchema = z.enum(["rest", "post_exercise", "post_meal", "unknown"]);
export const environmentSchema = z.enum([
  "mars_transit",
  "station",
  "lunar_base",
  "rover",
  "remote_clinic",
]);
export const linkStateSchema = z.enum(["live", "delayed", "blackout"]);
export const outboxStateSchema = z.enum(["queued", "sent", "delivered", "acknowledged"]);

export const symptomSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  onset: z.enum(["just_now", "today", "several_days"]),
  trend: z.enum(["better", "same", "worse"]),
  urgent: z.boolean(),
});

export const observationSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1),
  crewId: z.string().min(1),
  metric: metricSchema,
  value: z.number().nullable(),
  unit: z.string(),
  observedAt: iso,
  enteredAt: iso,
  lane: laneSchema,
  method: z.string(),
  context: contextSchema,
  rawValue: z.string().optional(),
  quality: qualitySchema,
  qualityReason: z.string().nullable().optional().default(null),
  environment: z.string(),
  procedureVersion: z.string(),
  artifact: z.enum(["motion"]).nullable().optional(),
});

export const checkInSchema = z
  .object({
    id: z.string().min(1),
    crewId: z.string().min(1),
    observedAt: iso,
    enteredAt: iso,
    sleepMinutes: z.number().nullable(),
    mood: z.number().nullable(),
    stress: z.number().nullable(),
    rested: z.number().nullable(),
    connected: z.number().nullable(),
    symptoms: z.array(symptomSchema),
    details: z.string(),
  })
  .loose();

export const encounterSchema = z
  .object({
    id: z.string().min(1),
    crewId: z.string().min(1),
    kind: z.enum(["assessment", "emergency", "daily"]),
    pathwayId: z.string(),
    startedAt: iso,
    endedAt: iso.nullable(),
  })
  .loose();

export const handoffSchema = z
  .object({
    id: z.string().min(1),
    crewId: z.string().min(1),
    createdAt: iso,
    includePrivate: z.boolean(),
  })
  .loose();

export const outboxItemSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1),
  crewId: z.string().min(1),
  kind: z.enum(["handoff", "support", "report"]),
  createdAt: iso,
  sentAt: iso.nullable().optional().default(null),
  acknowledgedAt: iso.nullable().optional().default(null),
  state: outboxStateSchema.optional(),
  delaySeconds: z.number().min(0).optional().default(0),
  title: z.string().optional().default("Handoff"),
  payload: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()).optional().default({}),
  private: z.boolean().optional().default(false),
  includePrivate: z.boolean().optional().default(false),
  history: z
    .array(z.object({ state: outboxStateSchema, at: iso, note: z.string() }))
    .optional()
    .default([]),
});

export type OutboxItemInput = z.infer<typeof outboxItemSchema>;

export const linkPutSchema = z.object({
  state: linkStateSchema,
  environment: environmentSchema,
});

export const sampleSchema = z.object({
  deviceId: z.string().min(1),
  crewId: z.string().min(1),
  metric: metricSchema,
  value: z.number(),
  observedAt: iso,
  contact: z.enum(["good", "poor", "none"]),
  motion: z.number().optional(),
});

export const syncedCollections = ["observations", "checkins", "encounters"] as const;
export type SyncCollection = (typeof syncedCollections)[number];

export const observationSyncSchema = z.array(observationSchema).max(500);
export const checkInSyncSchema = z.array(checkInSchema).max(500);
export const encounterSyncSchema = z.array(encounterSchema).max(500);

 FILE: backend/src/server.ts

import Fastify, { type FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import cors from "@fastify/cors";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { ZodError } from "zod";
import { loadConfig, type Config } from "./config.js";
import { openDatabase, type Db } from "./db.js";
import { ApiError } from "./errors.js";
import { createBus, type Ctx } from "./context.js";
import { registerHealth } from "./routes/health.js";
import { registerLink } from "./routes/link.js";
import { registerPackets } from "./routes/packets.js";
import { registerSurgeon } from "./routes/surgeon.js";
import { registerStream } from "./routes/stream.js";
import { registerSync } from "./routes/sync.js";
import { registerIngest } from "./routes/ingest.js";
import { registerAudit } from "./routes/audit.js";
import { startScheduler } from "./scheduler.js";

declare module "fastify" {
  interface FastifyInstance {
    ctx: Ctx;
  }
}

export type BuildOptions = {
  db?: Db;
  now?: () => Date;
  /** Tests disable the timer and call runDueTransitions with their own clock. */
  scheduler?: boolean;
};

export async function buildApp(
  config: Config,
  options: BuildOptions = {},
): Promise<FastifyInstance> {
  const ownsDb = !options.db;
  const db = options.db ?? openDatabase(config.dbPath, config.delayScale);
  const ctx: Ctx = {
    db,
    config,
    bus: createBus(),
    now: options.now ?? (() => new Date()),
  };

  const app = Fastify({ logger: false, trustProxy: true });
  app.decorate("ctx", ctx);

  await app.register(cors, {
    origin: config.corsOrigin.split(",").map((origin) => origin.trim()),
    methods: ["GET", "POST", "PUT", "OPTIONS"],
    allowedHeaders: ["Authorization", "Content-Type"],
    credentials: false,
  });

  app.setErrorHandler((error, _request, reply) => {
    if (error instanceof ApiError) {
      return reply
        .status(error.status)
        .send({ error: { code: error.code, message: error.message } });
    }
    if (error instanceof ZodError) {
      const message = error.issues
        .map((issue) => `${issue.path.join(".") || "body"}: ${issue.message}`)
        .join("; ");
      return reply.status(400).send({ error: { code: "VALIDATION", message } });
    }
    const err = error as { statusCode?: number; code?: string; message?: string };
    const status = typeof err.statusCode === "number" ? err.statusCode : 500;
    if (status >= 500) {
      app.log.error(error);
      return reply
        .status(500)
        .send({ error: { code: "INTERNAL", message: "The server could not complete the request." } });
    }
    return reply.status(status).send({
      error: { code: err.code ?? "REQUEST", message: err.message ?? "Request failed." },
    });
  });

  app.setNotFoundHandler((_request, reply) =>
    reply
      .status(404)
      .send({ error: { code: "NOT_FOUND", message: "No such route." } }),
  );

  registerHealth(app, ctx);
  registerLink(app, ctx);
  registerPackets(app, ctx);
  registerSurgeon(app, ctx);
  registerStream(app, ctx);
  registerSync(app, ctx);
  registerIngest(app, ctx);
  registerAudit(app, ctx);

  const stopScheduler = options.scheduler === false ? null : startScheduler(ctx);

  app.addHook("onClose", async () => {
    stopScheduler?.();
    if (ownsDb) db.close();
  });

  return app;
}

const isMain =
  process.argv[1] !== undefined && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href;

if (isMain) {
  const config = loadConfig();
  const app = await buildApp(config);
  try {
    await app.listen({ port: config.port, host: config.host });
    app.log.info?.(`CrewSignal backend on http://${config.host}:${config.port}`);
    console.log(`CrewSignal backend listening on http://${config.host}:${config.port}`);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

 FILE: backend/src/sync.ts
 lines: 51

import type { Observation, Quality } from "./types.js";
import { gate, qualityRank } from "./quality/gate.js";

export type Reconciliation = {
  quality: Quality;
  qualityReason: string | null;
  changed: boolean;
};

/**
 * Run the server gate for manual/live lanes (plausibility + staleness) and
 * plausibility only for replay/synthetic. When the client and server disagree,
 * the stricter flag wins and the reason records the disagreement.
 */
export function reconcileObservation(observation: Observation, now: Date): Reconciliation {
  const result = gate(observation, now, {
    ignoreStaleness: observation.lane === "replay" || observation.lane === "synthetic",
  });

  const clientQuality = observation.quality;
  const clientRank = qualityRank[clientQuality] ?? 0;
  const serverRank = qualityRank[result.quality] ?? 0;

  if (clientQuality === result.quality) {
    return {
      quality: clientQuality,
      qualityReason: observation.qualityReason ?? result.qualityReason,
      changed: false,
    };
  }

  if (serverRank > clientRank) {
    const note =
      clientQuality === "ok"
        ? `Server gate flagged this as ${result.quality}.`
        : `Server gate was stricter (${result.quality}) than the device flag (${clientQuality}).`;
    return {
      quality: result.quality,
      qualityReason: result.qualityReason ? `${result.qualityReason} ${note}` : note,
      changed: true,
    };
  }

  return {
    quality: clientQuality,
    qualityReason: observation.qualityReason
      ? `${observation.qualityReason} Server gate reported ${result.quality}.`
      : `Server gate reported ${result.quality}; the stricter client flag was kept.`,
    changed: false,
  };
}

/** Domain types the server stores. Kept independent of the frontend package. */

export type Metric =
  | "heart_rate"
  | "skin_temp"
  | "bp_systolic"
  | "bp_diastolic"
  | "glucose"
  | "sleep_minutes"
  | "mood"
  | "stress"
  | "rested"
  | "connected"
  | "motion";

export type Lane = "manual" | "live" | "replay" | "synthetic";
export type Quality = "ok" | "no_contact" | "stale" | "out_of_range" | "motion" | "not_measured";
export type ObservationContext = "rest" | "post_exercise" | "post_meal" | "unknown";

export type Observation = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  metric: Metric;
  value: number | null;
  unit: string;
  observedAt: string;
  enteredAt: string;
  lane: Lane;
  method: string;
  context: ObservationContext;
  rawValue?: string;
  quality: Quality;
  qualityReason: string | null;
  environment: string;
  procedureVersion: string;

};

  | "lunar_base"
  | "rover"
  | "remote_clinic";

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { crew, ground, makeApp, packetBody, type TestApp } from "./helpers.js";

let current: TestApp | null = null;

afterEach(async () => {
  await current?.app.close();
  current = null;
});

async function setup(): Promise<TestApp> {
  current = await makeApp();
  return current;
}

describe("audit chain", () => {
  it("verifies a clean chain", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "PUT",
      url: "/v1/link",
      headers: crew,
      payload: { state: "delayed", environment: "station" },
    });
    await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/v1/packets", headers: crew, payload: packetBody("a1") });
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/audit/verify",
      headers: ground,
    });
    const body = response.json() as { ok: boolean; checked: number; firstBrokenId: number | null };
    expect(body.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(body.checked).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(body.firstBrokenId).toBeNull();
  });

  it("detects a tampered row and reports the first break", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations",
      headers: crew,
      payload: [],
    });
    await app.inject({
      method: "PUT",
      url: "/v1/link",
      headers: crew,
      payload: { state: "blackout", environment: "rover" },
    });
    app.ctx.db.prepare("UPDATE audit_log SET action = 'edited' WHERE id = 1").run();
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/audit/verify",
      headers: ground,
    });
    const body = response.json() as {
      ok: boolean;
      firstBrokenId: number | null;
      expected: string;
      actual: string;
    };
    expect(body.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(body.firstBrokenId).toBe(1);
    expect(body.expected).not.toBe(body.actual);
  });

  it("requires the ground key", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/audit/verify",
      headers: crew,
    });
    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(403);
  });
});

import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { buildApp } from "../src/server.js";
import { loadConfig } from "../src/config.js";
import { openDatabase, type Db } from "../src/db.js";

export const crew = { authorization: "Bearer crew-demo-key" };
export const ground = { authorization: "Bearer ground-demo-key" };
export const device = { authorization: "Bearer device-demo-key" };

export type TestApp = {
  app: FastifyInstance;
  advance: (ms: number) => void;
  now: () => Date;
  setClock: (date: Date) => void;
};

export async function makeApp(
  env: Record<string, string> = {},
  options: { db?: Db; start?: Date } = {},
): Promise<TestApp> {
  let clock = options.start ?? new Date("2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z");
  const config = loadConfig({ DB_PATH: ":memory:", ...env } as NodeJS.ProcessEnv);
  const app = await buildApp(config, {
    db: options.db,
    now: () => clock,
    scheduler: false,
  });
  return {
    app,
    advance: (ms) => {
      clock = new Date(clock.getTime() + ms);
    },
    now: () => clock,
    setClock: (date) => {
      clock = date;
    },
  };
}

export function packetBody(id: string, overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    id,
    crewId: "alex-morgan",
    kind: "handoff",
    createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    includePrivate: false,
    private: false,
    title: "Handoff",
    delaySeconds: 0,
    payload: {
      id: `handoff-${id}`,
      crewId: "alex-morgan",
      createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
      includePrivate: false,
      observations: [
        { metric: "mood", value: 2 },
        { metric: "heart_rate", value: 80 },
      ],
      trends: [{ metric: "stress", text: "up" }],
      notes: "steady",
    },
    history: [],
    ...overrides,
  };
}

export function tempDbPath(): { path: string; cleanup: () => void } {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "crewsignal-test-"));
  return {
    path: join(dir, "test.db"),
    cleanup: () => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }),
  };
}

export function openMemory(): Db {
  return openDatabase(":memory:");
}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { FastifyInstance } from "fastify";
import { crew, makeApp, packetBody, type TestApp } from "./helpers.js";

let current: TestApp | null = null;

afterEach(async () => {
  await current?.app.close();
  current = null;
});

async function setup(): Promise<TestApp> {
  current = await makeApp();
  return current;
}

describe("packet ingest", () => {
  it("is idempotent by client id", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    const first = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("p1"),
    });
    const second = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("p1"),
    });
    expect(first.statusCode).toBe(201);
    expect(second.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((second.json() as { created: boolean }).created).toBe(false);
    const count = app.ctx.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM packets").get() as { c: number };
    expect(count.c).toBe(1);
    const events = app.ctx.db
      .prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM packet_events WHERE packet_id = 'p1'")
      .get() as { c: number };
    expect(events.c).toBe(1);
  });

  it("rejects during blackout and stores nothing", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "PUT",
      url: "/v1/link",
      headers: crew,
      payload: { state: "blackout", environment: "mars_transit" },
    });
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("p2"),
    });
    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(503);
    expect((response.json() as { error: { code: string } }).error.code).toBe("LINK_BLACKOUT");
    const count = app.ctx.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM packets").get() as { c: number };
    expect(count.c).toBe(0);
  });

  it("strips private fields unless explicitly shared", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    const hidden = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("p3", { includePrivate: false }),
    });
    const shared = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("p4", { includePrivate: true }),
    });
    const hiddenPayload = (hidden.json() as { packet: { payload: Record<string, unknown> } }).packet
      .payload;
    const sharedPayload = (shared.json() as { packet: { payload: Record<string, unknown> } }).packet
      .payload;
    expect(hiddenPayload.observations).toEqual([{ metric: "heart_rate", value: 80 }]);
    expect(hiddenPayload.trends).toEqual([]);
    expect(sharedPayload.observations).toHaveLength(2);
    const sharedTrends = sharedPayload.trends as Array<{ metric: string }>;
    expect(sharedTrends[0]?.metric).toBe("stress");
  });

  it("sets the visible time from the compressed scenario delay", async () => {
    const { app, advance } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "PUT",
      url: "/v1/link",
      headers: crew,
      payload: { state: "delayed", environment: "station" },
    });
    const created = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("p5"),
    });
    const packet = (created.json() as { packet: { sentAt: string; visibleAt: string } }).packet;
    expect(new Date(packet.visibleAt).getTime() - new Date(packet.sentAt).getTime()).toBe(1000);
    advance(500);
    const early = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/packets/p5?crewId=alex-morgan",
      headers: crew,
    });
    expect((early.json() as { packet: { state: string } }).packet.state).toBe("sent");
  });

  it("scopes packet reads to the named crew member", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("p6"),
    });
    const mine = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/packets/p6?crewId=alex-morgan",
      headers: crew,
    });
    const other = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/packets/p6?crewId=kenji-sato",
      headers: crew,
    });
    expect(mine.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect(other.statusCode).toBe(404);
  });

  it("rejects unauthenticated requests with a stable error shape", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    const response = await app.inject({ method: "GET", url: "/v1/link" });
    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(response.json()).toEqual({
      error: {
        code: "UNAUTHORIZED",
        message: "Send Authorization: Bearer <key> with a valid key.",
      },
    });
  });
});

export type { FastifyInstance };

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildApp } from "../src/server.js";
import { loadConfig } from "../src/config.js";
import { openDatabase } from "../src/db.js";
import { runDueTransitions } from "../src/scheduler.js";
import { crew, ground, makeApp, packetBody, tempDbPath, type TestApp } from "./helpers.js";

let current: TestApp | null = null;

afterEach(async () => {
  await current?.app.close();
  current = null;
});

async function delayedStation() {
  current = await makeApp();
  const { app } = current;
  await app.inject({
    method: "PUT",
    url: "/v1/link",
    headers: crew,
    payload: { state: "delayed", environment: "station" },
  });
  await app.inject({ method: "POST", url: "/v1/packets", headers: crew, payload: packetBody("s1") });
  return current;
}

describe("delivery and acknowledgement timing", () => {
  it("delivers only after the visible time and exactly once", async () => {
    const test = await delayedStation();
    expect(runDueTransitions(test.app.ctx, test.now()).delivered).toEqual([]);
    test.advance(1000);
    expect(runDueTransitions(test.app.ctx, test.now()).delivered).toEqual(["s1"]);
    test.advance(5000);
    expect(runDueTransitions(test.app.ctx, test.now()).delivered).toEqual([]);
    const events = test.app.ctx.db
      .prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM packet_events WHERE packet_id = 's1' AND state = 'delivered'")
      .get() as { c: number };
    expect(events.c).toBe(1);
  });

  it("survives a restart and delivers once, on time", async () => {
    const temp = tempDbPath();
    let clock = new Date("2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z");
    const config = loadConfig({ DB_PATH: temp.path } as NodeJS.ProcessEnv);
    const first = await buildApp(config, { now: () => clock, scheduler: false });
    await first.inject({
      method: "PUT",
      url: "/v1/link",
      headers: crew,
      payload: { state: "delayed", environment: "station" },
    });
    await first.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/packets",
      headers: crew,
      payload: packetBody("restart-1"),
    });
    await first.close();

    clock = new Date(clock.getTime() + 1000);
    const db = openDatabase(temp.path);
    const second = await buildApp(config, { db, now: () => clock, scheduler: false });
    expect(runDueTransitions(second.ctx, clock).delivered).toEqual(["restart-1"]);
    expect(runDueTransitions(second.ctx, clock).delivered).toEqual([]);
    const row = db.prepare("SELECT state FROM packets WHERE id = 'restart-1'").get() as {
      state: string;
    };
    expect(row.state).toBe("delivered");
    await second.close();
    db.close();
    temp.cleanup();
  });

  it("rejects acknowledgement before delivery", async () => {
    const test = await delayedStation();
    const response = await test.app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/surgeon/packets/s1/ack",
      headers: ground,
    });
    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(409);
    expect((response.json() as { error: { code: string } }).error.code).toBe("NOT_DELIVERED");
  });

  it("makes the ack visible only after the return delay", async () => {
    const test = await delayedStation();
    test.advance(1000);
    runDueTransitions(test.app.ctx, test.now());

    const ack = await test.app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/surgeon/packets/s1/ack",
      headers: ground,
    });
    const acked = (ack.json() as { packet: { state: string; ackVisibleAt: string } }).packet;
    expect(acked.state).toBe("delivered");

    test.advance(500);
    const early = await test.app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/packets/s1?crewId=alex-morgan",
      headers: crew,
    });
    expect((early.json() as { packet: { state: string } }).packet.state).toBe("delivered");

    test.advance(600);
    runDueTransitions(test.app.ctx, test.now());
    const after = await test.app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/packets/s1?crewId=alex-morgan",
      headers: crew,
    });
    expect((after.json() as { packet: { state: string } }).packet.state).toBe("acknowledged");
  });

  it("treats a second acknowledgement as idempotent", async () => {
    const test = await delayedStation();
    test.advance(1000);
    runDueTransitions(test.app.ctx, test.now());
    const first = await test.app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/surgeon/packets/s1/ack",
      headers: ground,
    });
    const second = await test.app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/surgeon/packets/s1/ack",
      headers: ground,
    });
    expect(second.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((second.json() as { alreadyAcknowledged: boolean }).alreadyAcknowledged).toBe(true);
    const firstAck = (first.json() as { packet: { ackAt: string } }).packet.ackAt;
    const secondAck = (second.json() as { packet: { ackAt: string } }).packet.ackAt;
    expect(secondAck).toBe(firstAck);
  });

  it("hides undelivered packets from the surgeon inbox", async () => {
    const test = await delayedStation();
    const inbox = await test.app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/surgeon/inbox",
      headers: ground,
    });
    expect((inbox.json() as { packets: unknown[] }).packets).toHaveLength(0);
    test.advance(1000);
    runDueTransitions(test.app.ctx, test.now());
    const later = await test.app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/surgeon/inbox",
      headers: ground,
    });
    expect((later.json() as { packets: unknown[] }).packets).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

 FILE: backend/test/sync.test.ts

================================================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { crew, makeApp, type TestApp } from "./helpers.js";

let current: TestApp | null = null;

afterEach(async () => {
  await current?.app.close();
  current = null;
});

function observation(id: string, overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    id,
    crewId: "alex-morgan",
    metric: "heart_rate",
    value: 80,
    unit: "bpm",
    observedAt: "2025-12-31T22:00:00.000Z",
    enteredAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    lane: "manual",
    method: "manual_entry",
    context: "rest",
    quality: "ok",
    qualityReason: null,
    environment: "mars_transit",
    procedureVersion: "demo-procedures-1",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

async function setup(): Promise<TestApp> {
  current = await makeApp();
  return current;
}

describe("sync", () => {
  it("upserts idempotently and advances the cursor", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    const first = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations",
      headers: crew,
      payload: [observation("o1"), observation("o2")],
    });
    expect(first.statusCode).toBe(200);
    expect((first.json() as { cursor: number }).cursor).toBe(2);

    const again = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations",
      headers: crew,
      payload: [observation("o1")],
    });
    expect((again.json() as { applied: number }).applied).toBe(1);
    const count = app.ctx.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM observations").get() as { c: number };
    expect(count.c).toBe(2);

    const pulled = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations?crewId=alex-morgan&since=0",
      headers: crew,
    });
    expect((pulled.json() as { items: unknown[] }).items).toHaveLength(2);
    const next = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: `/v1/sync/observations?crewId=alex-morgan&since=${(again.json() as { cursor: number }).cursor}`,
      headers: crew,
    });
    expect((next.json() as { items: unknown[] }).items).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("applies staleness only to manual and live rows", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations",
      headers: crew,
      payload: [
        observation("manual-stale"),
        observation("replay-old", { lane: "replay", method: "actigraphy" }),
        observation("synthetic-old", { lane: "synthetic" }),
        observation("fresh", { observedAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z" }),
      ],
    });
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations?crewId=alex-morgan&since=0",
      headers: crew,
    });
    const items = (response.json() as { items: Array<{ id: string; quality: string }> }).items;
    const quality = Object.fromEntries(items.map((item) => [item.id, item.quality]));
    expect(quality["manual-stale"]).toBe("stale");
    expect(quality["replay-old"]).toBe("ok");
    expect(quality["synthetic-old"]).toBe("ok");
    expect(quality.fresh).toBe("ok");
  });

  it("keeps the stricter flag when client and server disagree", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations",
      headers: crew,
      payload: [
        observation("implausible", { value: 400 }),
        observation("client-flagged", { value: 80, quality: "no_contact" }),
      ],
    });
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations?crewId=alex-morgan&since=0",
      headers: crew,
    });
    const items = (
      response.json() as {
        items: Array<{ id: string; quality: string; qualityReason: string | null }>;
      }
    ).items;
    const implausible = items.find((item) => item.id === "implausible");
    expect(implausible?.quality).toBe("out_of_range");
    expect(implausible?.qualityReason).toContain("Server gate");
    expect(items.find((item) => item.id === "client-flagged")?.quality).toBe("no_contact");
  });

  it("stores late manual entries by observed time without inventing a value", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations",
      headers: crew,
      payload: [
        observation("late", {
          observedAt: "2025-12-31T18:00:00.000Z",
          enteredAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
        }),
        observation("missing", { value: null, quality: "not_measured" }),
      ],
    });
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "GET",
      url: "/v1/sync/observations?crewId=alex-morgan&since=0",
      headers: crew,
    });
    const items = (response.json() as { items: Array<{ id: string; quality: string }> }).items;
    expect(items.find((item) => item.id === "late")?.quality).toBe("stale");
    expect(items.find((item) => item.id === "missing")?.quality).toBe("not_measured");
  });

  it("rejects unknown collections", async () => {
    const { app } = await setup();
    const response = await app.inject({
      method: "POST",
      url: "/v1/sync/notes",
      headers: crew,
      payload: [],
    });
    expect(response.statusCode).toBe(404);
  });
});

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import "./globals.css";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/shell/app-shell";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "CrewSignal — Mars Transit Health Companion",
  description:
    "An offline-first health companion for a Mars transit crew. Demo software, not a medical device.",
  applicationName: "CrewSignal",
  icons: {
    icon: [
      { url: "/icon-light-32x32.png", media: "(prefers-color-scheme: light)" },
      { url: "/icon-dark-32x32.png", media: "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" },
      { url: "/icon.svg", type: "image/svg+xml" },
    ],
    apple: "/apple-icon.png",
  },
};

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  colorScheme: "light dark",
  themeColor: [
    { media: "(prefers-color-scheme: light)", color: "white" },
    { media: "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)", color: "black" },
  ],
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body className="antialiased">
        <AppShell>{children}</AppShell>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
