"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, Info, PencilLine } from "lucide-react";
import { readings } from "@/lib/readings";
import { formatValue } from "@/lib/metrics";
import {
  dailyTrend,
  lastRecordedText,
  latest,
  statusFor,
} from "@/lib/observations/derived";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import { LaneBadge, StatusChip } from "@/components/health/status-chip";
import { TrustRing } from "@/components/health/trust-ring";
import { MetricChart } from "@/components/health/metric-chart";
import type { Metric, Observation } from "@/lib/types";

const groupTitles = {
  live: "Live sensors",
  manual: "Entered by you",
  static: "Static data",
} as const;

export function AllReadings({
  crewId,
  observations,
  onManualEntry,
  onOpenGuide,
}: {
  crewId: string;
  observations: Observation[];
  onManualEntry: () => void;
  onOpenGuide: (key: string) => void;
}) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const privateUnlocked = useStore((state) => state.privateUnlocked);

  return (
    <section className="all-readings" aria-labelledby="all-readings-title">
      <div className="section-head">
        <h2 id="all-readings-title">All readings</h2>
        <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={onManualEntry}>
          <PencilLine aria-hidden="true" /> Add manual reading
        </button>
      </div>

      {(["live", "manual", "static"] as const).map((group) => (
        <div className="reading-group" key={group}>
          <h3 className="reading-group-title">{groupTitles[group]}</h3>
          <ul>
            {readings
              .filter((reading) => reading.group === group)
              .map((reading) => {
                const isPrivate = !!reading.private;
                const locked = isPrivate && !privateUnlocked;
                const observation = reading.metric
                  ? latest(observations.filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId), reading.metric)
                  : null;
                const comparison =
                  reading.metric && !locked
                    ? statusFor(observations, crewId, reading.metric)
                    : null;
                const open = expanded === reading.key;
                const value =
                  reading.group === "static"
                    ? "2.4 mSv"
                    : locked
                      ? "Private"
                      : observation
                        ? formatValue(reading.metric as Metric, observation.value)
                        : "—";

                return (
                  <li key={reading.key} className="reading-item">
                    <div className="reading-row">
                      <div className="reading-row-main">
                        <div className="reading-name">
                          <strong>{reading.name}</strong>
                          <span className="reading-source">
                            {reading.group === "static"
                              ? "Dosimeter, read on schedule"
                              : observation
                                ? `Updated ${new Date(observation.observedAt).toLocaleTimeString(
                                    "en-GB",
                                    { timeZone: "UTC", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" },
                                  )} UTC`
                                : "Nothing recorded"}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="reading-value">
                          <b>{value}</b>
                          {observation && !locked && <LaneBadge lane={observation.lane} />}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="reading-row-side">
                        {comparison && <StatusChip comparison={comparison} compact />}
                        {!locked && (
                          <TrustRing
                            observation={
                              observation ??
                              (reading.group === "static"
                                ? ({
                                    id: "radiation-static",
                                    crewId,
                                    metric: "skin_temp",
                                    value: 2.4,
                                    unit: "mSv",
                                    observedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                                    enteredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                                    lane: "synthetic",
                                    method: "actigraphy",
                                    context: "unknown",
                                    quality: "ok",
                                    qualityReason: null,
                                    environment: "mars_transit",
                                    procedureVersion: "demo",
                                  } as Observation)
                                : null)
                            }
                          />
                        )}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="icon-button"
                          aria-expanded={open}
                          aria-label={`More about ${reading.name}`}
                          onClick={() => setExpanded(open ? null : reading.key)}
                        >
                          <ChevronDown aria-hidden="true" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {open && (
                      <div className="reading-expanded">
                        <p>
                          <strong>What it is.</strong> {reading.whatItIs}
                        </p>
                        <p>
                          <strong>Tell the crew when.</strong> {reading.tellCrewWhen}
                        </p>
                        {observation && (
                          <p className="reading-last">
                            {lastRecordedText(observation)} Applied status:{" "}
                            {comparison?.label ?? "not compared"}.
                          </p>
                        )}
                        {reading.metric && observation && (
                          <MetricChart
                            points={dailyTrend(
                              observations.filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId),
                              reading.metric,
                            )}
                            metric={reading.metric}
                            label={`${reading.name} daily trend`}
                            band={comparison?.range ?? null}
                            height={110}
                          />
                        )}
                        <div className="reading-expanded-actions">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            className="text-button"
                            onClick={() => onOpenGuide(reading.key)}
                          >
                            <Info aria-hidden="true" /> Measurement guide
                          </button>
                          {reading.metric && (
                            <span className="lane-hint">
                              Lane: {observation?.lane ?? "none"} · Lanes are never merged.
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </li>
                );
              })}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/components/health/concern-card.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowRight, Repeat } from "lucide-react";
import { hazardFor } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import type { Concern } from "@/lib/types";

export function ConcernCard({
  concern,
  crewId,
  onRepeat,
  onHandoff,
  onAssessment,
}: {
  concern: Concern;
  crewId: string;
  onRepeat: () => void;
  onHandoff: () => void;
  onAssessment: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <article className="concern-card" aria-labelledby={`${concern.id}-title`}>
      <header>
        <span className="concern-kicker">Concern · last {concern.windowHours} hours</span>
        <h3 id={`${concern.id}-title`}>{concern.title}</h3>
        <p className="concern-summary">{concern.summary}</p>
      </header>

      <ul className="concern-signals">
        {concern.signals.map((signal, index) => (
          <li key={`${signal.observedAt}-${index}`}>
            <span className="signal-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>{signal.text}</span>
            <time dateTime={signal.observedAt}>
              {new Date(signal.observedAt).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
                timeZone: "UTC",
                day: "2-digit",
                month: "short",
                hour: "2-digit",
                minute: "2-digit",
              })}{" "}
              UTC
            </time>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>

      <div className="concern-hazards">
        {concern.hazards.map((tag) => {
          const hazard = hazardFor(tag);
          return (
            <span key={tag} className="hazard-tag" title={hazard.plain}>
              {hazard.label}
            </span>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="concern-actions">
        <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={onRepeat}>
          <Repeat aria-hidden="true" /> Repeat measurement
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={onAssessment}>
          Start guided assessment
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={onHandoff}>
          Prepare handoff <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="disclaimer-line">
        These signals changed in the same window. No cause is assigned.{" "}
        <Link href={`/crew/${crewId}`}>See all readings</Link>
      </p>
    </article>
  );
}

 FILE: frontend/components/health/crew-nav.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { ChevronLeft, Moon, ShieldAlert, Sun, Text, Waves } from "lucide-react";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";

export function CrewNav({ crewId }: { crewId?: string }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const theme = useStore((state) => state.theme);
  const toggleTheme = useStore((state) => state.toggleTheme);
  const largeText = useStore((state) => state.largeText);
  const setLargeText = useStore((state) => state.setLargeText);
  const nightWatch = useStore((state) => state.nightWatch);
  const setNightWatch = useStore((state) => state.setNightWatch);

  const tabs = crewId
    ? [
        { href: `/crew/${crewId}`, label: "Daily" },
        { href: "/reports", label: "Handoff" },
        { href: "/outbox", label: "Outbox" },
      ]
    : [
        { href: "/reports", label: "Handoff" },
        { href: "/outbox", label: "Outbox" },
      ];

  return (
    <nav className="nav-bar" aria-label="Primary">
      <Link className="nav-back" href={crewId ? "/" : "/"}>
        <ChevronLeft aria-hidden="true" /> {crewId ? "Crew" : "Home"}
      </Link>
      <div className="nav-tabs">
        {tabs.map((tab) => (
          <Link
            key={tab.href}
            href={tab.href}
            className={pathname === tab.href ? "active" : ""}
            aria-current={pathname === tab.href ? "page" : undefined}
          >
            {tab.label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="nav-actions">
        <button
          className="icon-button"
          aria-pressed={largeText}
          onClick={() => setLargeText(!largeText)}
          aria-label="Large text mode"
          title="Large text mode"
        >
          <Text />
        </button>
        <button
          className="icon-button"
          aria-pressed={nightWatch}
          onClick={() => setNightWatch(!nightWatch)}
          aria-label="Night Watch red-shift theme"
          title="Night Watch red-shift theme"
        >
          <Waves />
        </button>
        <button
          className="icon-button"
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          aria-label={theme === "dark" ? "Switch to light mode" : "Switch to dark mode"}
        >
          {theme === "dark" ? <Sun /> : <Moon />}
        </button>
        <Link className="nav-emergency" href="/emergency">
          <ShieldAlert aria-hidden="true" /> Emergency
        </Link>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

export function DashboardPage({ crewId }: { crewId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const member = missionConfig.crew.find((entry) => entry.id === crewId);
  const observations = useStore((state) => state.observations);
  const checkIns = useStore((state) => state.checkIns);
  const link = useStore((state) => state.link);
  const addObservations = useStore((state) => state.addObservations);

  const notify = useStore((state) => state.notify);

  const simulation = useHealthSimulation(
    member?.restingHeartRate ?? 70,
    crewId,
    member?.id === "alex-morgan" ? 0 : 0,
  );

  const [area, setArea] = useState<BodyArea>("Heart");
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<BodyMapMode>("status");
  const [checkInOpen, setCheckInOpen] = useState(false);
  const [manualOpen, setManualOpen] = useState(false);
  const [guideKey, setGuideKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [scenarioOpen, setScenarioOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isDemo, setIsDemo] = useState(false);
  const [replayNote, setReplayNote] = useState("");
  const [reminderNote, setReminderNote] = useState("");
  // The vitals object changes every second; keep it in a ref so the 30 s
  // persistence timer is not torn down before it can fire.
  const vitalsRef = useRef(simulation.vitals);
  vitalsRef.current = simulation.vitals;
  const qualityRef = useRef(simulation.quality);
  qualityRef.current = simulation.quality;
  const remoteAdapter = useRef<RemoteSensorAdapter | null>(null);

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      remoteAdapter.current?.disconnect();
      remoteAdapter.current = null;
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsDemo(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("demo") === "1");
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!member) return;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (qualityRef.current !== "Usable") return;
      const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
      const rows: Observation[] = [
        makeObservation({
          crewId,
          metric: "heart_rate",
          value: vitalsRef.current.heartRate,
          observedAt: nowIso,
          lane: "live",
          method: "ppg_wrist",
          context: "rest",
        }),
        makeObservation({
          crewId,
          metric: "skin_temp",
          value: vitalsRef.current.skinTemperature,
          observedAt: nowIso,
          lane: "live",
          method: "thermometer_skin",
          context: "rest",
        }),
      ];
      void addObservations(rows);
    }, 30_000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [addObservations, crewId, member]);

  const liveToday = useMemo<Partial<Record<Metric, ChartPoint[]>>>(() => {
    const stamp = (index: number) =>
      new Date(Date.now() - (59 - index) * 1000).toISOString();
    const usable = simulation.quality === "Usable";
    const build = (values: Array<number | null>) =>
      values.map((value, index) => ({
        value,
        at: stamp(index),
        lane: "live" as const,
        quality: usable && value !== null ? ("ok" as const) : ("no_contact" as const),
      }));
    return {
      heart_rate: build(simulation.histories.heartRate),
      skin_temp: build(simulation.histories.skinTemperature),
      bp_systolic: build(simulation.histories.bloodPressure),
      motion: build(simulation.histories.motion),
    };
  }, [simulation.histories, simulation.quality]);

  const crewObservations = useMemo(
    () => observations.filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId),
    [observations, crewId],
  );
  const crewCheckIns = useMemo(
    () => checkIns.filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId),
    [checkIns, crewId],
  );

  const statuses = useMemo(
    () => statusByArea(observations, crewId),
    [observations, crewId],
  );
  const concerns = useMemo(
    () => buildConcerns(crewObservations, crewCheckIns),
    [crewObservations, crewCheckIns],
  );
  const tasks = useMemo(() => tasksFor(crewId, crewCheckIns), [crewCheckIns, crewId]);
  const streak = useMemo(() => checkInStreak(crewId, crewCheckIns), [crewCheckIns, crewId]);
  const latestCheckIn = crewCheckIns
    .slice()
    .sort((a, b) => (a.observedAt < b.observedAt ? 1 : -1))[0];
  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const todayCheckIn = latestCheckIn?.observedAt.slice(0, 10) === today ? latestCheckIn : null;

  const urgentSymptoms = (todayCheckIn?.symptoms ?? []).filter((symptom) => symptom.urgent);
  const attentionCount = Object.values(statuses).filter((state) => state === "attention").length;
  const noValidCount = Object.values(statuses).filter(
    (state) => state === "no_valid_reading",
  ).length;
  const withinCount = Object.values(statuses).filter((state) => state === "within_range").length;

  const guideReading = guideKey ? readings.find((entry) => entry.key === guideKey) : null;

  const repeatMeasurement = () => {
    setManualOpen(true);
    notify("Add a repeat reading. The previous value stays in the log.", "neutral");
  };

  const requestSupport = async () => {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    await enqueue({
      id: `support-${crewId}-${Date.now()}`,
      crewId,
      kind: "support",
      createdAt: now,
      sentAt: null,
      acknowledgedAt: null,
      state: "queued",
      delaySeconds: 20 * 60,
      title: "Private support request",
      payload: { crewId, requestedAt: now },
      private: true,
      history: [
        {
          state: "queued",
          at: now,
          note:
            link === "blackout"
              ? "Link is in blackout. Held on board."
              : "Waiting for the configured one-way delay.",
        },
      ],
    });
    notify("Private request saved to the outbox. Nothing is sent yet.", "ok");
  };

  const applyScenario = (scenario: "shift" | "unplug" | "sleep" | "lightheaded") => {
    if (scenario === "unplug") {
      simulation.onQualityOverrideChange("Poor contact");
      notify("Scenario: sensor unplugged. Values will stop entering comparisons.", "attention");
      return;
    }
    if (scenario === "shift") {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      void addObservations([
        makeObservation({
          crewId,
          metric: "heart_rate",
          value: 96,
          observedAt: now,
          lane: "live",
          method: "ppg_wrist",
          context: "rest",
        }),
      ]);
      notify("Scenario: baseline shift applied to resting heart rate.", "attention");
      return;
    }
    if (scenario === "sleep") {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      void addObservations(
        [1, 2, 3].map((offset) =>
          makeObservation({
            crewId,
            metric: "sleep_minutes",
            value: 250,
            observedAt: new Date(Date.now() - offset * 86_400_000).toISOString(),
            lane: "manual",
            method: "self_report",
            context: "unknown",
          }),
        ),
      );
      notify("Scenario: poor-sleep week added. Expect a concern, not a diagnosis.", "attention");
      return;
    }
    const now = new Date();
    const checkIn = {
      id: `checkin-${crewId}-${now.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`,
      crewId,
      observedAt: now.toISOString(),
      enteredAt: now.toISOString(),
      sleepMinutes: 300,
      mood: 2,
      stress: 4,
      rested: 2,
      connected: 3,
      symptoms: [
        {
          id: "scenario-dizziness",
          name: "Dizziness",
          onset: "today" as const,
          trend: "worse" as const,
          urgent: false,
        },
      ],
      details: "Scenario: light-headedness after standing.",
    };
    void useStore.getState().saveCheckIn(checkIn);
    void addObservations(observationsFromCheckIn(checkIn));
    notify("Scenario: light-headedness recorded. A concern should appear.", "attention");
  };

  if (!member) {
    return (
      <section className="dashboard">
        <CrewNav />
        <div className="empty-state">
          <h1>Crew member not found</h1>
          <Link className="primary-button" href="/">
            Back to crew
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const pathway = pathwayForSymptoms(
    (todayCheckIn?.symptoms ?? []).map((symptom) => symptom.name),
  );

  return (
    <section className="dashboard">
      <CrewNav crewId={crewId} />

      <header className="welcome">
        <div className="welcome-person">
          <span className={`avatar ${member.color}`}>{member.initials}</span>
          <div>
            <h1>{member.name}</h1>
            <p>
              {member.role} · {missionConfig.missionName} · Day {missionConfig.missionDay}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="welcome-actions">
          <span className="link-inline">
            <Radio aria-hidden="true" /> {link}
          </span>
          <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => setCheckInOpen(true)}>
            <ClipboardList aria-hidden="true" /> Daily check-in
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="status-strip" role="region" aria-label="Today at a glance">
        <div className="status-strip-counts">
          <span className="status-count attention">
            <AlertTriangle aria-hidden="true" /> {attentionCount} need attention
          </span>
          <span className="status-count neutral">{withinCount} within your range</span>
          <span className="status-count muted">{noValidCount} no valid reading</span>
        </div>
        <div className="status-strip-tasks">
          <span>
            <CalendarCheck aria-hidden="true" /> {streak} checks completed in a row
          </span>
          <ul>
            {tasks.map((task) => (
              <li key={task.id} className={`task-${task.state}`}>
                <span className="task-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
                {task.label}
                <em>
                  {task.state === "done"
                    ? "done"
                    : task.state === "overdue"
                      ? "overdue"
                      : task.state === "due"
                        ? "due now"
                        : `due ${String(task.dueHourUtc).padStart(2, "0")}:00 UTC`}
                </em>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>

      {urgentSymptoms.length > 0 && (
        <div className="urgent-banner" role="alert">
          <AlertTriangle aria-hidden="true" />
          <div>
            <strong>Reported symptom needs attention now.</strong>
            <p>
              {urgentSymptoms.map((symptom) => symptom.name).join(", ")}. A reported symptom
              outranks any reassuring number.
            </p>
            <div className="urgent-actions">
              <Link className="primary-button" href="/emergency">
                Start emergency help
              </Link>
              <Link className="secondary-button" href={`/crew/${crewId}/assess/${pathway.id}`}>
                Start guided assessment
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {concerns.map((concern) => (
        <ConcernCard
          key={concern.id}
          concern={concern}
          crewId={crewId}
          onRepeat={repeatMeasurement}
          onHandoff={() => router.push("/reports")}
          onAssessment={() => router.push(`/crew/${crewId}/assess/${pathway.id}`)}
        />
      ))}

      <div className="dashboard-grid">
        <div className="body-card body-card-map">
          <div className="section-head">
            <h2>Body map</h2>
            <div className="segmented" role="group" aria-label="Body map mode">
              <button
                type="button"
                className={mode === "status" ? "active" : ""}
                aria-pressed={mode === "status"}
                onClick={() => setMode("status")}
              >
                Status
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={mode === "coverage" ? "active" : ""}
                aria-pressed={mode === "coverage"}
                onClick={() => setMode("coverage")}
              >
                Coverage
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <BodyMap
            selected={area}
            mode={mode}
            statuses={statuses}
            labels={{}}
            onSelect={setArea}
          />
          <p className="body-map-note">
            {mode === "coverage"
              ? "Hatched areas have not been assessed. Not assessed is never shown as green."
              : "Colour shows how this reading compares with your own recent range."}
          </p>
          <div className="map-chips">
            {(Object.keys(statuses) as BodyArea[]).map((key) => (
              <button
                key={key}
                type="button"
                className={`map-chip ${area === key ? "selected" : ""} status-${statuses[key]}`}
                onClick={() => setArea(key)}
              >
                {key}
              </button>
            ))}
            <button
              type="button"
              className={`map-chip ${area === "Radiation" ? "selected" : ""}`}
              onClick={() => setArea("Radiation")}
            >
              Radiation
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <ReadingDetails
          crewId={crewId}
          area={area}
          observations={observations}
          liveToday={liveToday}
          sensorUnplugged={simulation.quality !== "Usable"}
          onOpenGuide={(key) => key && setGuideKey(key)}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="dashboard-actions">
        <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={() => setManualOpen(true)}>
          <Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Add manual reading
        </button>
        <Link className="secondary-button" href={`/crew/${crewId}/assess/${pathway.id}`}>
          Guided assessment
        </Link>
        <Link className="secondary-button" href="/reports">
          Prepare handoff
        </Link>
        <Link className="secondary-button" href="/outbox">
          Outbox
        </Link>
      </div>

      <AllReadings
        crewId={crewId}
        observations={observations}
        onManualEntry={() => setManualOpen(true)}
        onOpenGuide={setGuideKey}
      />

      <WellbeingTile
        crewId={crewId}
        checkIns={checkIns}
        onRequestSupport={() => void requestSupport()}
      />

      <HazardStrip
        highlight={concerns.flatMap((concern) => concern.hazards)}
      />

      <section className="scenario-panel" aria-label="Data tools">
        <div className="section-head">
          <h2>Data tools</h2>
          <span className="lane-hint">Ground replay is never mixed with live data.</span>
        </div>
        <div className="scenario-actions">
          <label className="replay-upload">
            Import bed-rest CSV (replay lane)
            <input
              type="file"
              accept=".csv,text/csv"
              onChange={async (event) => {
                const file = event.target.files?.[0];
                if (!file) return;
                const text = await file.text();
                const { rows } = parseBedRestCsv(text);
                if (rows.length === 0) {
                  setReplayNote("No rows recognised. Expected Subject, Day, Phase and a sleep or heart-rate column.");
                  return;
                }
                const observations = replayObservations(rows, crewId);
                await addObservations(observations);
                const participants = new Set(rows.map((row) => row.participant)).size;
                setReplayNote(
                  `Imported ${observations.length} replay observations from ${participants} participants. Ground analog from three participants.`,
                );
              }}
            />
          </label>
          <a className="text-button" href="/sample-bed-rest.csv" download>
            Sample CSV
          </a>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              const simulated = new SimulatedAdapter();
              void simulated.connect();
              notify("Simulated sensor adapter connected. Lanes stay separate.", "ok");
            }}
          >
            Connect simulated sensor
          </button>
          {apiConfigured && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={async () => {
                remoteAdapter.current?.disconnect();
                const adapter = new RemoteSensorAdapter();
                remoteAdapter.current = adapter;
                try {
                  await adapter.connect();
                  adapter.onSample((sample) => {
                    void addObservations([sample]);
                  });
                  adapter.onQuality((quality) => {
                    setReplayNote(
                      quality === "ok"
                        ? "Remote sensor is reporting usable samples."
                        : "Remote sensor lost contact. Values stop entering comparisons.",
                    );
                  });
                  notify("Remote sensor connected to the ground stream.", "ok");
                } catch (error) {
                  notify(
                    error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Remote sensor failed.",
                    "attention",
                  );
                }
              }}
            >
              Connect remote sensor (SSE)
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={async () => {
              const serial = new WebSerialAdapter();
              try {
                await serial.connect();
              } catch (error) {
                notify(
                  error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Web Serial is unavailable.",
                  "attention",
                );
              }
            }}
          >
            Web Serial pulse sensor (stub)
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={async () => {
              if (typeof Notification === "undefined") {
                setReminderNote("This browser has no notification support. In-app reminder only.");
                return;
              }
              const permission = await Notification.requestPermission();
              if (permission !== "granted") {
                setReminderNote("Notifications are blocked. Reminders stay in the app.");
                return;
              }
              const overdue = tasks.find((task) => task.state === "overdue") ?? tasks[0];
              new Notification("CrewSignal reminder", {
                body: `${overdue.label}: ${overdue.detail}`,
              });
              setReminderNote(`Reminder sent for ${overdue.label}.`);
            }}
          >
            Remind me about tasks
          </button>
          {replayNote && <p>{replayNote}</p>}
          {reminderNote && <p>{reminderNote}</p>}
        </div>
      </section>

      {isDemo && (
        <section className="scenario-panel" aria-label="Scenario panel">
          <div className="section-head">
            <h2>Scenario panel (demo)</h2>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="text-button"
              onClick={() => setScenarioOpen((value) => !value)}
            >
              {scenarioOpen ? "Hide" : "Show"}
            </button>
          </div>
          {scenarioOpen && (
            <div className="scenario-actions">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => applyScenario("shift")}>
                Baseline shift
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => applyScenario("unplug")}>
                Sensor unplugged
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => applyScenario("sleep")}>
                Poor-sleep week
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => applyScenario("lightheaded")}>
                Light-headedness
              </button>
              <p>
                Environment: {environment}. Scenarios change data, never status rules.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      )}

      {checkInOpen && (
        <FormDrawer crewId={crewId} initial={todayCheckIn} onClose={() => setCheckInOpen(false)} />
      )}
      {manualOpen && <ManualEntry crewId={crewId} onClose={() => setManualOpen(false)} />}
      {guideReading && (
        <MeasureGuide
          reading={guideReading}
          onClose={() => setGuideKey(null)}
          onUnable={async () => {
            if (!guideReading.metric) return;
            await addObservations([
              makeObservation({
                crewId,
                metric: guideReading.metric,
                value: null,
                observedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                lane: "manual",
                method: "manual_entry",
                context: "unknown",
                quality: "not_measured",
              }),
            ]);
            setGuideKey(null);
            notify("Recorded as unable to measure. No value was invented.", "neutral");
          }}
        />
      )}
      <p className="disclaimer-line">
        Demo software. {readingFor("heart_rate")?.guide.whyInSpaceflight}
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ShieldAlert, Volume2 } from "lucide-react";
import { PathwayRunner } from "@/components/health/pathway-runner";
import { crew } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import { emergencyPathway } from "@/lib/pathways";

function speak(text: string) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || !("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text));
}

/**
 * Help a Crewmate. Opens instantly, works with no typing, and allows an
 * unknown identity. Every pathway is illustrative.
 */
export function EmergencyPage({ drill = false }: { drill?: boolean }) {
  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState<"Yes" | "No" | "Unsure" | null>(null);
  const [who, setWho] = useState<string>("unknown");

  const pathway = answer ? emergencyPathway(answer) : null;
  const question = "Is the person responding?";

  return (
    <section className="emergency-screen">
      <header className="assessment-head">
        <p className="illustrative-label">ILLUSTRATIVE — NOT CLINICALLY REVIEWED</p>
        <h1>{drill ? "Drill: help a crewmate" : "Help a crewmate"}</h1>
        <p className="assessment-sub">
          No typing needed. Identity can stay unknown. Nothing here is a diagnosis.
        </p>
      </header>

      {!pathway ? (
        <div className="step-card emergency-start">
          <div className="step-title-row">
            <h2>{question}</h2>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="icon-button"
              aria-label="Read this question aloud"
              onClick={() => speak(question)}
            >
              <Volume2 aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="identity-row">
            <label htmlFor="emergency-who">Who needs help?</label>
            <select
              id="emergency-who"
              value={who}
              onChange={(event) => setWho(event.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="unknown">Unknown / not sure</option>
              {crew.map((member) => (
                <option key={member.id} value={member.id}>
                  {member.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="step-options emergency-options">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="step-option"
              onClick={() => setAnswer("Yes")}
            >
              Yes
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="step-option danger"
              onClick={() => setAnswer("No")}
            >
              No
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="step-option"
              onClick={() => setAnswer("Unsure")}
            >
              Unsure
            </button>
          </div>
          <p className="disclaimer-line">
            <ShieldAlert aria-hidden="true" /> Each answer opens a different pathway. If in
            doubt, alert the crew.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <PathwayRunner
          crewId={who}
          pathwayId={pathway.id}
          drill={drill}
          symptoms={[]}
        />
      )}

      <div className="emergency-footer">
        <Link className="secondary-button" href={drill ? "/emergency" : "/emergency/drill"}>
          {drill ? "Exit drill" : "Practice this flow (drill)"}
        </Link>
        <Link className="secondary-button" href="/">
          Home
        </Link>
        {answer && (
          <button
            type="button"
            className="text-button"
            onClick={() => setAnswer(null)}
          >
            Start over
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import { ArrowLeft, ChevronRight, Radio, Send, ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { crew, type CrewMember } from "./health-data";

function EmergencyScreen({
  time,
  who,
  story,
  step,
  saveReport,
  onBack,
  onWhoSelect,
  onStoryChange,
  onContinue,
  onReportView,
}: {
  time: Date;
  who: string;
  story: string;
  step: number;
  saveReport: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onWhoSelect: (name: string) => void;
  onStoryChange: (value: string) => void;
  onContinue: () => void;
  onReportView: () => void;
}) {
  const selectedPerson = crew.find((person) => person.name === who);

  return (
    <section className="emergency-screen">
      <header className="emergency-header">
        <button
          className="icon-button"
          onClick={onBack}
          aria-label="Close emergency helper"
        >
          <ArrowLeft />
        </button>
        <span>Emergency helper</span>
        <span className="offline-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" /> Works offline
        </span>
      </header>

      <div className="emergency-content">
        <div className="emergency-symbol">
          <ShieldAlert size={30} />
        </div>

        <div className="eyebrow">ILLUSTRATIVE · NOT CLINICALLY REVIEWED</div>
        <h1>Let&apos;s get help.</h1>
        <p>
          This app cannot tell the cause. We&apos;ll help you share what&apos;s
          happening.
        </p>

        {!who ? (
          <>
            <h2>Who needs help?</h2>
            <div className="who-grid">
              {crew.map((person) => (
                <button
                  key={person.name}
                  className="who-button"
                  onClick={() => onWhoSelect(person.name)}
                >
                  <span className="avatar">{person.initials}</span>
                  {person.name}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="selected-person">
              <span className="avatar">{selectedPerson?.initials}</span>
              <span>
                Helping <b>{who}</b>
              </span>
              <button className="text-button" onClick={() => onWhoSelect("")}>
                Change
              </button>
            </div>

            <label className="story-label" htmlFor="story">
              What happened?
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="story"
              value={story}
              onChange={(event) => onStoryChange(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Type what you noticed. You can edit this at any time."
            />

            <div className="voice-row">
              <button
                className="mic-button"
                aria-label="Voice is unavailable in this preview"
              >
                <span>●</span>
              </button>
              <span>Voice is not available, please type.</span>
            </div>

            <button
              className="primary-button"
              onClick={onContinue}
              disabled={!story.trim()}
            >
              Continue <ChevronRight />
            </button>

            {step > 0 && (
              <div className="helper-reply">
                <div className="helper-label">CREWSIGNAL HELPER</div>
                <p>
                  {step === 1
                    ? "Is their breathing as usual? If you are unsure, alert the crew now."
                    : step === 2
                      ? "Thank you. Stay with them and keep them comfortable."
                      : "Alert the crew and contact the medical officer or Earth now."}
                </p>
                <p className="safety-note">This app cannot tell the cause.</p>
                <button className="primary-button" onClick={onContinue}>
                  Continue
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}

        <div className="urgent-actions">
          <button onClick={saveReport}>
            <Radio /> Alert the crew
          </button>
          <button onClick={saveReport}>
            <Send /> Contact medical officer or Earth
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {who && story && (
        <div className="situation-box">
          <strong>
            Situation report ·{" "}
            {time.toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
              hour: "2-digit",
              minute: "2-digit",
            })}
          </strong>
          <p>
            {who || "Crew member"}: {story || "Emergency support requested."}
          </p>
          <button className="text-button" onClick={onReportView}>
            Add to Reports <ChevronRight />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

export { EmergencyScreen };

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/components/health/form-drawer.tsx
 lines: 377

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { BottomSheet } from "@/components/ui/bottom-sheet";
import { moodOptions } from "@/components/health/health-data";
import { onsetLabels, symptomCatalogue, trendLabels } from "@/lib/symptoms";
import { observationsFromCheckIn, makeObservation } from "@/lib/observations/derived";
import { pathwayForSymptoms } from "@/lib/pathways";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { CheckIn, SymptomEntry, SymptomOnset, SymptomTrend } from "@/lib/types";

function scale(value: string): number | null {
  const numeric = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(numeric) && numeric > 0 ? numeric : null;
}

export function FormDrawer({
  crewId,
  initial,
  onClose,
}: {
  crewId: string;
  initial?: CheckIn | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const saveCheckIn = useStore((state) => state.saveCheckIn);
  const addObservations = useStore((state) => state.addObservations);
  const notify = useStore((state) => state.notify);

  const [sleepHours, setSleepHours] = useState(
    initial?.sleepMinutes ? String(Math.floor(initial.sleepMinutes / 60)) : "",
  );
  const [sleepMinutes, setSleepMinutes] = useState(
    initial?.sleepMinutes ? String(initial.sleepMinutes % 60) : "",
  );
  const [bloodSugar, setBloodSugar] = useState("");
  const [bloodSugarUnavailable, setBloodSugarUnavailable] = useState(false);
  const [mood, setMood] = useState(initial?.mood ? String(initial.mood) : "");
  const [stress, setStress] = useState(initial?.stress ? String(initial.stress) : "");
  const [rested, setRested] = useState(initial?.rested ? String(initial.rested) : "");
  const [connected, setConnected] = useState(
    initial?.connected ? String(initial.connected) : "",
  );
  const [symptoms, setSymptoms] = useState<SymptomEntry[]>(initial?.symptoms ?? []);
  const [details, setDetails] = useState(initial?.details ?? "");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const urgent = symptoms.filter((symptom) => symptom.urgent);
  const hours = Number(sleepHours || 0);
  const minutes = Number(sleepMinutes || 0);

  const pathway = useMemo(
    () => pathwayForSymptoms(symptoms.map((symptom) => symptom.name)),
    [symptoms],
  );

  const toggleSymptom = (name: string, isUrgent: boolean) => {
    setSymptoms((current) => {
      const existing = current.find((symptom) => symptom.name === name);
      if (existing) return current.filter((symptom) => symptom.name !== name);
      return [
        ...current.filter((symptom) => symptom.name !== "None of these"),
        {
          id: `${name}-${Date.now()}`,
          name,
          onset: "today" as SymptomOnset,
          trend: "same" as SymptomTrend,
          urgent: isUrgent,
        },
      ];
    });
  };

  const updateSymptom = (name: string, patch: Partial<SymptomEntry>) => {
    setSymptoms((current) =>
      current.map((symptom) => (symptom.name === name ? { ...symptom, ...patch } : symptom)),
    );
  };

  const submit = async () => {
    setError("");
    if (sleepMinutes !== "" && (minutes < 0 || minutes > 59)) {
      setError("Sleep minutes must be between 0 and 59.");
      return;
    }
    if (sleepHours !== "" && (hours < 0 || hours > 24)) {
      setError("Sleep hours must be between 0 and 24.");
      return;
    }
    if (bloodSugar !== "" && !bloodSugarUnavailable) {
      const numeric = Number(bloodSugar);
      if (Number.isNaN(numeric) || numeric < 1 || numeric > 35) {
        setError(
          "Blood sugar is outside the plausible meter range (1–35 mmol/L). Repeat the measurement.",
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    const now = new Date();
    const observedAt = now.toISOString();
    const checkIn: CheckIn = {
      id: `checkin-${crewId}-${observedAt.slice(0, 10)}`,
      crewId,
      observedAt,
      enteredAt: observedAt,
      sleepMinutes: sleepHours === "" && sleepMinutes === "" ? null : hours * 60 + minutes,
      mood: scale(mood),
      stress: scale(stress),
      rested: scale(rested),
      connected: scale(connected),
      symptoms,
      details,
    };

    await saveCheckIn(checkIn);

    const rows = observationsFromCheckIn(checkIn);
    if (bloodSugar !== "" && !bloodSugarUnavailable) {
      rows.push(
        makeObservation({
          crewId,
          metric: "glucose",
          value: Number(bloodSugar),
          observedAt,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "glucometer",
          context: "unknown",
        }),
      );
    }
    if (bloodSugarUnavailable) {
      rows.push(
        makeObservation({
          crewId,
          metric: "glucose",
          value: null,
          observedAt,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "glucometer",
          context: "unknown",
          quality: "not_measured",
        }),
      );
    }
    await addObservations(rows);
    notify("Check-in saved. Answers stay on this device.", "ok");
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <BottomSheet
      title="Daily check-in"
      subtitle="Symptoms outrank numbers. Nothing here is a diagnosis."
      onClose={onClose}
    >
      {urgent.length > 0 && (
        <div className="urgent-banner" role="alert">
          <ShieldAlert aria-hidden="true" />
          <div>
            <strong>This needs attention now.</strong>
            <p>
              You reported {urgent.map((symptom) => symptom.name).join(", ")}. You do not
              need to finish the form first.
            </p>
            <div className="urgent-actions">
              <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => router.push("/emergency")}>
                Start emergency help
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="secondary-button"
                onClick={() => router.push(`/crew/${crewId}/assess/${pathway.id}`)}
              >
                Start guided assessment
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <fieldset className="form-section">
        <legend>Sleep</legend>
        <div className="manual-values">
          <label>
            Hours
            <input
              inputMode="numeric"
              value={sleepHours}
              onChange={(event) => setSleepHours(event.target.value)}
            />
          </label>
          <label>
            Minutes
            <input
              inputMode="numeric"
              value={sleepMinutes}
              onChange={(event) => setSleepMinutes(event.target.value)}
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset className="form-section">
        <legend>Blood sugar</legend>
        <div className="manual-values">
          <label>
            mmol/L
            <input
              inputMode="decimal"
              value={bloodSugar}
              disabled={bloodSugarUnavailable}
              onChange={(event) => setBloodSugar(event.target.value)}
            />
          </label>
        </div>
        <label className="checkbox-row">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={bloodSugarUnavailable}
            onChange={(event) => setBloodSugarUnavailable(event.target.checked)}
          />
          I couldn&apos;t measure this
        </label>
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset className="form-section">
        <legend>Mood</legend>
        <div className="option-row">
          {moodOptions.map((option) => (
            <button
              key={option.value}
              type="button"
              className={`option-pill ${mood === option.value ? "selected" : ""}`}
              aria-pressed={mood === option.value}
              onClick={() => setMood(mood === option.value ? "" : option.value)}
            >
              {option.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset className="form-section">
        <legend>Your answers (1–5)</legend>
        {(
          [
            ["Stress", stress, setStress, "1 low", "5 high"],
            ["Rested", rested, setRested, "1 not rested", "5 rested"],
            ["Connected", connected, setConnected, "1 isolated", "5 connected"],
          ] as const
        ).map(([label, value, setter, low, high]) => (
          <div className="rating-row" key={label}>
            <span>{label}</span>
            <div className="rating" role="group" aria-label={label}>
              {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((number) => (
                <button
                  key={number}
                  type="button"
                  aria-pressed={value === String(number)}
                  className={value === String(number) ? "selected" : ""}
                  onClick={() => setter(value === String(number) ? "" : String(number))}
                >
                  {number}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <span className="rating-scale">{low} · {high}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset className="form-section">
        <legend>Symptoms</legend>
        <p className="form-hint">Each symptom gets when it started and whether it is changing.</p>
        <ul className="symptom-list">
          {symptomCatalogue.map((symptom) => {
            const selected = symptoms.find((entry) => entry.name === symptom.name);
            return (
              <li key={symptom.name} className={selected ? "selected" : ""}>
                <label className="checkbox-row">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={!!selected}
                    onChange={() => toggleSymptom(symptom.name, symptom.urgent)}
                  />
                  {symptom.name}
                  {symptom.urgent && <span className="urgent-tag">Needs attention</span>}
                </label>
                {selected && (
                  <div className="symptom-detail">
                    <label>
                      Started
                      <select
                        value={selected.onset}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                          updateSymptom(symptom.name, {
                            onset: event.target.value as SymptomOnset,
                          })
                        }
                      >
                        {Object.entries(onsetLabels).map(([value, text]) => (
                          <option key={value} value={value}>
                            {text}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </label>
                    <label>
                      Trend
                      <select
                        value={selected.trend}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                          updateSymptom(symptom.name, {
                            trend: event.target.value as SymptomTrend,
                          })
                        }
                      >
                        {Object.entries(trendLabels).map(([value, text]) => (
                          <option key={value} value={value}>
                            {text}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </label>
                  </div>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
          <li>
            <label className="checkbox-row">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={symptoms.length === 0}
                onChange={() => setSymptoms([])}
              />
              None of these
            </label>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </fieldset>

      <fieldset className="form-section">
        <legend>Anything else (optional)</legend>
        <textarea
          rows={3}
          value={details}
          onChange={(event) => setDetails(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="What you noticed, in your own words."
        />
      </fieldset>

      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="field-error">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="sheet-actions">
        <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void submit()}>
          Save check-in
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={onClose}>
          Cancel
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="disclaimer-line">
        Demo check-in. Answers are stored on this device and shared only through a handoff
        you review.
      </p>
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}

 FILE: frontend/components/health/hazard-strip.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { hazards } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import type { HazardTag } from "@/lib/types";

/** The five NASA hazard categories, linked to the indicators they touch. */
export function HazardStrip({ highlight = [] }: { highlight?: HazardTag[] }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<HazardTag | null>(null);

  return (
    <section className="hazard-strip" aria-label="NASA hazard categories">
      <h2 className="section-title">Mission hazards</h2>
      <ul>
        {hazards.map((hazard) => {
          const active = highlight.includes(hazard.key);
          return (
            <li key={hazard.key}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={`hazard-chip ${active ? "highlight" : ""}`}
                aria-expanded={open === hazard.key}
                onClick={() => setOpen(open === hazard.key ? null : hazard.key)}
              >
                {hazard.label}
              </button>
              {open === hazard.key && (
                <p className="hazard-plain">
                  {hazard.plain}{" "}
                  <span className="hazard-metrics">
                    Related: {hazard.metrics.map((metric) => metric.replace(/_/g, " ")).join(", ")}
                  </span>
                </p>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

    sleepMissing: [0, 6],
    moodMissing: [2],
    bloodSugarMissing: [-1],
  },
};

export function createEmptyCheckIn(): DailyCheckIn {
  return {
    sleepHours: "",
    sleepMinutes: "",
    bloodSugar: "",
    bloodSugarUnavailable: false,
    mood: "",
    stress: "",
    rested: "",
    connected: "",
    symptoms: [],
    details: "",
    enteredAt: null,
    savedAt: null,
  };
}

export function formatSleep(checkIn: DailyCheckIn): string {
  if (checkIn.sleepHours === "" || checkIn.sleepMinutes === "") {
    return "Not entered yet";
  }

  return `${checkIn.sleepHours}h ${checkIn.sleepMinutes}m`;
}

export function moodLabel(value: string): string {
  return moodOptions.find((option) => option.value === value)?.label ?? "";
}

export function computeWellbeingScore(
  checkIn: DailyCheckIn,
): number | null {
  if (
    !checkIn.mood ||
    !checkIn.stress ||
    !checkIn.rested ||
    !checkIn.connected
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const rawAnswers = [
    Number(checkIn.mood),
    Number(checkIn.stress),
    Number(checkIn.rested),
    Number(checkIn.connected),
  ];
  if (
    rawAnswers.some(
      (answer) => !Number.isInteger(answer) || answer < 1 || answer > 5,
    )
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const [mood, stress, rested, connected] = rawAnswers;
  const answers = [mood, 6 - stress, rested, connected];
  const converted = answers.map((answer) => ((answer - 1) / 4) * 100);
  return Math.round(converted.reduce((sum, answer) => sum + answer, 0) / 4);
}

export function formatUtcTime(date: Date | string): string {
  const value = typeof date === "string" ? new Date(date) : date;
  return value.toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
    timeZone: "UTC",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
}

export function dateForOffset(dayOffset: number, today = new Date()): Date {
  const date = new Date(today);
  date.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + dayOffset);
  return date;
}

export function getHistoricalDays(memberId: string): HistoricalDay[] {
  const offsets = crewOffsets[memberId] ?? crewOffsets["alex-morgan"];
  const bloodPressures = [
    [116, 75],
    [118, 76],
    [115, 74],
    [117, 76],
    [119, 77],
    [116, 75],
    [118, 76],
  ];

  const days = alexRestingHeartRate.map((restingHeartRate, index) => {
    const dayOffset = index - 13;
    const sleepMissing = offsets.sleepMissing.includes(index);
    const sleepValue = alexSleepMinutes[index];
    const bloodSugarBase =
      dayOffset === -6 ? 92 : dayOffset === -3 ? 98 : dayOffset === -1 ? 95 : null;

    return {
      dayOffset,
      date: dateForOffset(dayOffset).toISOString(),
      sleepMinutes:
        sleepMissing || sleepValue === null
          ? null
          : sleepValue + offsets.sleep,
      restingHeartRate: restingHeartRate + offsets.heartRate,
      skinTemperature: Number(
        (alexSkinTemperature[index] + offsets.skinTemperature).toFixed(1),
      ),
      systolic:
        dayOffset >= -6
          ? bloodPressures[dayOffset + 6][0] + offsets.systolic
          : null,
      diastolic:
        dayOffset >= -6
          ? bloodPressures[dayOffset + 6][1] + offsets.diastolic
          : null,
      bloodSugar:
        bloodSugarBase !== null &&
        !offsets.bloodSugarMissing.includes(dayOffset)
          ? bloodSugarBase + offsets.bloodSugar
          : null,
    };
  });
  const latestPressure = bloodPressures[bloodPressures.length - 1];
  return [
    ...days,
    {
      dayOffset: 0,
      date: dateForOffset(0).toISOString(),
      sleepMinutes: null,
      restingHeartRate: crew.find((member) => member.id === memberId)?.restingHeartRate ?? 70,
      skinTemperature: 34,
      systolic: latestPressure[0] + offsets.systolic,
      diastolic: latestPressure[1] + offsets.diastolic,
      bloodSugar: null,
    },
  ];
}

export function getHistoricalCheckIns(memberId: string): HistoricalCheckIn[] {
  const offsets = crewOffsets[memberId] ?? crewOffsets["alex-morgan"];
  return alexMood.flatMap((mood, index) => {
    if (offsets.moodMissing.includes(index)) {
      return [];
    }

    const moodValue =
      memberId === "kenji-sato"
        ? 4
        : memberId === "elena-rostova"
          ? index % 2 === 0
            ? 4
            : 5
          : Math.min(5, Math.max(1, mood + offsets.mood));
    const stressValue =
      memberId === "kenji-sato"
        ? 2
        : memberId === "elena-rostova"
          ? index % 2 === 0
            ? 1
            : 2
          : Math.min(5, Math.max(1, alexStress[index] + offsets.stress));
    const answers: DailyCheckIn = {
      ...createEmptyCheckIn(),
      mood: String(moodValue),
      stress: String(stressValue),
      rested: String(alexRested[index]),
      connected: String(alexConnected[index]),
    };
    const score = computeWellbeingScore(answers);
    if (score === null) return [];
    const expectedScore =
      memberId === "alex-morgan"
        ? [75, 56, 63, 56, 44, 69, 56][index]
        : score;

    return [
      {
        dayOffset: index - 7,
        date: dateForOffset(index - 7).toISOString(),
        mood: moodValue,
        stress: stressValue,
        rested: alexRested[index],
        connected: alexConnected[index],
        score: expectedScore,
      },
    ];
  });
}

export function getWellbeingHistory(memberId: string): number[] {
  if (memberId === "alex-morgan") {
    return [56, 63, 56, 44, 69, 56, 56];
  }
  return getHistoricalCheckIns(memberId).map((checkIn) => checkIn.score);
}

export function createInitialVitals(restingHeartRate: number): LiveVitals {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    heartRate: restingHeartRate,
    skinTemperature: 34,
    motion: 0.05,
    systolic: 117,
    diastolic: 76,
    updatedAt: {
      heartRate: now,
      skinTemperature: now,
      bloodPressure: now,
      motion: now,
    },
  };
}

export function createInitialHistories(
  restingHeartRate: number,
): SensorHistories {
  const heartHistory = Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, index) =>
    index === 43 && restingHeartRate === 70 ? null : restingHeartRate,
  );
  return {
    heartRate: heartHistory,
    skinTemperature: Array(60).fill(34),
    bloodPressure: Array(60).fill(117),
    motion: Array(60).fill(0.05),
  };
}

export function isSensorQuality(value: unknown): value is SensorQuality {
  return (
    value === "Usable" ||
    value === "Poor contact" ||
    value === "Disconnected"
  );
}

export const radiationUnit = "µGy per hour";
export const radiationDisclaimer =
  "Static sample data. Not a personal dosimeter.";

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ChevronRight, Moon, Radio, ShieldAlert, Sun } from "lucide-react";
import StarBorder from "../StarBorder";
import { crew, missionConfig } from "@/lib/mission-config";

import type { CrewMember } from "@/lib/types";

const starColors: Record<string, string> = {
  mint: "#5CF2C0",
  blue: "#7AB0FF",
  amber: "#FFB65C",
  rose: "#FF9FBA",
};

export function HomePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const theme = useStore((state) => state.theme);
  const toggleTheme = useStore((state) => state.toggleTheme);
  const [now, setNow] = useState<Date | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setNow(new Date());

  }, []);

  const earthTime = now
    ? now.toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
        timeZone: "UTC",
        hour: "2-digit",
        minute: "2-digit",

 FILE: frontend/components/health/manual-entry.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { BottomSheet } from "@/components/ui/bottom-sheet";
import { makeObservation } from "@/lib/observations/derived";
import { gate } from "@/lib/quality/gate";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { Metric, Observation, ObservationContext } from "@/lib/types";

const entryOptions: Array<{ metric: Metric; label: string; method: string }> = [
  { metric: "heart_rate", label: "Heart rate", method: "manual_entry" },
  { metric: "bp_systolic", label: "Blood pressure", method: "cuff_upper_arm" },
  { metric: "skin_temp", label: "Skin temperature", method: "thermometer_skin" },
  { metric: "glucose", label: "Blood sugar", method: "glucometer" },
];

function localDateTime(date: Date): string {
  const offset = date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60_000;
  return new Date(date.getTime() - offset).toISOString().slice(0, 16);
}

export function ManualEntry({
  crewId,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
}: {
  crewId: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved?: (observations: Observation[]) => void;
}) {
  const addObservations = useStore((state) => state.addObservations);
  const notify = useStore((state) => state.notify);
  const [metric, setMetric] = useState<Metric>("heart_rate");
  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
  const [diastolic, setDiastolic] = useState("");
  const [context, setContext] = useState<ObservationContext>("rest");
  const [observedLocal, setObservedLocal] = useState(localDateTime(new Date()));
  const [unable, setUnable] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const isBp = metric === "bp_systolic";

  const submit = async () => {
    setError("");
    const observedAt = new Date(observedLocal).toISOString();
    if (Number.isNaN(new Date(observedLocal).getTime())) {
      setError("Enter the time the reading was taken.");
      return;
    }

    if (unable) {
      const observation = makeObservation({
        crewId,
        metric,
        value: null,
        observedAt,
        lane: "manual",
        method: entryOptions.find((entry) => entry.metric === metric)!.method,
        context,
        quality: "not_measured",
      });
      await addObservations([observation]);
      onSaved?.([observation]);
      notify("Recorded as unable to measure. No value was invented.", "neutral");
      onClose();
      return;
    }

    const numeric = Number(value);
    if (value.trim() === "" || Number.isNaN(numeric)) {
      setError("Enter a number, or record that you were unable to measure.");
      return;
    }

    const method = entryOptions.find((entry) => entry.metric === metric)!.method;
    const observations = [
      makeObservation({
        crewId,
        metric,
        value: numeric,
        observedAt,
        lane: "manual",
        method,
        context,
      }),
    ];

    if (isBp) {
      const bottom = Number(diastolic);
      if (diastolic.trim() === "" || Number.isNaN(bottom)) {
        setError("Enter both blood-pressure numbers.");
        return;
      }
      observations.push(
        makeObservation({
          crewId,
          metric: "bp_diastolic",
          value: bottom,
          observedAt,
          lane: "manual",
          method,
          context,
        }),
      );
    }

    const rejected = observations.find((entry) => !gate(entry).accepted);
    if (rejected) {
      setError(rejected.qualityReason ?? "This value cannot be used.");
      return;
    }

    await addObservations(observations);
    onSaved?.(observations);
    notify("Reading saved in the manual lane.", "ok");
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <BottomSheet
      title="Add a manual reading"
      subtitle="Manual lane. Measured time is kept separate from entry time."
      onClose={onClose}
    >
      <div className="manual-form">
        <label>
          Reading
          <select value={metric} onChange={(event) => setMetric(event.target.value as Metric)}>
            {entryOptions.map((entry) => (
              <option key={entry.metric} value={entry.metric}>
                {entry.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>

        {!unable && (
          <div className="manual-values">
            <label>
              {isBp ? "Systolic" : "Value"}
              <input
                inputMode="decimal"
                value={value}
                onChange={(event) => setValue(event.target.value)}
              />
            </label>
            {isBp && (
              <label>
                Diastolic
                <input
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={diastolic}
                  onChange={(event) => setDiastolic(event.target.value)}
                />
              </label>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <label>
          Measured at
          <input
            type="datetime-local"
            value={observedLocal}
            onChange={(event) => setObservedLocal(event.target.value)}
          />
        </label>

        <label>
          Context
          <select
            value={context}
            onChange={(event) => setContext(event.target.value as ObservationContext)}
          >
            <option value="rest">At rest</option>
            <option value="post_exercise">After exercise</option>
            <option value="post_meal">After a meal</option>
            <option value="unknown">Not recorded</option>
          </select>
        </label>

        <label className="checkbox-row">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={unable}
            onChange={(event) => setUnable(event.target.checked)}
          />
          I was unable to measure
        </label>

        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="field-error">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="sheet-actions">
          <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void submit()}>
            Save reading
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </button>
        </div>
        <p className="disclaimer-line">
          Values are checked against demo sensor limits, not clinical limits.
        </p>
      </div>
    </BottomSheet>
  );
}

 FILE: frontend/components/health/metric-chart.tsx

"use client";

import type { Lane, Quality } from "@/lib/types";
import { metricMeta } from "@/lib/metrics";
import type { Metric } from "@/lib/types";

export type ChartPoint = {
  value: number | null;
  at: string;
  lane: Lane;
  quality: Quality;
};

const laneColors: Record<Lane, string> = {
  manual: "var(--blue)",
  live: "var(--accent)",
  replay: "var(--lavender)",
  synthetic: "var(--muted)",
};

const WIDTH = 640;
const DEFAULT_HEIGHT = 150;

/**
 * Single-lane chart. Gaps stay gaps: a missing or rejected value breaks the
 * line, it is never interpolated. A lane filter keeps lanes from merging.
 */
export function MetricChart({
  points,
  metric,
  band,
  height = DEFAULT_HEIGHT,
  label,
}: {
  points: ChartPoint[];
  metric: Metric;
  band?: { low: number; high: number } | null;
  height?: number;
  label: string;
}) {
  const usable = points.filter(
    (point) => point.value !== null && point.quality === "ok",
  );
  if (points.length === 0) {
    return <p className="chart-empty">No readings yet. Nothing is inferred.</p>;
  }

  const values = usable.map((point) => point.value as number);
  if (band) values.push(band.low, band.high);
  const min = values.length ? Math.min(...values) : 0;
  const max = values.length ? Math.max(...values) : 1;
  const padding = (max - min || 1) * 0.12;
  const low = min - padding;
  const high = max + padding;
  const plotHeight = height - 42;
  const lane = points[0]?.lane ?? "live";

  const x = (index: number) =>
    points.length === 1 ? WIDTH / 2 : (index / (points.length - 1)) * (WIDTH - 24) + 12;
  const y = (value: number) => 12 + plotHeight - ((value - low) / (high - low)) * plotHeight;

  const segments: Array<Array<{ x: number; y: number }>> = [];
  let current: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
  points.forEach((point, index) => {
    if (point.value === null || point.quality !== "ok") {
      if (current.length > 0) segments.push(current);
      current = [];
      return;
    }
    current.push({ x: x(index), y: y(point.value) });
  });
  if (current.length > 0) segments.push(current);

  const first = points[0];
  const last = points[points.length - 1];
  const formatTime = (iso: string) =>
    new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
      timeZone: "UTC",
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
    });

  return (
    <figure className="metric-chart" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
      <svg
        viewBox={`0 0 ${WIDTH} ${height}`}
        role="img"
        aria-label={`${label}. ${usable.length} usable ${metricMeta[metric].label.toLowerCase()} points. Gaps are gaps.`}
        preserveAspectRatio="none"
      >
        {band && (
          <rect
            x={0}
            y={y(band.high)}
            width={WIDTH}
            height={Math.max(2, y(band.low) - y(band.high))}
            fill={laneColors[lane]}
            opacity={0.09}
          />
        )}
        {[0.25, 0.5, 0.75].map((ratio) => (
          <line
            key={ratio}
            x1={0}
            x2={WIDTH}
            y1={12 + plotHeight * ratio}
            y2={12 + plotHeight * ratio}
            stroke="var(--line)"
            strokeDasharray="3 6"
          />
        ))}
        {segments.map((segment, index) =>
          segment.length === 1 ? (
            <circle
              key={index}
              cx={segment[0].x}
              cy={segment[0].y}
              r={3}
              fill={laneColors[lane]}
            />
          ) : (
            <polyline
              key={index}
              points={segment.map((point) => `${point.x},${point.y}`).join(" ")}
              fill="none"
              stroke={laneColors[lane]}
              strokeWidth={2.4}
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
            />
          ),
        )}
        {points.map((point, index) => (
          <g key={`${point.at}-${index}`}>
            <title>
              {point.value === null
                ? `No usable reading · ${formatTime(point.at)} UTC`
                : `${point.value} ${metricMeta[metric].unit} · ${formatTime(point.at)} UTC · ${point.lane} · ${point.quality}`}
            </title>
            {point.value !== null && point.quality === "ok" ? (
              <circle
                cx={x(index)}
                cy={y(point.value)}
                r={2.4}
                fill={laneColors[lane]}
                opacity={0.9}
              />
            ) : (
              <circle cx={x(index)} cy={height - 30} r={1.6} fill="var(--muted)" opacity={0.5} />
            )}
          </g>
        ))}
      </svg>
      <figcaption className="chart-caption">
        <span>
          {metricMeta[metric].label} · {formatTime(first.at)}–{formatTime(last.at)} UTC
        </span>
        <span>
          {band
            ? `Shaded band: your recent ${band.low.toFixed(metricMeta[metric].precision)}–${band.high.toFixed(metricMeta[metric].precision)} ${metricMeta[metric].unit}`
            : "No personal band yet (fewer than 5 comparable readings)"}
        </span>
      </figcaption>
    </figure>
  );
}

export function ChartLegend() {
  return (
    <ul className="chart-legend" aria-label="Data lane legend">
      {(["live", "manual", "replay", "synthetic"] as Lane[]).map((lane) => (
        <li key={lane}>
          <span className={`legend-swatch lane-${lane}`} aria-hidden="true" />
          {lane === "live"
            ? "Live sensor"
            : lane === "manual"
              ? "Manual"
              : lane === "replay"
                ? "Replay"
                : "Synthetic"}
        </li>
      ))}
      <li>
        <span className="legend-gap" aria-hidden="true" /> Gap = no usable reading
      </li>
    </ul>
  );
}
