"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { RefreshCw, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { CrewNav } from "@/components/health/crew-nav";
import { environmentFor, formatDelay } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { OutboxItem, OutboxState } from "@/lib/types";

const stateCopy: Record<OutboxState, string> = {
  queued: "Queued on board",
  sent: "Sent",
  delivered: "Delivered",
  acknowledged: "Acknowledged by the flight surgeon",
};

const stateOrder: OutboxState[] = ["queued", "sent", "delivered", "acknowledged"];

export function OutboxPage() {
  const outbox = useStore((state) => state.outbox);
  const link = useStore((state) => state.link);
  const environment = useStore((state) => state.environment);
  const advanceOutbox = useStore((state) => state.advanceOutbox);
  const cancelOutbox = useStore((state) => state.cancelOutbox);
  const notify = useStore((state) => state.notify);
  const hydrated = useStore((state) => state.hydrated);
  const serverConfigured = useStore((state) => state.serverConfigured);
  const serverReachable = useStore((state) => state.serverReachable);
  const reconcileOutbox = useStore((state) => state.reconcileOutbox);

  const crewIds = useMemo(
    () => [...new Set(outbox.map((item) => item.crewId))].join(","),
    [outbox],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!serverConfigured || !hydrated || !crewIds) return;
    for (const crewId of crewIds.split(",")) void reconcileOutbox(crewId);
  }, [crewIds, hydrated, reconcileOutbox, serverConfigured]);

  const preset = environmentFor(environment);
  const ordered = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...outbox].sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? 1 : -1)),
    [outbox],
  );

  return (
    <section className="reports-page outbox-page">
      <CrewNav />
      <header className="reports-head">
        <div>
          <p className="illustrative-label">HONEST DELIVERY STATES</p>
          <h1>Outbox</h1>
          <p className="assessment-sub">
            The state shown here is exactly the state stored on this device. Nothing is
            marked delivered or acknowledged unless that actually happened.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="reports-actions">
          <span className={`link-pill ${link}`}>{link}</span>
          {serverConfigured && (
            <span className={`server-pill ${serverReachable ? "up" : "down"}`}>
              Ground server {serverReachable ? "reachable" : "unreachable"}
            </span>
          )}
          <button
            type="button"
            className="secondary-button"
            onClick={() => void advanceOutbox()}
          >
            <RefreshCw aria-hidden="true" /> Check link
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <p className="outbox-note">
        {serverConfigured
          ? serverReachable
            ? "The ground server owns delivery states. Sent, delivered and acknowledged appear only when the server reports them."
            : "Ground server unreachable. Packets stay queued; the same-device BroadcastChannel fallback only syncs this browser."
          : link === "blackout"
            ? "Blackout: packets stay queued on board until the link returns."
            : link === "live"
              ? "Link is live. Queued packets move to Sent immediately."
              : `Delayed link: queued packets move to Sent after the configured one-way delay of ${formatDelay(preset.delaySeconds)}.`}
      </p>

      {ordered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty-state">
          <h2>Nothing queued</h2>
          <p>Handoffs and private support requests will appear here.</p>
          <Link className="primary-button" href="/reports">
            Prepare a handoff
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="outbox-list">
          {ordered.map((item) => (
            <OutboxRow
              key={item.id}
              item={item}
              onCancel={() => void cancelOutbox(item.id)}
              onAcknowledge={() => {
                void useStore.getState().acknowledge(item.id);
                notify("Acknowledged locally. This is a demo receipt.", "ok");
              }}
              allowLocalAck={!serverConfigured}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function OutboxRow({
  item,
  onCancel,
  onAcknowledge,
  allowLocalAck,
}: {
  item: OutboxItem;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onAcknowledge: () => void;
  allowLocalAck: boolean;
}) {
  const reached = stateOrder.indexOf(item.state);
  return (
    <li className="outbox-item">
      <header>
        <div>
          <h2>{item.title}</h2>
          <p>
            Created{" "}
            {new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
              timeZone: "UTC",
              day: "2-digit",
              month: "short",
              hour: "2-digit",
              minute: "2-digit",
            })}{" "}
            UTC · one-way delay {mathDelay(item.delaySeconds)}
          </p>
        </div>
        <span className={`outbox-state state-${item.state}`}>{stateCopy[item.state]}</span>
      </header>

      <ol className="state-timeline">
        {stateOrder.map((state, index) => (
          <li
            key={state}
            className={index <= reached ? "reached" : ""}
            aria-current={state === item.state ? "step" : undefined}
          >
            <span className="state-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
            {stateCopy[state]}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>

      <ul className="state-history">
        {item.history.map((entry) => (
          <li key={`${entry.state}-${entry.at}`}>
            <time dateTime={entry.at}>
              {new Date(entry.at).toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
                timeZone: "UTC",
                hour: "2-digit",
                minute: "2-digit",
              })}
            </time>{" "}
            — {entry.note}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>

      <div className="outbox-actions">
        {item.private && <span className="private-flag">Private</span>}
        {item.state === "queued" && (
          <button type="button" className="text-button" onClick={onCancel}>
            <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" /> Cancel before send
          </button>
        )}
        {item.state === "sent" && allowLocalAck && (
          <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={onAcknowledge}>
            Mark acknowledged (demo)
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </li>
  );
}

function mathDelay(seconds: number): string {
  return seconds >= 60 ? `${Math.round(seconds / 60)} min` : `${seconds} s`;
}

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/components/health/particles.tsx
 lines: 488
================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

/* ---------- Tweak these ---------- */
const CONFIG = {
  stars: { desktop: 130, phone: 70 },
  drift: { x: -0.94, y: -0.34 }, // stars drift toward the upper left
  driftSpeed: { far: 6, near: 14 }, // pixels per second (far stars slower)
  planets: { desktop: 3, phone: 2 },
  shootingStarEvery: [7, 15], // seconds between shooting stars
  cometEvery: [35, 60], // seconds between comets
  parallax: 12, // max pointer parallax in px
};

const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
const rand = (a: number, b: number) => a + Math.random() * (b - a);
const rgba = (hex: string, a: number) => {
  const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
  return `rgba(${(n >> 16) & 255},${(n >> 8) & 255},${n & 255},${a})`;
};

// Index-matched palettes (repeats make white stars more common).
const DARK_COLORS = [
  "#FFFFFF",
  "#FFFFFF",
  "#FFFFFF",
  "#FFFFFF",
  "#F4F7FF",
  "#F4F7FF",
  "#5CF2C0",
  "#7AB0FF",
  "#FFB65C",
];
const LIGHT_COLORS = [
  "#2F5BD3",
  "#067A58",
  "#4A5478",
  "#2F5BD3",
  "#067A58",
  "#4A5478",
  "#067A58",
  "#2F5BD3",
  "#C2540A",
];

const PLANET_TYPES = [
  { c1: "#E8896A", c2: "#7A2E1A", bands: false, ring: false }, // rust
  { c1: "#A9CBFF", c2: "#2A4A8C", bands: false, ring: true }, // ice, ringed
  { c1: "#7BF5D0", c2: "#0E6B55", bands: true, ring: false }, // mint gas
  { c1: "#FFC986", c2: "#8A4B14", bands: true, ring: false }, // amber gas
];

type Star = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
  r: number;
  color: number;
  base: number;
  tw: number;
  phase: number;
  spikes: boolean;
};
type Planet = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
  r: number;
  kind: number;
  moon: boolean;
  moonAngle: number;
};
type Streak = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  age: number;
  life: number;
  len: number;
  comet: boolean;
};

const sprites = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
function glowSprite(color: string) {
  let c = sprites.get(color);
  if (c) return c;
  c = document.createElement("canvas");
  c.width = c.height = 64;
  const g = c.getContext("2d")!;
  const grad = g.createRadialGradient(32, 32, 0, 32, 32, 32);
  grad.addColorStop(0, rgba("#FFFFFF", 1));
  grad.addColorStop(0.12, rgba(color, 0.95));
  grad.addColorStop(0.35, rgba(color, 0.28));
  grad.addColorStop(1, rgba(color, 0));
  g.fillStyle = grad;
  g.fillRect(0, 0, 64, 64);
  sprites.set(color, c);
  return c;
}

function readDark() {
  const el = document.documentElement;
  if (el.classList.contains("dark")) return true;
  if (el.classList.contains("light")) return false;
  const dt = el.getAttribute("data-theme");
  if (dt) return dt === "dark";
  return window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
}

export function Particles({ enabled = true }: { enabled?: boolean }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const canvas = ref.current!;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d")!;
    const reduced = window.matchMedia(
      "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",
    ).matches;

    let w = 0,
      h = 0,
      raf = 0,
      last = performance.now(),
      time = 0;
    let dark = readDark();
    let stars: Star[] = [];
    let planets: Planet[] = [];
    let streaks: Streak[] = [];
    let nextStar = 3; // first shooting star soon, so it is seen
    let nextComet = 14;
    const mouse = { tx: 0, ty: 0, x: 0, y: 0 };

    const seed = () => {
      const phone = w < 600;
      const n = phone ? CONFIG.stars.phone : CONFIG.stars.desktop;
      stars = Array.from({ length: n }, () => {
        const roll = Math.random();
        const z = roll < 0.55 ? 0.35 : roll < 0.85 ? 0.65 : 1;
        return {
          x: rand(0, w),
          y: rand(0, h),
          z,
          r: rand(0.5, 1) * (0.8 + z * 1.2),
          color: Math.floor(rand(0, DARK_COLORS.length)),
          base: rand(0.45, 0.95) * (0.6 + 0.4 * z),
          tw: rand(0.8, 2.6),
          phase: rand(0, TAU),
          spikes: z === 1 && Math.random() < 0.45,
        };
      });
      const pn = phone ? CONFIG.planets.phone : CONFIG.planets.desktop;
      const offset = Math.floor(rand(0, PLANET_TYPES.length));
      planets = Array.from({ length: pn }, (_, i) => ({
        x: rand(0, w),
        y: rand(0, h),
        z: rand(0.4, 0.9),
        r: rand(10, 26),
        kind: (offset + i) % PLANET_TYPES.length,
        moon: i === 0,
        moonAngle: rand(0, TAU),
      }));
    };

    const resize = () => {
      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      w = window.innerWidth;
      h = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.width = w * dpr;
      canvas.height = h * dpr;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      seed();
      if (reduced) draw();
    };

    const spawn = (comet: boolean) => {
      const angle = rand(2.55, 2.85); // moves left and down
      const speed = comet ? rand(140, 200) : rand(750, 1000);
      streaks.push({
        x: rand(comet ? 0.55 : 0.35, comet ? 1 : 1.05) * w,
        y: rand(-0.05, comet ? 0.35 : 0.4) * h,
        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
        age: 0,
        life: comet ? rand(7, 10) : rand(0.9, 1.3),
        len: comet ? rand(260, 340) : rand(130, 220),
        comet,
      });
    };

    const update = (dt: number) => {
      const { x: dx, y: dy } = CONFIG.drift;
      for (const s of stars) {
        const t = (s.z - 0.35) / 0.65;
        const sp =
          CONFIG.driftSpeed.far +
          (CONFIG.driftSpeed.near - CONFIG.driftSpeed.far) * t;
        s.x += dx * sp * dt;
        s.y += dy * sp * dt;
        if (s.x < -10) {
          s.x = w + 10;
          s.y = rand(0, h);
        }
        if (s.y < -10) {
          s.y = h + 10;
          s.x = rand(0, w);
        }
      }
      for (const p of planets) {
        const sp = 2.5 + 4 * p.z;
        p.x += dx * sp * dt;
        p.y += dy * sp * dt;
        const m = p.r * 3;
        if (p.x < -m) {
          p.x = w + m;
          p.y = rand(0, h);
        }
        if (p.y < -m) {
          p.y = h + m;
          p.x = rand(0, w);
        }
      }
      nextStar -= dt;
      nextComet -= dt;
      if (nextStar <= 0) {
        spawn(false);
        if (Math.random() < 0.2) spawn(false);
        nextStar = rand(
          CONFIG.shootingStarEvery[0],
          CONFIG.shootingStarEvery[1],
        );
      }
      if (nextComet <= 0) {
        spawn(true);
        nextComet = rand(CONFIG.cometEvery[0], CONFIG.cometEvery[1]);
      }
      streaks = streaks.filter((s) => {
        s.age += dt;
        s.x += s.vx * dt;
        s.y += s.vy * dt;
        return s.age < s.life && s.x > -400 && s.y < h + 400;
      });
      mouse.x += (mouse.tx - mouse.x) * 0.05;
      mouse.y += (mouse.ty - mouse.y) * 0.05;
    };

    const drawStar = (s: Star) => {
      const col = (dark ? DARK_COLORS : LIGHT_COLORS)[s.color];
      const tw = reduced ? 0.7 : 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(time * s.tw + s.phase);
      const a = dark
        ? s.base * (0.4 + 0.6 * tw)
        : s.base * 0.55 * (0.5 + 0.5 * tw);
      const x = s.x + mouse.x * CONFIG.parallax * s.z;
      const y = s.y + mouse.y * CONFIG.parallax * s.z;
      const size = s.r * (dark ? 7 : 5) * (0.85 + 0.3 * tw);
      ctx.globalAlpha = a;
      ctx.drawImage(glowSprite(col), x - size / 2, y - size / 2, size, size);
      if (s.spikes) {
        const len = s.r * 5 * (0.7 + 0.3 * tw);
        ctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.7;
        ctx.strokeStyle = col;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x - len, y);
        ctx.lineTo(x + len, y);
        ctx.moveTo(x, y - len);
        ctx.lineTo(x, y + len);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    };

    const drawPlanet = (p: Planet) => {
      const k = PLANET_TYPES[p.kind];
      const r = p.r;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(
        p.x + mouse.x * CONFIG.parallax * p.z,
        p.y + mouse.y * CONFIG.parallax * p.z,
      );
      ctx.globalAlpha = dark ? 0.92 : 0.5;
      const halo = ctx.createRadialGradient(0, 0, r * 0.9, 0, 0, r * 1.7);
      halo.addColorStop(0, rgba(k.c1, dark ? 0.28 : 0.15));
      halo.addColorStop(1, rgba(k.c1, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = halo;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 1.7, 0, TAU);
      ctx.fill();
      const ring = (from: number, to: number) => {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.rotate(-0.35);
        ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(k.c1, 0.55);
        ctx.lineWidth = r * 0.16;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, r * 1.9, r * 0.55, 0, from, to);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
      };
      if (k.ring) ring(Math.PI, TAU); // back half of the ring
      const body = ctx.createRadialGradient(
        -r * 0.4,
        -r * 0.4,
        r * 0.1,
        0,
        0,
        r * 1.05,
      );
      body.addColorStop(0, k.c1);
      body.addColorStop(1, k.c2);
      ctx.fillStyle = body;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, TAU);
      ctx.fill();
      if (k.bands) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, TAU);
        ctx.clip();
        ctx.rotate(-0.25);
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba(k.c2, 0.22);
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++)
          ctx.fillRect(-r, -r + (i * 2 + 0.6) * (r / 5), 2 * r, r / 9);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      const shade = ctx.createRadialGradient(
        -r * 0.45,
        -r * 0.45,
        r * 0.55,
        -r * 0.2,
        -r * 0.2,
        r * 1.35,
      );
      shade.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
      shade.addColorStop(1, dark ? "rgba(3,6,18,0.7)" : "rgba(18,26,51,0.3)");
      ctx.fillStyle = shade;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, TAU);
      ctx.fill();
      if (k.ring) ring(0, Math.PI); // front half of the ring
      if (p.moon) {
        const a = p.moonAngle + time * 0.25;
        ctx.fillStyle = dark ? rgba("#DCE6FF", 0.9) : rgba("#4A5478", 0.7);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(Math.cos(a) * r * 1.9, Math.sin(a) * r * 0.6, r * 0.2, 0, TAU);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    };

    const drawStreak = (s: Streak) => {
      const sp = Math.hypot(s.vx, s.vy);
      const dirAngle = Math.atan2(s.vy / sp, s.vx / sp);
      const env = Math.min(1, s.age / 0.2, (s.life - s.age) / 0.4);
      if (env <= 0) return;
      const tails = s.comet
        ? [
            {
              len: s.len,
              w: 3,
              c: dark ? "#7AB0FF" : "#2F5BD3",
              off: 0,
              a: 0.9,
            },
            {
              len: s.len * 0.6,
              w: 7,
              c: dark ? "#FFB65C" : "#C2540A",
              off: 0.12,
              a: 0.5,
            },
          ]
        : [
            {
              len: s.len,
              w: 2,
              c: dark ? "#FFFFFF" : "#2F5BD3",
              off: 0,
              a: 0.95,
            },
          ];
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.lineCap = "round";
      for (const t of tails) {
        const ang = dirAngle + t.off;
        const tx = s.x - Math.cos(ang) * t.len,
          ty = s.y - Math.sin(ang) * t.len;
        const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(s.x, s.y, tx, ty);
        g.addColorStop(0, rgba(t.c, t.a * env));
        g.addColorStop(1, rgba(t.c, 0));
        ctx.strokeStyle = g;
        ctx.lineWidth = t.w;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(tx, ty);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      const size = s.comet ? 30 : 16;
      ctx.globalAlpha = env;
      ctx.drawImage(
        glowSprite(dark ? "#FFFFFF" : "#2F5BD3"),
        s.x - size / 2,
        s.y - size / 2,
        size,
        size,
      );
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    };

    function draw() {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      for (const s of stars) drawStar(s);
      for (const p of planets) drawPlanet(p);
      for (const s of streaks) drawStreak(s);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }

    const frame = (now: number) => {
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      const dt = Math.min((now - last) / 1000, 0.05);
      last = now;
      time += dt;
      update(dt);
      draw();
    };

    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.hidden) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      else if (!reduced) {
        last = performance.now();
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      }
    };
    const onPointer = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      mouse.tx = (e.clientX / w) * 2 - 1;
      mouse.ty = (e.clientY / h) * 2 - 1;
    };

    // Re-read the theme whenever the page switches between light and dark.
    const mo = new MutationObserver(() => {
      dark = readDark();
      if (reduced) draw();
    });
    mo.observe(document.documentElement, {
      attributes: true,
      attributeFilter: ["class", "data-theme"],
    });

    let resizeTimer: number;
    const onResize = () => {
      window.clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
      resizeTimer = window.setTimeout(resize, 150);
    };

    resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", onResize);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    if (!reduced) {
      window.addEventListener("pointermove", onPointer, { passive: true });
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
      mo.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", onPointer);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    };
  }, [enabled]);

  if (!enabled) return null;
  return (
    <canvas
      ref={ref}
      aria-hidden="true"
      style={{
        position: "fixed",
        inset: 0,
        width: "100%",
        height: "100%",
        pointerEvents: "none",
        zIndex: 0,
      }}
    />
  );
}

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/components/health/pathway-runner.tsx
 lines: 435

"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Clock, CornerUpLeft, ShieldAlert, Volume2 } from "lucide-react";
import { ManualEntry } from "@/components/health/manual-entry";
import {
  advance,
  equipmentMissing,
  optionsFor,
  pathwayStep,
  reassessRemainingMs,
  startPathway,
  type RunnerState,
} from "@/lib/pathways/runner";
import { pathwayById } from "@/lib/pathways";
import { buildEncounter } from "@/lib/encounters/build";
import { missionConfig } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { CheckIn, Encounter } from "@/lib/types";

function elapsed(startedAt: string, now: number): string {
  const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - new Date(startedAt).getTime()) / 1000));
  return `${String(Math.floor(seconds / 60)).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function speak(text: string) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || !("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text));
}

export function PathwayRunner({
  crewId,
  pathwayId,
  encounterId,
  drill = false,
  symptoms = [],
}: {
  crewId: string;
  pathwayId: string;
  encounterId?: string | null;
  drill?: boolean;
  symptoms?: CheckIn["symptoms"];
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathway = pathwayById(pathwayId);
  const saveEncounter = useStore((state) => state.saveEncounter);
  const notify = useStore((state) => state.notify);
  const [state, setState] = useState<RunnerState | null>(() =>
    pathway ? startPathway(pathway) : null,
  );
  const [manualOpen, setManualOpen] = useState(false);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());
  const [savedEncounter, setSavedEncounter] = useState<Encounter | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state || !pathway) return;
    if (state.mode !== "done" && state.mode !== "emergency") return;
    if (savedEncounter) return;
    const encounter = buildEncounter(state, crewId, symptoms);
    setSavedEncounter(encounter);
    void saveEncounter(encounter);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [state?.mode]);

  const step = useMemo(
    () => (pathway && state ? pathwayStep(pathway, state.stepId) : null),
    [pathway, state],
  );

  if (!pathway || !state) {
    return (
      <section className="assessment">
        <h1>Pathway not found</h1>
        <p>This pathway is missing. Nothing was inferred.</p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const missing =
    step?.type === "measure" && equipmentMissing(step, missionConfig.equipment);
  const between = state.mode === "reassess" ? reassessRemainingMs(state, new Date(now)) : 0;

  const dispatch = (event: Parameters<typeof advance>[2]) =>
    setState((current) => (current ? advance(current, pathway, event) : current));

  if (state.mode === "done") {
    return (
      <section className="assessment">
        <header className="assessment-head">
          <p className="illustrative-label">{pathway.label}</p>
          <h1>{pathway.title}</h1>
          <p className="assessment-sub">Assessment complete. Nothing here is a diagnosis.</p>
        </header>
        <div className="assessment-summary">
          <h2>What was recorded</h2>
          <ul>
            {state.answers.map((answer, index) => (
              <li key={`${answer.stepId}-${index}`}>
                <span>{answer.question}</span>
                <strong>{answer.answer}</strong>
              </li>
            ))}
            {state.unknowns.map((unknown) => (
              <li key={unknown} className="unknown-row">
                <span>Unknown</span>
                <strong>{unknown}</strong>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <h2>Actions</h2>
          <ul>
            {state.actions.map((action) => (
              <li key={action.at}>
                <span>{action.text}</span>
                <strong>
                  {new Date(action.at).toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
                    timeZone: "UTC",
                    hour: "2-digit",
                    minute: "2-digit",
                  })}{" "}
                  UTC
                </strong>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <p className="disclaimer-line">
            {state.incomplete
              ? "This assessment is incomplete: something could not be measured or assessed."
              : "All steps were answered."}{" "}
            Outcomes are prompts for the crew, not diagnoses.
          </p>
          {drill && (
            <div className="drill-result">
              <h2>Drill result</h2>
              <p>
                {state.answers.length} answers, {state.unknowns.length} unknowns, {
                  elapsed(state.startedAt, now)
                }{" "}
                elapsed. This scores completeness and speed only — never health.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="sheet-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="primary-button"
              onClick={() =>
                router.push(
                  `/reports?encounter=${encodeURIComponent(savedEncounter?.id ?? "")}${
                    drill ? "&drill=1" : ""
                  }`,
                )
              }
            >
              Generate handoff
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="secondary-button"
              onClick={() => router.push(`/crew/${crewId}`)}
            >
              Back to readings
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (state.mode === "emergency") {
    return (
      <section className="assessment emergency">
        <header className="assessment-head">
          <p className="illustrative-label">{pathway.label}</p>
          <h1>Escalate now</h1>
          <p className="assessment-sub">
            An answer on this step says: get the crew medical officer. This is a prompt, not
            a diagnosis.
          </p>
        </header>
        <div className="urgent-banner" role="alert">
          <ShieldAlert aria-hidden="true" />
          <div>
            <strong>Alert the crew and contact the medical officer.</strong>
            <p>Keep the person company. Record what you see and when it started.</p>
            <div className="urgent-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="primary-button"
                onClick={() => notify("Crew alert sent on the local channel.", "attention")}
              >
                Alert the crew
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="secondary-button"
                onClick={() => router.push("/reports")}
              >
                Prepare handoff
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="secondary-button"
          onClick={() => router.push(`/crew/${crewId}`)}
        >
          <CornerUpLeft aria-hidden="true" /> Back
        </button>
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section className="assessment">
      <header className="assessment-head">
        <p className="illustrative-label">{pathway.label}</p>
        <h1>{pathway.title}</h1>
        <div className="assessment-meta">
          <span>
            <Clock aria-hidden="true" /> {elapsed(state.startedAt, now)} elapsed
          </span>
          {currentStepIndex(state, pathway) !== null && (
            <span>
              Step {(currentStepIndex(state, pathway) ?? 0) + 1} of {pathway.steps.length}
            </span>
          )}
          {encounterId && <span>Encounter {encounterId.slice(0, 8)}</span>}
        </div>
      </header>

      {state.mode === "reassess" && pathway.reassess ? (
        <div className="step-card">
          <p className="reassess-timer" role="timer" aria-live="off">
            {between > 0
              ? `Reassess in ${Math.ceil(between / 60000)} min`
              : "Reassessment is due now"}
          </p>
          <h2>{pathway.reassess.question}</h2>
          <div className="step-options">
            {pathway.reassess.options.map((option) => (
              <button
                key={option}
                type="button"
                className="step-option"
                onClick={() => dispatch({ type: "reassess", option, at: new Date().toISOString() })}
              >
                {option}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : step ? (
        <div className="step-card">
          <div className="step-title-row">
            <h2>{step.text}</h2>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="icon-button"
              aria-label="Read this step aloud"
              onClick={() => speak(step.text)}
            >
              <Volume2 aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {step.type === "question" || step.type === "urgent_check" ? (
            <div className="step-options">
              {optionsFor(step).map((option) => (
                <button
                  key={option}
                  type="button"
                  className={`step-option ${step.type === "urgent_check" && option === "Yes" ? "danger" : ""}`}
                  onClick={() =>
                    dispatch({ type: "answer", option, at: new Date().toISOString() })
                  }
                >
                  {option}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {step.type === "measure" && (
            <div className="step-options">
              {missing ? (
                <>
                  <p className="field-error">
                    Equipment unavailable: {step.needs?.replace(/_/g, " ")}. This assessment
                    will be marked incomplete.
                  </p>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="step-option"
                    onClick={() =>
                      dispatch({ type: "equipment_unavailable", at: new Date().toISOString() })
                    }
                  >
                    Continue without it
                  </button>
                  {step.alt && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="step-option"
                      onClick={() =>
                        dispatch({ type: "equipment_unavailable", at: new Date().toISOString() })
                      }
                    >
                      Go to alternative step
                    </button>
                  )}
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="step-option"
                    onClick={() => setManualOpen(true)}
                  >
                    Enter a reading
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="step-option muted"
                    onClick={() =>
                      dispatch({
                        type: "measurement",
                        observation: null,
                        at: new Date().toISOString(),
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    Unable to measure
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {step.type === "note" && (
            <div className="step-options">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="step-option"
                onClick={() =>
                  dispatch({
                    type: "measurement",
                    observation: null,
                    at: new Date().toISOString(),
                  })
                }
              >
                Continue
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {step.type === "action" && (
            <div className="step-options">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="step-option"
                onClick={() =>
                  dispatch({ type: "action_complete", at: new Date().toISOString() })
                }
              >
                Done — start {step.timer_minutes ?? 0} min timer
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <section className="action-log" aria-label="Action log">
        <h2>Action log</h2>
        <p className="form-hint">Tap an earlier answer to change it. Nothing is hidden.</p>
        <ol>
          {state.answers.map((answer, index) => (
            <li key={`${answer.stepId}-${index}`}>
              <span>{answer.question}</span>
              <strong>{answer.answer}</strong>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="text-button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setState({
                    ...state,
                    mode: "step",
                    stepId: answer.stepId,
                    answers: state.answers.slice(0, index),
                  })
                }
              >
                Change
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </section>

      {manualOpen && (
        <ManualEntry
          crewId={crewId}
          onClose={() => setManualOpen(false)}
          onSaved={(observations) => {
            const first = observations[0];
            if (!first) return;
            dispatch({
              type: "measurement",
              observation: first,
              at: new Date().toISOString(),
            });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function currentStepIndex(
  state: RunnerState,
  pathway: { steps: Array<{ id: string }> },
): number | null {
  if (!state.stepId) return null;
  const index = pathway.steps.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === state.stepId);
  return index >= 0 ? index : null;
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Info, ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";
import {
  areaMetrics,
  dailyTrend,
  latest,
  statusFor,
} from "@/lib/observations/derived";
import { formatValue, metricMeta } from "@/lib/metrics";
import { readingFor } from "@/lib/readings";
import { MetricChart, ChartLegend, type ChartPoint } from "@/components/health/metric-chart";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/health/status-chip";
import { TrustRing, formatClock } from "@/components/health/trust-ring";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { BodyArea, Metric, Observation } from "@/lib/types";

export function ReadingDetails({
  crewId,
  area,
  observations,
  liveToday,
  sensorUnplugged,
  onOpenGuide,
}: {
  crewId: string;
  area: BodyArea;
  observations: Observation[];
  liveToday: Partial<Record<Metric, ChartPoint[]>>;
  sensorUnplugged: boolean;
  onOpenGuide: (key: string) => void;
}) {
  const privateUnlocked = useStore((state) => state.privateUnlocked);
  const metrics = areaMetrics[area] ?? [];
  const [headTab, setHeadTab] = useState<"mood" | "sleep_minutes">("mood");

  const shown =
    area === "Head" ? [headTab] : metrics.length > 0 ? metrics : [];

  return (
    <section className="body-card reading-details" aria-labelledby="details-title">
      <header className="details-head">
        <div>
          <span className="eyebrow-line" />
          <h2 id="details-title">{area}</h2>
        </div>
        {area === "Head" && (
          <div className="head-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Head readings">
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={headTab === "mood"}
              onClick={() => setHeadTab("mood")}
            >
              Mood
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={headTab === "sleep_minutes"}
              onClick={() => setHeadTab("sleep_minutes")}
            >
              Sleep
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      {area === "Radiation" ? (
        <div className="details-static">
          <p className="details-value">
            2.4 <small>mSv cumulative</small>
          </p>
          <p>
            Passive dosimeters are read on a schedule. This is not a live measurement and
            cannot be compared with other readings.
          </p>
          <button type="button" className="text-button" onClick={() => onOpenGuide("radiation")}>
            <Info aria-hidden="true" /> Measurement guide
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        shown.map((metric) => (
          <MetricDetail
            key={metric}
            metric={metric}
            crewId={crewId}
            observations={observations}
            liveToday={liveToday}
            sensorUnplugged={sensorUnplugged}
            locked={
              !!readingFor(metric)?.private && !privateUnlocked
            }
            onOpenGuide={onOpenGuide}
          />
        ))
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function MetricDetail({
  metric,
  crewId,
  observations,
  liveToday,
  locked,
  sensorUnplugged,
  onOpenGuide,
}: {
  metric: Metric;
  crewId: string;
  observations: Observation[];
  liveToday: Partial<Record<Metric, ChartPoint[]>>;
  locked: boolean;
  sensorUnplugged: boolean;
  onOpenGuide: (key: string) => void;
}) {
  const meta = metricMeta[metric];
  const rawNewest = latest(
    observations.filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId),
    metric,
  );
  // A sensor that has stopped reporting cannot leave a reassuring status on
  // screen: the value becomes "No valid reading" with a plain reason.
  const liveMetric = ["heart_rate", "skin_temp", "bp_systolic", "bp_diastolic", "motion"].includes(metric);
  const unplugged = sensorUnplugged && liveMetric;
  const newest = unplugged && rawNewest ? { ...rawNewest, quality: "no_contact" as const, qualityReason: "The sensor is not reporting. This is not a current measurement." } : rawNewest;
  const comparison = unplugged
    ? {
        state: "no_valid_reading" as const,
        label: "No valid reading",
        detail: "The sensor is not reporting. Check the contact and try again.",
        comparableCount: 0,
        range: null,
      }
    : statusFor(observations, crewId, metric);
  const reading = readingFor(metric);
  const livePoints = liveToday[metric] ?? [];
  const trendPoints = dailyTrend(
    observations.filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId),
    metric,
  );
  const [laneFilter, setLaneFilter] = useState<Observation["lane"] | "all">("all");
  const lanes = new Set(trendPoints.map((point) => point.lane));
  // Lanes are never merged: "all" still shows one lane at a time, the newest.
  const activeLane = laneFilter === "all" ? trendPoints[trendPoints.length - 1]?.lane : laneFilter;
  const filteredTrend = trendPoints.filter((point) => point.lane === activeLane);

  if (locked) {
    return (
      <div className="metric-detail locked">
        <h3>{meta.label}</h3>
        <p>Private answer. Unlock it in the wellbeing tile to see the value and trend.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="metric-detail">
      <div className="metric-head">
        <div>
          <h3>{meta.label}</h3>
          <p className="metric-sub">{reading?.whatItIs}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="metric-head-side">
          <span className="details-value">
            {newest ? formatValue(metric, newest.value) : "—"}
          </span>
          <TrustRing observation={newest} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="metric-row">
        <StatusChip comparison={comparison} />
        {newest && (
          <span className="metric-method">
            {newest.lane} · measured {formatClock(newest.observedAt)} UTC
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="metric-charts">
        <div>
          <h4>Today (live)</h4>
          <MetricChart
            points={livePoints.length > 0 ? livePoints : trendPoints.slice(-1)}
            metric={metric}
            band={comparison.range}
            label={`${meta.label} today`}
            height={130}
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="chart-head">
            <h4>14-day trend (daily)</h4>
            <div className="lane-filter" role="group" aria-label="Data lane">
              {(["all", "live", "manual", "replay", "synthetic"] as const)
                .filter((lane) => lane === "all" || lanes.has(lane))
                .map((lane) => (
                  <button
                    key={lane}
                    type="button"
                    className={laneFilter === lane ? "active" : ""}
                    aria-pressed={laneFilter === lane}
                    onClick={() => setLaneFilter(lane)}
                  >
                    {lane === "all" ? "Newest lane" : lane}
                  </button>
                ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <MetricChart
            points={filteredTrend}
            metric={metric}
            band={comparison.range}
            label={`${meta.label} 14 day trend`}
            height={130}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <ChartLegend />

      <div className="metric-foot">
        <p>
          <strong>Tell the crew when.</strong> {reading?.tellCrewWhen}
        </p>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="text-button"
          onClick={() => onOpenGuide(reading?.key ?? "")}
        >
          <Info aria-hidden="true" /> Measurement guide
        </button>
      </div>

      {comparison.state === "attention" && (
        <p className="metric-attention">
          <ShieldAlert aria-hidden="true" /> Differs from your recent readings. This is a
          prompt to check, not a diagnosis.
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/components/health/reports-page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Download, Printer, Send, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { CrewNav } from "@/components/health/crew-nav";
import { LaneBadge } from "@/components/health/status-chip";
import { TrustRing } from "@/components/health/trust-ring";
import { formatValue, metricMeta } from "@/lib/metrics";
import { buildHandoff, summariseHandoff } from "@/lib/handoff/build";
import { environmentFor, missionConfig } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { Handoff } from "@/lib/types";

export function ReportsPage() {
  const observations = useStore((state) => state.observations);
  const checkIns = useStore((state) => state.checkIns);
  const encounters = useStore((state) => state.encounters);
  const environment = useStore((state) => state.environment);
  const enqueue = useStore((state) => state.enqueue);
  const notify = useStore((state) => state.notify);

  const [crewId, setCrewId] = useState(missionConfig.crew[0].id);
  const [encounterId, setEncounterId] = useState<string>("");
  const [includePrivate, setIncludePrivate] = useState(false);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");

  const crewEncounters = encounters.filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId);
  const encounter =
    crewEncounters.find((entry) => entry.id === encounterId) ?? crewEncounters[0] ?? null;
  const latestCheckIn =
    checkIns
      .filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId)
      .sort((a, b) => (a.observedAt < b.observedAt ? 1 : -1))[0] ?? null;

  const handoff: Handoff = useMemo(
    () =>
      buildHandoff({
        crewId,
        encounter,
        checkIn: latestCheckIn,
        observations,
        includePrivate,
        notes,
      }),
    [crewId, encounter, latestCheckIn, observations, includePrivate, notes],
  );

  const summary = summariseHandoff(handoff);
  const emergencyEncounters = encounters.filter(
    (entry) => entry.kind === "emergency" && entry.crewId === crewId,
  );
  const member = missionConfig.crew.find((entry) => entry.id === crewId)!;

  const download = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify({ handoff, summary }, null, 2)], {
      type: "application/json",
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = `crewsignal-handoff-${crewId}.json`;
    anchor.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const queue = async () => {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const preset = environmentFor(environment);
    await enqueue({
      id: `handoff-${handoff.id}-${Date.now()}`,
      crewId,
      kind: "handoff",
      createdAt: now,
      sentAt: null,
      acknowledgedAt: null,
      state: "queued",
      delaySeconds: preset.delaySeconds,
      title: `Handoff · ${member.name}`,
      payload: handoff,
      private: includePrivate,
      history: [
        {
          state: "queued",
          at: now,
          note: "Queued on board. Nothing has been sent yet.",
        },
      ],
    });
    notify("Handoff queued in the outbox. Delivery state is shown honestly.", "ok");
  };

  return (
    <section className="reports-page">
      <CrewNav />
      <header className="reports-head">
        <div>
          <p className="illustrative-label">HANDOFF PACKET — REVIEW BEFORE SHARING</p>
          <h1>Prepare a handoff</h1>
          <p className="assessment-sub">
            You choose what is shared. Private answers stay off unless you tick them.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="reports-actions no-print">
          <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={() => window.print()}>
            <Printer aria-hidden="true" /> Print / PDF
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={download}>
            <Download aria-hidden="true" /> Download JSON
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void queue()}>
            <Send aria-hidden="true" /> Queue for Earth
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="report-controls no-print">
        <label>
          Crew member
          <select
            value={crewId}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setCrewId(event.target.value);
              setEncounterId("");
            }}
          >
            {missionConfig.crew.map((entry) => (
              <option key={entry.id} value={entry.id}>
                {entry.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Source
          <select value={encounter?.id ?? ""} onChange={(event) => setEncounterId(event.target.value)}>
            <option value="">Latest check-in{encounter ? " + latest encounter" : ""}</option>
            {crewEncounters.map((entry) => (
              <option key={entry.id} value={entry.id}>
                {entry.kind} · {new Date(entry.startedAt).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
                  timeZone: "UTC",
                  day: "2-digit",
                  month: "short",
                  hour: "2-digit",
                  minute: "2-digit",
                })}{" "}
                UTC
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="checkbox-row">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={includePrivate}
            onChange={(event) => setIncludePrivate(event.target.checked)}
          />
          Include mood, stress, rested and connected
        </label>
        <label className="report-notes">
          Note to the flight surgeon
          <textarea rows={2} value={notes} onChange={(event) => setNotes(event.target.value)} />
        </label>
      </div>

      <article className="report-card">
        <h2>
          <span className="report-number">01</span> Who and when
        </h2>
        <p>
          {member.name} · {member.role} · {missionConfig.missionName}, day{" "}
          {missionConfig.missionDay}. Prepared{" "}
          {new Date(handoff.createdAt).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
            timeZone: "UTC",
            day: "2-digit",
            month: "short",
            hour: "2-digit",
            minute: "2-digit",
          })}{" "}
          UTC.
        </p>
        <p>Recipients: {handoff.recipients.join(", ")}.</p>
      </article>

      <article className="report-card">
        <h2>
          <span className="report-number">02</span> Reported symptoms
        </h2>
        {handoff.symptoms.length === 0 ? (
          <p>No symptoms reported.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="report-list">
            {handoff.symptoms.map((symptom) => (
              <li key={symptom.id}>
                <strong>{symptom.name}</strong> — started {symptom.onset.replace(/_/g, " ")},
                trend {symptom.trend}
                {symptom.urgent && <span className="urgent-tag">Needs attention</span>}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        {emergencyEncounters.length > 0 && (
          <div className="emergency-report">
            <h3>Emergency situation report</h3>
            {emergencyEncounters.slice(0, 2).map((entry) => (
              <p key={entry.id}>
                {new Date(entry.startedAt).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
                  timeZone: "UTC",
                  day: "2-digit",
                  month: "short",
                  hour: "2-digit",
                  minute: "2-digit",
                })}{" "}
                UTC — {entry.pathwayId}, outcome {entry.outcome}.{" "}
                {entry.actions.map((action) => action.text).join(" ") || "No actions logged."}
              </p>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </article>

      <article className="report-card">
        <h2>
          <span className="report-number">03</span> Measurements
        </h2>
        {handoff.observations.length === 0 ? (
          <p>No measurements in the last 72 hours.</p>
        ) : (
          <table className="report-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th scope="col">Reading</th>
                <th scope="col">Value</th>
                <th scope="col">Lane</th>
                <th scope="col">Measured (UTC)</th>
                <th scope="col">Quality</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {handoff.observations.slice(0, 40).map((entry) => (
                <tr key={entry.id}>
                  <td>{metricMeta[entry.metric].label}</td>
                  <td>{formatValue(entry.metric, entry.value)}</td>
                  <td>
                    <LaneBadge lane={entry.lane} />
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    {new Date(entry.observedAt).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
                      timeZone: "UTC",
                      hour: "2-digit",
                      minute: "2-digit",
                    })}
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span className="report-quality">
                      {entry.quality}
                      <TrustRing observation={entry} size={16} />
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
        <p className="disclaimer-line">
          Quality flags are shown, not hidden. Flagged values are excluded from comparisons.
        </p>
      </article>

      <article className="report-card">
        <h2>
          <span className="report-number">04</span> Trends compared with this person
        </h2>
        {handoff.trends.length === 0 ? (
          <p>Not enough comparable history to describe a trend.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="report-list">
            {handoff.trends.map((trend) => (
              <li key={trend.metric}>{trend.text}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
        {handoff.unknowns.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <h3>Unknowns</h3>
            <ul className="report-list">
              {handoff.unknowns.map((unknown) => (
                <li key={unknown}>{unknown}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </>
        )}
      </article>

      <article className="report-card">
        <h2>
          <span className="report-number">05</span> Summary
        </h2>
        <ul className="report-list">
          {summary.map((line) => (
            <li key={line}>{line}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        {handoff.notes && <p className="report-note">Crew note: {handoff.notes}</p>}
        <p className="disclaimer-line">
          <ShieldCheck aria-hidden="true" /> Generated from the data above. Private answers{" "}
          {includePrivate ? "are included" : "are excluded"}. Demo packet, not a medical record.
        </p>
      </article>

      <p className="no-print">
        <Link className="text-button" href={`/crew/${crewId}`}>
          Edit source data on the daily page
        </Link>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

import { Check, TriangleAlert, Unplug } from "lucide-react";
import type { SensorQuality } from "./health-data";

function SensorQualityBadge({ status }: { status: SensorQuality }) {
  const Icon =
    status === "Usable" ? Check : status === "Poor contact" ? TriangleAlert : Unplug;

  return (
    <span className={`reading-status-word ${status.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "-")}`}>
      <Icon size={13} />
      {status}
    </span>
  );
}

import {
  AlertTriangle,
  CircleDashed,
  CircleHelp,
  CircleCheck,
  CircleDot,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { Comparison } from "@/lib/types";

const icons = {
  logged: CircleDot,
  attention: AlertTriangle,
  no_valid_reading: CircleDashed,
  within_range: CircleCheck,
  insufficient_history: CircleHelp,
} as const;

/** Status is never colour alone: every state ships an icon and text. */
export function StatusChip({
  comparison,
  compact = false,
}: {
  comparison: Comparison;
  compact?: boolean;
}) {
  const Icon = icons[comparison.state];
  return (
    <span
      className={`comparison-chip status-${comparison.state} ${compact ? "compact" : ""}`}
      title={comparison.detail}
    >
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
      <span>{comparison.label}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

export function LaneBadge({ lane }: { lane: "manual" | "live" | "replay" | "synthetic" }) {
  const labels = {
    manual: "Manual",
    live: "Live",
    replay: "Replay",
    synthetic: "Synthetic",
  } as const;
  return <span className={`lane-badge lane-${lane}`}>{labels[lane]}</span>;
}

 FILE: frontend/components/health/surgeon-page.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { CheckCheck, Monitor } from "lucide-react";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { Handoff, OutboxItem } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  acknowledgeOnServer,
  connectStream,
  fetchSurgeonInbox,
  packetToOutbox,
  type ServerPacket,
} from "@/lib/outbox/http-transport";

function isHandoff(payload: OutboxItem["payload"]): payload is Handoff {
  return typeof payload === "object" && payload !== null && "observations" in payload;
}

/** Ground console. Reads the real inbox when a backend is configured. */
export function SurgeonPage() {
  const outbox = useStore((state) => state.outbox);
  const acknowledge = useStore((state) => state.acknowledge);
  const serverConfigured = useStore((state) => state.serverConfigured);
  const serverReachable = useStore((state) => state.serverReachable);
  const setServerStatus = useStore((state) => state.setServerStatus);

  const [serverPackets, setServerPackets] = useState<ServerPacket[] | null>(null);
  const [streamNote, setStreamNote] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!serverConfigured) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    const load = async () => {
      const result = await fetchSurgeonInbox();
      if (cancelled) return;
      if (result.ok) {
        setServerPackets(result.packets);
        setStreamNote(null);
        setServerStatus(true);
      } else {
        setStreamNote(result.message);
        if (result.status === 0) setServerStatus(false);
      }
    };
    void load();
    const stop = connectStream({
      onDelivered: () => void load(),
      onAcknowledged: () => void load(),
      onError: () => setStreamNote("Stream disconnected. Reconnecting and polling."),
    });
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => void load(), 5000);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      stop();
      window.clearInterval(timer);
    };
  }, [serverConfigured, setServerStatus]);

  const localReceived = outbox
    .filter((item) => item.state !== "queued")
    .sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? 1 : -1));
  const rows = (
    serverConfigured ? (serverPackets ?? []).map(packetToOutbox) : localReceived
  ).sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? 1 : -1));

  const handleAcknowledge = async (item: OutboxItem) => {
    if (!serverConfigured) {
      await acknowledge(item.id);
      return;
    }
    const result = await acknowledgeOnServer(item.id);
    if (!result.ok) {
      setStreamNote(result.message);
      if (result.status === 0) setServerStatus(false);
      return;
    }
    setServerPackets((current) =>
      (current ?? []).map((packet) => (packet.id === item.id ? result.packet : packet)),
    );
  };

  return (
    <section className="surgeon-page">
      <header className="surgeon-head">
        <div>
          <p className="surgeon-kicker">
            <Monitor aria-hidden="true" /> GROUND CONSOLE · DEMO
          </p>
          <h1>Flight surgeon inbox</h1>
          <p>
            Packets appear here only after the configured link delay. Private entries are
            hidden unless the astronaut shared them.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Link className="secondary-button" href="/outbox">
          Astronaut outbox
        </Link>
      </header>

      {serverConfigured && (
        <p className={`surgeon-server ${serverReachable ? "up" : "down"}`}>
          Ground server: {serverReachable ? "reachable" : "unreachable"}
          {streamNote ? ` · ${streamNote}` : ""}
        </p>
      )}

      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty-state">
          <h2>No packets received</h2>
          <p>Queue a handoff or wait for the delay to elapse.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="surgeon-list">
          {rows.map((item) => {
            const handoff = isHandoff(item.payload) ? item.payload : null;
            return (
              <li key={item.id} className="surgeon-card">
                <header>
                  <div>
                    <h2>{item.title}</h2>
                    <p>
                      Received{" "}
                      {new Date(item.sentAt ?? item.createdAt).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
                        timeZone: "UTC",
                        day: "2-digit",
                        month: "short",
                        hour: "2-digit",
                        minute: "2-digit",
                      })}{" "}
                      UTC · {item.state}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {item.state !== "acknowledged" ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="primary-button"
                      onClick={() => void handleAcknowledge(item)}
                    >
                      <CheckCheck aria-hidden="true" /> Acknowledge
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="ack-pill">
                      <CheckCheck aria-hidden="true" /> Acknowledged
                    </span>
                  )}
                </header>

                {handoff ? (
                  <>
                    <section>
                      <h3>Symptoms</h3>
                      {handoff.symptoms.length === 0 ? (
                        <p>None reported.</p>
                      ) : (
                        <ul>
                          {handoff.symptoms.map((symptom) => (
                            <li key={symptom.id}>
                              {symptom.name} · {symptom.onset} · {symptom.trend}
                            </li>
                          ))}
                        </ul>
                      )}
                    </section>
                    <section>
                      <h3>Measurements</h3>
                      <ul>
                        {handoff.observations.slice(0, 12).map((entry) => (
                          <li key={entry.id}>
                            {entry.metric.replace(/_/g, " ")}:{" "}
                            {entry.value === null ? "not measured" : entry.value} {entry.unit}{" "}
                            · {entry.lane} · {entry.quality}
                          </li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    </section>
                    <section>
                      <h3>Timeline</h3>
                      <ol>
                        {handoff.actions.map((action) => (
                          <li key={`${action.at}-${action.text}`}>
                            {new Date(action.at).toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
                              timeZone: "UTC",
                              hour: "2-digit",
                              minute: "2-digit",
                            })}{" "}
                            UTC — {action.text}
                          </li>
                        ))}
                      </ol>
                      {handoff.unknowns.length > 0 && (
                        <p className="surgeon-unknown">
                          Unknowns: {handoff.unknowns.join("; ")}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </section>
                    <p className="disclaimer-line">
                      Private answers: {handoff.includePrivate ? "shared" : "not shared"}.
                      Demo packet — not clinically reviewed.
                    </p>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <p>
                    {item.kind === "support"
                      ? "Private support request. Contents withheld unless shared."
                      : "Packet payload not available in this demo."}
                  </p>
                )}
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ShieldQuestion } from "lucide-react";
import { BottomSheet } from "@/components/ui/bottom-sheet";
import { metricMeta, methodLabels, contextLabels } from "@/lib/metrics";
import { isLateEntry } from "@/lib/quality/gate";
import type { Observation, Quality } from "@/lib/types";

const qualityText: Record<Quality, string> = {
  ok: "Usable",
  no_contact: "Not enough sensor contact",
  stale: "Old reading, not current",
  out_of_range: "Outside the plausible sensor range",
  motion: "Movement spoiled this reading",
  not_measured: "Nothing measured",
};

const qualityAction: Record<Quality, string> = {
  ok: "No action needed. Keep the sensor in place.",
  no_contact: "Check the sensor contact and try again.",
  stale: "Take a new reading if you need a current number.",
  out_of_range: "Repeat the measurement before using this number.",
  motion: "Sit still and repeat the measurement.",
  not_measured: "Measure it, or record that you were unable to.",
};

const laneText = {
  manual: "Manual entry",
  live: "Live sensor",
  replay: "Replay (ground file)",
  synthetic: "Synthetic demo data",
} as const;

export function formatClock(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "—";
  return date.toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
    timeZone: "UTC",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
}

export function TrustRing({
  observation,
  size = 22,
}: {
  observation: Observation | null;
  size?: number;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  if (!observation) {
    return (
      <span className="trust-ring trust-empty" aria-label="No observation">
        <ShieldQuestion aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
    );
  }

  const quality = observation.quality;
  const late = isLateEntry(observation);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={`trust-ring quality-${quality} lane-${observation.lane}`}
        style={{ width: size, height: size }}
        onClick={(event) => {
          event.stopPropagation();
          setOpen(true);
        }}
        aria-label={`Why can't I trust this number? ${metricMeta[observation.metric].label}, ${qualityText[quality]}, ${laneText[observation.lane]}`}
      >
        <span className="trust-ring-core" aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <BottomSheet
          title="Why can't I trust this number?"
          subtitle={`${metricMeta[observation.metric].label} · ${qualityText[quality]}`}
          onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        >
          <dl className="trust-details">
            <div>
              <dt>How this number was produced</dt>
              <dd>{laneText[observation.lane]}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Method</dt>
              <dd>{methodLabels[observation.method] ?? observation.method}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Measured at</dt>
              <dd>{formatClock(observation.observedAt)} UTC</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Entered at</dt>
              <dd>
                {formatClock(observation.enteredAt)} UTC
                {late && (
                  <span className="trust-note">
                    {" "}
                    Entered later than measured. Judged by the measured time.
                  </span>
                )}
              </dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Context</dt>
              <dd>{contextLabels[observation.context]}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Quality</dt>
              <dd>
                {observation.qualityReason ?? qualityText[quality]}
              </dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>What to do</dt>
              <dd>{qualityAction[quality]}</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
          <p className="disclaimer-line">
            Sensor checks only. This is not a diagnosis and not a substitute for
            medical review.
          </p>
        </BottomSheet>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/components/health/use-health-simulation.ts
 lines: 122

"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  createInitialHistories,
  createInitialVitals,
  type LiveVitals,
  type QualityOverride,
  type SensorHistories,
  type SensorQuality,
} from "./health-data";

const HISTORY_LENGTH = 60;

function nextHistory(
  current: Array<number | null>,
  value: number,
  usable: boolean,
): Array<number | null> {
  return [...current.slice(-(HISTORY_LENGTH - 1)), usable ? value : null];
}

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

export function useHealthSimulation(
  restingHeartRate: number,
  crewId: string,
  baselineShift = 0,
) {
  const [vitals, setVitals] = useState<LiveVitals>(() =>
    createInitialVitals(restingHeartRate + baselineShift),
  );
  const vitalsRef = useRef(vitals);
  const [histories, setHistories] = useState<SensorHistories>(() =>
    createInitialHistories(restingHeartRate + baselineShift),
  );
  const [qualityOverride, setQualityOverride] =
    useState<QualityOverride>("Auto");
  const qualityOverrideRef = useRef<QualityOverride>("Auto");
  const quality: SensorQuality =
    qualityOverride === "Auto" ? "Usable" : qualityOverride;

  useEffect(() => {
    const initialVitals = createInitialVitals(restingHeartRate + baselineShift);
    vitalsRef.current = initialVitals;
    setVitals(initialVitals);
    setHistories(createInitialHistories(restingHeartRate + baselineShift));

    let elapsedSeconds = 0;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      elapsedSeconds += 1;
      const current = vitalsRef.current;
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const next: LiveVitals = {
        ...current,
        heartRate: Math.round(
          clamp(current.heartRate + (Math.random() * 3 - 1.5), 55, 95),
        ),
        skinTemperature: Number(
          clamp(
            current.skinTemperature + (Math.random() * 0.06 - 0.03),
            33.85,
            34.15,
          ).toFixed(1),
        ),
        motion:
          elapsedSeconds % 40 < 4
            ? Number((0.2 + Math.random() * 0.1).toFixed(2))
            : Number(clamp(0.05 + Math.random() * 0.02, 0.03, 0.08).toFixed(2)),
        updatedAt: {
          ...current.updatedAt,
          heartRate: now,
          skinTemperature: now,
          motion: now,
        },
      };

      if (elapsedSeconds % 30 === 0) {
        next.systolic = 117 + Math.round(Math.random() * 6 - 3);
        next.diastolic = 76 + Math.round(Math.random() * 4 - 2);
        next.updatedAt = { ...next.updatedAt, bloodPressure: now };
      }

      vitalsRef.current = next;
      setVitals(next);

      const usable = qualityOverrideRef.current === "Auto" ||
        qualityOverrideRef.current === "Usable";
      setHistories((previous) => ({
        heartRate: nextHistory(previous.heartRate, next.heartRate, usable),
        skinTemperature: nextHistory(
          previous.skinTemperature,
          next.skinTemperature,
          usable,
        ),
        bloodPressure: nextHistory(
          previous.bloodPressure,
          next.systolic,
          usable,
        ),
        motion: nextHistory(previous.motion, next.motion, usable),
      }));
    }, 1000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [crewId, restingHeartRate, baselineShift]);

  const changeQualityOverride = (next: QualityOverride) => {
    qualityOverrideRef.current = next;
    setQualityOverride(next);
  };

  return {
    vitals,
    histories,
    quality,
    qualityOverride,
    onQualityOverrideChange: changeQualityOverride,
  };
}

"use client";

import { bodyAreas } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import type { BodyArea, StatusState } from "@/lib/types";

export { Particles } from "./particles";

export type BodyMapMode = "status" | "coverage";

function BodyMap({
  selected,
  labels,
  onSelect,
  mode = "status",
  statuses = {},
}: {
  selected: BodyArea;
  labels: Partial<Record<BodyArea, string[]>>;
  onSelect: (area: BodyArea) => void;
  mode?: BodyMapMode;
  statuses?: Partial<Record<BodyArea, StatusState>>;
}) {
  const points: Record<Exclude<BodyArea, "Radiation">, [number, number]> = {
    Head: [160, 48],
    Heart: [170, 123],
    Arm: [94, 151],
    Skin: [148, 112],
    Abdomen: [160, 178],
    Legs: [163, 279],
  };
  const callouts: Record<
    Exclude<BodyArea, "Radiation">,
    {
      x: number;
      y: number;
      textX: number;
      textY: number;
      side: "left" | "right";
    }
  > = {
    Head: { x: 235, y: 42, textX: 243, textY: 38, side: "right" },
    Heart: { x: 235, y: 123, textX: 243, textY: 127, side: "right" },
    Arm: { x: 12, y: 151, textX: 4, textY: 147, side: "left" },
    Skin: { x: 235, y: 100, textX: 243, textY: 103, side: "right" },
    Abdomen: { x: 235, y: 184, textX: 243, textY: 188, side: "right" },
    Legs: { x: 235, y: 279, textX: 243, textY: 283, side: "right" },
  };
  const physicalAreas = bodyAreas.filter(
    (area): area is Exclude<BodyArea, "Radiation"> => area !== "Radiation",
  );

  return (
    <svg
      className="body-svg"
      viewBox="0 0 440 350"
      role="img"
      aria-label="Body map. Select a body area to see its readings."
    >
      <ellipse cx="160" cy="326" rx="72" ry="12" fill="var(--shadow)" />
      <g stroke="var(--body-line)" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinejoin="round">
        <circle cx="160" cy="42" r="28" fill="#f2d8c7" />
        <path
          d="M139 75 Q160 66 181 75 L198 91 221 169 Q223 181 210 184 L192 136 192 222 128 222 128 136 110 184 Q97 181 99 169 L122 91Z"
          fill="#dcefe9"
        />
        <path
          d="M128 222 L125 310 Q124 326 139 326 L152 326 158 236 164 236 170 326 184 326 Q198 325 195 310 L192 222Z"
          fill="#d9e4f5"
        />
        <path
          d="M147 97 Q160 87 173 97 L172 127 Q160 137 148 127Z"
          fill="#baded5"
          stroke="none"
        />
        <path
          d="M155 101 C142 94 141 111 160 121 C179 111 177 94 165 101 L160 105Z"
          fill="#ed8d7c"
          stroke="none"
        />
        <path
          d="M143 145 Q160 139 177 145 L175 182 Q160 190 145 182Z"
          fill="#f4dfc7"
          stroke="none"
        />
        <path d="M138 220 L182 220" fill="none" stroke="#a7cfc2" />
      </g>

      {physicalAreas.map((area) => {
        const [x, y] = points[area];
        const callout = callouts[area];
        const isSelected = selected === area;
        const values = labels[area] ?? ["Not entered yet"];
        const status = statuses[area] ?? "insufficient_history";
        const statusClass =
          mode === "coverage"
            ? status === "insufficient_history" || status === "logged"
              ? "coverage-missing"
              : "coverage-assessed"
            : `hotspot-${status}`;
        const lineStartX = callout.side === "left" ? 82 : x + 8;
        const lineEndX = callout.side === "left" ? 92 : callout.x;

        return (
          <g key={area}>
            <line
              x1={lineStartX}
              y1={y}
              x2={lineEndX}
              y2={callout.y}
              stroke="var(--muted)"
              strokeWidth="0.8"
            />
            <text
              x={callout.textX}
              y={callout.textY}
              className="body-reading-label"
              textAnchor={callout.side === "left" ? "start" : "start"}
            >
              <tspan>{values[0]}</tspan>
              {values.slice(1, 2).map((value) => (
                <tspan key={value} x={callout.textX} dy="13">
                  {value}
                </tspan>
              ))}
            </text>
            <g
              onClick={() => onSelect(area)}
              className={`body-hotspot ${statusClass}`}
              role="button"
              tabIndex={0}
              aria-label={`Select ${area}: ${values.join(", ")}. ${
                mode === "coverage"
                  ? status === "insufficient_history" || status === "logged"
                    ? "Not assessed."
                    : "Assessed."
                  : `Status: ${status.replace(/_/g, " ")}.`
              }`}
              onKeyDown={(event) => {
                if (event.key === "Enter" || event.key === " ") {
                  event.preventDefault();
                  onSelect(area);
                }
              }}
            >
              <circle
                cx={x}
                cy={y}
                r={isSelected ? 17 : 11}
                fill={isSelected ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--surface)"}
                fillOpacity={isSelected ? 0.25 : 0.85}
                stroke={isSelected ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--muted)"}
                strokeWidth={isSelected ? 3 : 1.5}
              />
              <circle cx={x} cy={y} r="3" className="hotspot-core" />
            </g>
          </g>
        );
      })}
    </svg>
  );
}

function LineChart({
  values,
  color = "var(--accent)",
  tall = false,
  label = "Simulated readings over time",
}: {
  values: Array<number | null>;
  color?: string;
  tall?: boolean;
  label?: string;
}) {
  const validValues = values.filter((value): value is number => value !== null);
  const min = validValues.length ? Math.min(...validValues) : 0;
  const max = validValues.length ? Math.max(...validValues) : 1;
  const spread = Math.max(max - min, 1);
  const points = values.map((value, index) =>
    value === null
      ? null
      : {
          x: values.length < 2 ? 300 : (index / (values.length - 1)) * 600,
          y: 120 - ((value - min) / spread) * 90,
        },
  );
  const segments: string[] = [];
  let segment = "";
  points.forEach((point) => {
    if (!point) {
      if (segment) segments.push(segment);
      segment = "";
      return;
    }

    segment += `${segment ? " L" : "M"}${point.x},${point.y}`;
  });
  if (segment) segments.push(segment);

  return (
    <svg
      className={`line-chart ${tall ? "chart-tall" : ""}`}
      viewBox="0 0 600 140"
      role="img"
      aria-label={label}
    >
      {[25, 60, 95, 130].map((y) => (
        <line
          key={y}
          x1="0"
          x2="600"
          y1={y}
          y2={y}
          stroke="var(--line)"
          strokeDasharray="3 6"
        />
      ))}
      {segments.map((path, index) => (
        <path
          key={index}
          d={path}
          fill="none"
          stroke={color}
          strokeWidth="3"
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
      ))}
    </svg>
  );
}

export { BodyMap, LineChart };
