"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Lock, LockOpen, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { BottomSheet } from "@/components/ui/bottom-sheet";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { CheckIn } from "@/lib/types";

const answers = [
  { key: "mood", label: "Mood", low: "Very difficult", high: "At ease" },
  { key: "stress", label: "Stress", low: "Low", high: "High" },
  { key: "rested", label: "Rested", low: "Not rested", high: "Rested" },
  { key: "connected", label: "Connected", low: "Isolated", high: "Connected" },
] as const;

function average(values: number[]): number | null {
  if (values.length === 0) return null;
  return values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length;
}

/** No composite score: the four answers and their trends are shown as-is. */
export function WellbeingTile({
  crewId,
  checkIns,
  onRequestSupport,
}: {
  crewId: string;
  checkIns: CheckIn[];
  onRequestSupport: () => void;
}) {
  const pinHash = useStore((state) => state.pinHash);
  const unlocked = useStore((state) => state.privateUnlocked);
  const setPin = useStore((state) => state.setPin);
  const unlockPrivate = useStore((state) => state.unlockPrivate);
  const lockPrivate = useStore((state) => state.lockPrivate);
  const notify = useStore((state) => state.notify);
  const [pin, setPinValue] = useState("");
  const [sheetOpen, setSheetOpen] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const mine = useMemo(
    () =>
      checkIns
        .filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId)
        .sort((a, b) => (a.observedAt < b.observedAt ? 1 : -1)),
    [checkIns, crewId],
  );
  const current = mine[0];
  const previous = mine.slice(1, 8);

  const submitPin = async () => {
    if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(pin)) {
      setError("Use four digits.");
      return;
    }
    if (pinHash) {
      const ok = await unlockPrivate(pin);
      if (!ok) {
        setError("That PIN does not match.");
        return;
      }
    } else {
      await setPin(pin);
      notify("Local PIN set. Private answers are unlocked on this device.", "ok");
    }
    setPinValue("");
    setError("");
  };

  if (!unlocked) {
    return (
      <section className="wellbeing-tile locked" aria-labelledby="wellbeing-title">
        <header>
          <h2 id="wellbeing-title">How you have been feeling</h2>
          <p>Private answers. Locked on this device.</p>
        </header>
        <div className="pin-row">
          <Lock aria-hidden="true" />
          <label htmlFor="wellbeing-pin">
            {pinHash ? "Enter your 4-digit PIN" : "Set a 4-digit PIN"}
          </label>
          <input
            id="wellbeing-pin"
            inputMode="numeric"
            autoComplete="off"
            maxLength={4}
            value={pin}
            onChange={(event) => setPinValue(event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))}
            aria-invalid={!!error}
            aria-describedby={error ? "pin-error" : undefined}
          />
          <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void submitPin()}>
            {pinHash ? "Unlock" : "Set PIN"}
          </button>
        </div>
        {error && (
          <p id="pin-error" role="alert" className="field-error">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <p className="disclaimer-line">
          A local lock, not encryption. Private answers are never shared unless you tick
          them in a handoff.
        </p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section className="wellbeing-tile" aria-labelledby="wellbeing-title">
      <header className="wellbeing-head">
        <div>
          <h2 id="wellbeing-title">How you have been feeling</h2>
          <p>Self-reported. No combined score is calculated.</p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" className="text-button" onClick={lockPrivate}>
          <LockOpen aria-hidden="true" /> Lock
        </button>
      </header>

      <ul className="answer-bars">
        {answers.map((entry) => {
          const value = current ? current[entry.key] : null;
          const history = previous
            .map((checkIn) => checkIn[entry.key])
            .filter((item): item is number => item !== null);
          const avg = average(history);
          const change = value !== null && avg !== null ? value - avg : null;
          return (
            <li key={entry.key}>
              <div className="answer-label">
                <strong>{entry.label}</strong>
                <span>
                  {value === null ? "Not answered" : `${value} / 5`}
                  {change !== null && change !== 0 && (
                    <em>
                      {" "}
                      {change > 0 ? "up" : "down"} {Math.abs(change).toFixed(1)} vs recent
                    </em>
                  )}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="answer-track" aria-hidden="true">
                <span style={{ width: `${value === null ? 0 : (value / 5) * 100}%` }} />
              </div>
              <div className="answer-scale">
                <span>{entry.low}</span>
                <span>{entry.high}</span>
              </div>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>

      <div className="wellbeing-note">
        <ShieldCheck aria-hidden="true" />
        <p>
          Private answers stay on this device and appear in a handoff only when you tick
          them.
        </p>
      </div>

      <button type="button" className="secondary-button" onClick={() => setSheetOpen(true)}>
        Request private support
      </button>

      {sheetOpen && (
        <BottomSheet
          title="Request private support"
          subtitle="Queued in your outbox. Nothing is sent while the link is down."
          onClose={() => setSheetOpen(false)}
        >
          <p>
            This creates a private request in your outbox. It is not a diagnosis and it
            does not share your mood answers.
          </p>
          <div className="sheet-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="primary-button"
              onClick={() => {
                onRequestSupport();
                setSheetOpen(false);
              }}
            >
              Add private request to outbox
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="secondary-button"
              onClick={() => setSheetOpen(false)}
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
          </div>
        </BottomSheet>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { Particles } from "@/components/health/particles";
import { LinkRibbon } from "@/components/shell/link-ribbon";
import { EmergencyBar } from "@/components/shell/emergency-bar";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import {
  apiConfigured,
  connectStream,
  pingServer,
} from "@/lib/outbox/http-transport";

export function AppShell({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const theme = useStore((state) => state.theme);
  const largeText = useStore((state) => state.largeText);
  const nightWatch = useStore((state) => state.nightWatch);
  const toast = useStore((state) => state.toast);
  const storageError = useStore((state) => state.storageError);
  const hydrate = useStore((state) => state.hydrate);
  const advanceOutbox = useStore((state) => state.advanceOutbox);
  const refreshOutbox = useStore((state) => state.refreshOutbox);
  const clearToast = useStore((state) => state.clearToast);
  const setServerStatus = useStore((state) => state.setServerStatus);
  const applyServerPacket = useStore((state) => state.applyServerPacket);
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const emergencyMode = pathname.startsWith("/emergency");

  useEffect(() => {
    void hydrate();
  }, [hydrate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      void advanceOutbox();
    }, 2000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [advanceOutbox]);

  // Ground-server reachability and events are separate from the simulated link.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!apiConfigured) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    const check = async () => {
      const result = await pingServer();
      if (cancelled) return;
      if (result.ok) setServerStatus(true, result.link.delayScale);
      else setServerStatus(false);
    };
    void check();
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => void check(), 15_000);
    const stopStream = connectStream({
      onDelivered: (packet) => void applyServerPacket(packet),
      onAcknowledged: (packet) => void applyServerPacket(packet),
      onError: () => setServerStatus(false),
    });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      window.clearInterval(timer);
      stopStream();
    };
  }, [applyServerPacket, setServerStatus]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(clearToast, 5000);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast, clearToast]);

  // The mock ground console lives in another tab; keep the outbox honest.
  useEffect(() => {
    let channel: BroadcastChannel | null = null;
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel !== "undefined") {
      channel = new BroadcastChannel("crewsignal-outbox");
      channel.onmessage = () => void refreshOutbox();
    }
    const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
      if (event.key?.startsWith("crewsignal:repo:")) void refreshOutbox();
    };
    window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
      channel?.close();
    };
  }, [refreshOutbox]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") return;
    if (!("serviceWorker" in navigator)) return;
    navigator.serviceWorker.register("/sw.js").catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      className={`app-shell ${theme} ${largeText ? "large-text" : ""} ${
        nightWatch ? "night-watch" : ""
      } ${emergencyMode ? "emergency-mode" : ""}`}
    >
      <a className="skip-link" href="#main-content">
        Skip to main content
      </a>
      {!emergencyMode && <Particles />}
      <LinkRibbon />
      <div id="main-content">{children}</div>
      <EmergencyBar />
      <div className="sr-only" aria-live="polite" role="status">
        {toast?.message ?? ""}
      </div>
      {toast && (
        <div className={`app-toast toast-${toast.tone}`} role="status">
          {toast.message}
        </div>
      )}
      {storageError && (
        <div className="storage-warning" role="alert">
          {storageError}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { PhoneCall, ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";

/**
 * Persistent action bar. It never diagnoses and never asks for typing first.
 */
export function EmergencyBar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  if (pathname === "/emergency" || pathname === "/emergency/drill") return null;

  return (
    <div className="emergency-bar" role="region" aria-label="Emergency actions">
      <span className="emergency-bar-label">If someone needs help now</span>
      <Link className="emergency-bar-primary" href="/emergency">
        <ShieldAlert aria-hidden="true" /> Alert the crew
      </Link>
      <Link className="emergency-bar-secondary" href="/reports">
        <PhoneCall aria-hidden="true" /> Contact medical officer
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

 FILE: frontend/components/shell/link-ribbon.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Radio, WifiOff } from "lucide-react";
import {
  environmentFor,
  environments,
  formatDelay,
  missionConfig,
} from "@/lib/mission-config";
import { useStore } from "@/lib/store";
import type { LinkState } from "@/lib/types";

const linkLabels: Record<LinkState, string> = {
  live: "Live",
  delayed: "Delayed",
  blackout: "Blackout",
};

export function LinkRibbon() {
  const environment = useStore((state) => state.environment);
  const link = useStore((state) => state.link);
  const setEnvironment = useStore((state) => state.setEnvironment);
  const setLink = useStore((state) => state.setLink);
  const serverConfigured = useStore((state) => state.serverConfigured);
  const serverReachable = useStore((state) => state.serverReachable);
  const serverDelayScale = useStore((state) => state.serverDelayScale);
  const [online, setOnline] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => setOnline(navigator.onLine);
    update();
    window.addEventListener("online", update);
    window.addEventListener("offline", update);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("online", update);
      window.removeEventListener("offline", update);
    };
  }, []);

  const preset = environmentFor(environment);
  const effectiveLink: LinkState = !online && link !== "blackout" ? "blackout" : link;

  return (
    <div className="link-ribbon" role="region" aria-label="Link and environment">
      <div className="link-ribbon-main">
        <span className={`link-dot ${effectiveLink}`} aria-hidden="true" />
        <strong>{linkLabels[effectiveLink]}</strong>
        <span className="ribbon-sep">·</span>
        <label className="ribbon-field">
          <span className="sr-only">Environment</span>
          <select
            value={environment}
            onChange={(event) =>
              void setEnvironment(event.target.value as typeof environment)
            }
          >
            {environments.map((entry) => (
              <option key={entry.key} value={entry.key}>
                {entry.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <span className="ribbon-note">
          {effectiveLink === "blackout"
            ? "Packets stay queued on board."
            : `A reply from Earth would take about ${formatDelay(preset.delaySeconds)}.`}
          {serverConfigured && serverDelayScale !== 1 && (
            <>
              {" "}
              <span className="ribbon-compression">
                scenario delay {formatDelay(preset.delaySeconds)} · demo compressed ×
                {Math.round(1 / serverDelayScale)}
              </span>
            </>
          )}
        </span>
        {serverConfigured && (
          <span
            className={`ribbon-server ${
              serverReachable === null ? "checking" : serverReachable ? "up" : "down"
            }`}
            title="The simulated link state is separate from the ground server."
          >
            Ground server:{" "}
            {serverReachable === null ? "checking" : serverReachable ? "reachable" : "unreachable"}
          </span>
        )}
        {!online && (
          <span className="ribbon-offline">
            <WifiOff aria-hidden="true" /> Device offline
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="link-ribbon-controls" role="group" aria-label="Link state">
        {(["live", "delayed", "blackout"] as LinkState[]).map((state) => (
          <button
            key={state}
            type="button"
            className={link === state ? "active" : ""}
            aria-pressed={link === state}
            onClick={() => void setLink(state)}
          >
            {linkLabels[state]}
          </button>
        ))}
        <Link className="ribbon-link" href="/outbox">
          <Radio aria-hidden="true" /> Outbox
        </Link>
        <span className="ribbon-day">Day {missionConfig.missionDay}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

import './StarBorder.css';

const THEMES = {
  dark: {
    surface: '#0c0c0f',
    text: '#fafafa',
    track: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)',
    light: '#ffffff'
  },
  light: {
    surface: '#ffffff',
    text: '#18181b',
    track: 'rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.12)',
    light: '#18181b'
  }
};

const HOVERS = ['lap', 'brighten', 'reveal', 'none'];
const BLEED = 48;
const LAP = 0.9;
const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
const WHITE = [255, 255, 255];

const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
const wrap = (value, total) => ((value % total) + total) % total;
const ease = t => (t < 0.5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - (2 - 2 * t) ** 3 / 2);
const mix = (a, b, t) => [a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t];
const rgba = (rgb, alpha) =>
  `rgba(${Math.round(rgb[0])}, ${Math.round(rgb[1])}, ${Math.round(rgb[2])}, ${clamp(alpha, 0, 1).toFixed(3)})`;

let colorContext = null;

const toRgb = color => {
  colorContext = colorContext ?? document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
  if (!colorContext) return WHITE;
  colorContext.fillStyle = '#000000';
  colorContext.fillStyle = color;
  const value = String(colorContext.fillStyle);
  if (value.startsWith('#')) {
    const hex = parseInt(value.slice(1, 7), 16);
    return [(hex >> 16) & 255, (hex >> 8) & 255, hex & 255];
  }
  const parts = value.match(/[\d.]+/g)?.map(Number) ?? WHITE;
  return [parts[0], parts[1], parts[2]];
};

const outline = (width, height, radius) => {
  const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(radius, width / 2, height / 2));
  const arc = (Math.PI * r) / 2;
  const across = Math.max(0, width - 2 * r);
  const down = Math.max(0, height - 2 * r);
  const segments = [
    { length: across, x: r, y: 0, dx: 1, dy: 0, angle: null },
    { length: arc, x: width - r, y: r, dx: 0, dy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2 },
    { length: down, x: width, y: r, dx: 0, dy: 1, angle: null },
    { length: arc, x: width - r, y: height - r, dx: 0, dy: 0, angle: 0 },
    { length: across, x: width - r, y: height, dx: -1, dy: 0, angle: null },
    { length: arc, x: r, y: height - r, dx: 0, dy: 0, angle: Math.PI / 2 },
    { length: down, x: 0, y: height - r, dx: 0, dy: -1, angle: null },
    { length: arc, x: r, y: r, dx: 0, dy: 0, angle: Math.PI }
  ];
  return {
    r,
    segments,
    total: Math.max(
      1,
      segments.reduce((sum, segment) => sum + segment.length, 0)
    )
  };
};

const pointAt = (shape, distance, point) => {
  let s = wrap(distance, shape.total);
  for (const segment of shape.segments) {
    if (segment.length > 0 && s <= segment.length) {
      if (segment.angle === null) {
        point.x = segment.x + segment.dx * s;
        point.y = segment.y + segment.dy * s;
        point.nx = segment.dy;
        point.ny = -segment.dx;
      } else {
        const angle = segment.angle + s / shape.r;
        point.nx = Math.cos(angle);
        point.ny = Math.sin(angle);
        point.x = segment.x + point.nx * shape.r;
        point.y = segment.y + point.ny * shape.r;
      }
      return point;
    }
    s -= segment.length;
  }
  return point;
};

const project = (shape, px, py) => {
  let best = Infinity;
  let found = 0;
  let offset = 0;
  shape.segments.forEach(segment => {
    if (segment.length > 0) {
      let s = 0;
      let x = 0;
      let y = 0;
      if (segment.angle === null) {
        s = clamp((px - segment.x) * segment.dx + (py - segment.y) * segment.dy, 0, segment.length);
        x = segment.x + segment.dx * s;
        y = segment.y + segment.dy * s;
      } else {
        let angle = wrap(Math.atan2(py - segment.y, px - segment.x) - segment.angle, TAU);
        if (angle > Math.PI / 2) angle = angle > Math.PI * 1.25 ? 0 : Math.PI / 2;
        s = angle * shape.r;
        x = segment.x + Math.cos(segment.angle + angle) * shape.r;
        y = segment.y + Math.sin(segment.angle + angle) * shape.r;
      }
      const distance = (px - x) ** 2 + (py - y) ** 2;
      if (distance < best) {
        best = distance;
        found = offset + s;
      }
    }
    offset += segment.length;
  });
  return found;
};

const StarBorder = ({
  as: Component = 'button',
  children,
  className = '',
  style,
  color,
  trailColor,
  duration = 4,
  thickness = 1,
  radius = 12,
  trailLength = 0.3,
  stars = 1,
  glow = 0.6,
  sparkle = false,
  direction = 'clockwise',
  hover = 'lap',
  clickPulse = true,
  theme = 'dark',
  backgroundColor,
  textColor,
  borderColor,
  ...rest
}) => {
  const rootRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const wakeRef = useRef(null);
  const palette = THEMES[theme] ?? THEMES.dark;
  const light = color ?? palette.light;
  const settings = {
    light: theme === 'light',
    color: light,
    trailColor: trailColor ?? light,
    duration: Math.max(0.3, duration),
    thickness: Math.max(0.5, thickness),
    radius: Math.max(0, radius),
    trailLength: clamp(trailLength, 0, 1),
    stars: clamp(Math.round(stars), 1, 6),
    glow: clamp(glow, 0, 1),
    sparkle,
    direction: direction === 'counterclockwise' ? -1 : 1,
    hover: HOVERS.includes(hover) ? hover : 'lap',
    clickPulse
  };
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = rootRef.current;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const ctx = canvas?.getContext('2d');
    if (!root || !canvas || !ctx) return undefined;

    const reduce = window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ?? false;
    const point = { x: 0, y: 0, nx: 0, ny: -1 };
    const state = {
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      dpr: 1,
      phase: 0,
      velocity: 0,
      motion: 0,
      lap: -1,
      time: 0,
      reveal: 0,
      boost: 0,
      flash: 0,
      emit: 0,
      hovered: false,
      focused: false,
      pointerX: 0,
      pointerY: 0,
      visible: true
    };
    const pulses = [];
    const dust = [];
    const colors = { key: '', head: WHITE, tail: WHITE };
    let raf = 0;
    let last = performance.now();
    let alive = true;

    let resizePending = false;
    const applyMeasure = () => {
      state.width = root.offsetWidth;
      state.height = root.offsetHeight;
      state.dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round((state.width + BLEED * 2) * state.dpr));
      canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round((state.height + BLEED * 2) * state.dpr));
    };

    const measure = () => {
      resizePending = true;
      wake();
    };

    const local = event => {
      const rect = root.getBoundingClientRect();
      const scaleX = rect.width ? state.width / rect.width : 1;
      const scaleY = rect.height ? state.height / rect.height : 1;
      return [(event.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX, (event.clientY - rect.top) * scaleY];
    };

    const shapeFor = s =>
      outline(
        Math.max(1, state.width - s.thickness),
        Math.max(1, state.height - s.thickness),
        s.radius - s.thickness / 2
      );

    const stroke = (x1, y1, x2, y2, width, style) => {
      ctx.lineWidth = width;
      ctx.strokeStyle = style;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);
      ctx.stroke();
    };

    const drawTrail = (s, shape, head, length, sign, intensity, tip = true) => {
      if (length < 0.5 || intensity <= 0) return;
      const steps = clamp(Math.ceil(length / 2), 2, 90);
      pointAt(shape, head, point);
      let px = point.x;
      let py = point.y;
      for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
        pointAt(shape, head - sign * length * (i / steps), point);
        const u = 1 - (i - 0.5) / steps;
        const fade = u * u * intensity;
        let tone = mix(colors.tail, colors.head, u);
        if (!s.light) tone = mix(tone, WHITE, u ** 6 * 0.65);
        ctx.lineCap = tip && i === 1 ? 'round' : 'butt';
        stroke(px, py, point.x, point.y, 2 + 6 * s.glow, rgba(tone, fade * (s.light ? 0.1 : 0.2) * s.glow));
        stroke(px, py, point.x, point.y, s.thickness, rgba(tone, fade));
        px = point.x;
        py = point.y;
      }
    };

    const drawHead = (s, x, y, intensity, twinkle) => {
      if (intensity <= 0) return;
      const tone = colors.head;
      const reach = (12 + 40 * s.glow) * (s.light ? 0.6 : 1);
      const bloom = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, reach);
      const strength = (s.light ? 0.08 : 0.4) * s.glow * intensity;
      bloom.addColorStop(0, rgba(tone, strength));
      bloom.addColorStop(0.2, rgba(tone, strength * 0.4));
      bloom.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(tone, strength * 0.1));
      bloom.addColorStop(1, rgba(tone, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = bloom;
      ctx.fillRect(x - reach, y - reach, reach * 2, reach * 2);
      if (!s.sparkle) return;

      const size = (1 + s.thickness * 0.6) * (s.light ? 0.85 : 1);
      const core = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, size * 2.2);
      core.addColorStop(0, rgba(s.light ? tone : WHITE, intensity));
      core.addColorStop(s.light ? 0.3 : 0.4, rgba(s.light ? tone : mix(tone, WHITE, 0.5), intensity * 0.9));
      core.addColorStop(s.light ? 0.55 : 1, rgba(tone, s.light ? intensity * 0.12 : 0));
      core.addColorStop(1, rgba(tone, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = core;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(x, y, size * 2.2, 0, TAU);
      ctx.fill();

      const spike = (3 + 4 * s.glow + s.thickness * 1.5) * twinkle;
      const center = s.light ? tone : WHITE;
      const horizontal = ctx.createLinearGradient(x - spike, y, x + spike, y);
      horizontal.addColorStop(0, rgba(tone, 0));
      horizontal.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(center, intensity * 0.95));
      horizontal.addColorStop(1, rgba(tone, 0));
      stroke(x - spike, y, x + spike, y, s.light ? 0.75 : 1, horizontal);
      const vertical = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y - spike, x, y + spike);
      vertical.addColorStop(0, rgba(tone, 0));
      vertical.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(center, intensity * 0.95));
      vertical.addColorStop(1, rgba(tone, 0));
      stroke(x, y - spike, x, y + spike, s.light ? 0.75 : 1, vertical);
    };

    const spawnDust = (x, y, nx, ny, speed) => {
      if (dust.length > 48) return;
      const push = 3 + Math.random() * 10;
      const drift = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8;
      dust.push({
        x: x + nx * (Math.random() * 3 - 1),
        y: y + ny * (Math.random() * 3 - 1),
        vx: nx * push - ny * drift - speed * -ny * 0.04,
        vy: ny * push + nx * drift - speed * nx * 0.04,
        age: 0,
        life: 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.5,
        size: 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.7,
        seed: Math.random() * TAU
      });
    };

    const draw = (base, shape) => {
      const s = state.boost > 0.001 ? { ...base, glow: Math.min(1.5, base.glow + 0.4 * state.boost) } : base;
      ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      if (state.width < 2 || state.height < 2) return;
      const offset = (BLEED + s.thickness / 2) * state.dpr;
      ctx.setTransform(state.dpr, 0, 0, state.dpr, offset, offset);
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = s.light ? 'source-over' : 'lighter';
      ctx.lineCap = 'butt';

      const total = shape.total;
      const spacing = total / s.stars;
      const speed = Math.abs(state.motion);
      const sign = state.motion >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
      const span = s.trailLength * s.duration;
      const length = Math.min(speed * span, spacing * 0.92, total * 0.6);
      const intensity = state.reveal * (1 + 0.4 * state.boost + 0.6 * state.flash);

      const ring = state.flash * (s.light ? 0.18 : 0.32) + state.boost * state.reveal * (s.light ? 0.07 : 0.12);
      if (ring > 0.005) {
        ctx.lineWidth = s.thickness + 2 * s.glow;
        ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(colors.head, ring);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(
          0,
          0,
          Math.max(1, state.width - s.thickness),
          Math.max(1, state.height - s.thickness),
          Math.max(0, shape.r)
        );
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      const still = clamp(1 - speed / Math.max(1, (total / s.duration) * 0.6), 0, 1) * Math.min(1, intensity);
      const rest = (10 + 14 * s.glow + s.thickness * 2) * still;
      for (let i = 0; i < s.stars; i++) {
        const head = state.phase + i * spacing;
        drawTrail(s, shape, head, length, sign, Math.min(1, intensity));
        if (still > 0.01) {
          drawTrail(s, shape, head, rest, 1, still, false);
          drawTrail(s, shape, head, rest, -1, still, false);
        }
      }

      pulses.forEach(pulse => {
        const progress = pulse.age / 0.65;
        const fade = (1 - progress) ** 2;
        const travel = (total / 2) * (1 - (1 - Math.min(1, pulse.age / 0.5)) ** 2);
        const tail = Math.min(travel, total * 0.16);
        [1, -1].forEach(way => {
          const front = pulse.origin + way * travel;
          drawTrail(s, shape, front, tail, way, fade);
          pointAt(shape, front, point);
          drawHead(s, point.x, point.y, fade * 0.8, 0.8);
        });
      });

      for (let i = 0; i < s.stars; i++) {
        pointAt(shape, state.phase + i * spacing, point);
        const twinkle = 0.82 + 0.18 * Math.sin(state.time * 6.1 + i * 2.3) * Math.sin(state.time * 2.7 + i);
        drawHead(s, point.x, point.y, Math.min(1.3, intensity), twinkle);
      }

      dust.forEach(mote => {
        const left = 1 - mote.age / mote.life;
        const shimmer = 0.45 + 0.55 * Math.sin(state.time * 20 + mote.seed);
        const alpha = left * shimmer * state.reveal * (s.light ? 0.35 : 0.9);
        if (alpha <= 0.01) return;
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba(s.light ? colors.head : mix(colors.tail, WHITE, 0.6), alpha);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(mote.x, mote.y, mote.size * (0.6 + 0.4 * left), 0, TAU);
        ctx.fill();
      });
    };

    const frame = now => {
      raf = 0;
      if (resizePending) {
        resizePending = false;
        applyMeasure();
      }
      if (!alive) return;
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0.001, (now - last) / 1000));
      last = now;
      state.time += dt;

      const key = `${s.color}|${s.trailColor}`;
      if (key !== colors.key) {
        colors.key = key;
        colors.head = toRgb(s.color);
        colors.tail = toRgb(s.trailColor);
      }

      const shape = shapeFor(s);
      const total = shape.total;
      const spacing = total / s.stars;
      const engaged = state.hovered || state.focused;
      const cruise = reduce ? 0 : (s.direction * total) / s.duration;

      const revealTarget = s.hover === 'reveal' && !engaged ? 0 : 1;
      state.reveal += (revealTarget - state.reveal) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / (revealTarget > state.reveal ? 0.16 : 0.45)));
      const boostTarget = s.hover === 'brighten' && engaged ? 1 : 0;
      state.boost += (boostTarget - state.boost) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / (boostTarget > state.boost ? 0.18 : 0.5)));
      state.flash *= Math.exp(-dt / 0.22);

      state.velocity += (cruise - state.velocity) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.45));
      let extra = 0;
      if (state.lap >= 0) {
        const before = ease(Math.min(1, state.lap / LAP));
        state.lap += dt;
        extra = (ease(Math.min(1, state.lap / LAP)) - before) * total * s.direction;
        if (state.lap >= LAP) state.lap = -1;
      }
      state.motion = state.velocity + extra / dt;
      state.phase = wrap(state.phase + state.velocity * dt + extra, total);

      const pace = Math.abs(state.motion) / Math.max(1, Math.abs(total / s.duration));
      if (s.sparkle && !reduce && state.reveal > 0.05) {
        state.emit += dt * (2 + 6 * Math.min(2, pace)) * s.stars;
        while (state.emit > 1) {
          state.emit -= 1;
          pointAt(shape, state.phase + Math.floor(Math.random() * s.stars) * spacing, point);
          spawnDust(point.x, point.y, point.nx, point.ny, state.motion);
        }
      }
      for (let i = dust.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const mote = dust[i];
        mote.age += dt;
        mote.x += mote.vx * dt;
        mote.y += mote.vy * dt;
        mote.vx *= Math.exp(-dt * 2.2);
        mote.vy *= Math.exp(-dt * 2.2);
        if (mote.age >= mote.life) dust.splice(i, 1);
      }
      for (let i = pulses.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        pulses[i].age += dt;
        if (pulses[i].age >= 0.65) pulses.splice(i, 1);
      }

      draw(s, shape);

      const settling =
        Math.abs(revealTarget - state.reveal) > 0.002 ||
        Math.abs(boostTarget - state.boost) > 0.002 ||
        state.flash > 0.01 ||
        pulses.length > 0 ||
        dust.length > 0;
      const moving = Math.abs(state.motion) > 0.05 || cruise !== 0 || state.lap >= 0;
      const showing = state.reveal > 0.002 || revealTarget > 0;
      if (state.visible && (settling || (moving && showing) || state.hovered)) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };

    const wake = () => {
      if (raf || !alive || !state.visible) return;
      last = performance.now();
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
    wakeRef.current = wake;

    const onEnter = event => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      [state.pointerX, state.pointerY] = local(event);
      state.hovered = true;
      if (s.hover === 'reveal' && state.reveal < 0.15) {
        state.phase = project(shapeFor(s), state.pointerX - s.thickness / 2, state.pointerY - s.thickness / 2);
        state.velocity = 0;
      }
      if (s.hover === 'lap' && state.lap < 0 && !reduce) state.lap = 0;
      wake();
    };
    const onLeave = () => {
      state.hovered = false;
      wake();
    };
    const pulse = origin => {
      if (!settingsRef.current.clickPulse) return;
      pulses.push({ origin, age: 0 });
      if (pulses.length > 4) pulses.shift();
      state.flash = 1;
      wake();
    };
    const onDown = event => {
      if (event.button !== 0 && event.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const [x, y] = local(event);
      pulse(project(shapeFor(s), x - s.thickness / 2, y - s.thickness / 2));
    };
    const onClick = event => {
      if (event.detail === 0) pulse(state.phase);
    };
    const onFocus = () => {
      state.focused = root.matches(':focus-visible');
      if (state.focused && settingsRef.current.hover === 'lap' && state.lap < 0 && !reduce) state.lap = 0;
      wake();
    };
    const onBlur = () => {
      state.focused = false;
      wake();
    };

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(measure);
    resizeObserver.observe(root);
    const visibility = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      state.visible = entries.some(entry => entry.isIntersecting);
      if (state.visible) wake();
    });
    visibility.observe(root);
    root.addEventListener('pointerenter', onEnter);
    root.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    root.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
    root.addEventListener('click', onClick);
    root.addEventListener('focus', onFocus);
    root.addEventListener('blur', onBlur);
    measure();

    return () => {
      alive = false;
      wakeRef.current = null;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      visibility.disconnect();
      root.removeEventListener('pointerenter', onEnter);
      root.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
      root.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
      root.removeEventListener('click', onClick);
      root.removeEventListener('focus', onFocus);
      root.removeEventListener('blur', onBlur);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    wakeRef.current?.();
  });

  return (
    <Component
      ref={rootRef}
      className={`star-border${className ? ` ${className}` : ''}`}
      style={{
        '--star-border-radius': `${settings.radius}px`,
        '--star-border-thickness': `${settings.thickness}px`,
        '--star-border-surface': backgroundColor ?? palette.surface,
        '--star-border-text': textColor ?? palette.text,
        '--star-border-track': borderColor ?? palette.track,
        ...style
      }}
      {...(Component === 'button' && rest.type === undefined ? { type: 'button' } : null)}
      {...rest}
    >
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="star-border__light" aria-hidden="true" />
      {children}
    </Component>
  );
};

export default StarBorder;

"use client";

import { useEffect, useId, useRef } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";

/**
 * Accessible sheet/dialog: focus moves in, Tab is trapped, Esc closes,
 * focus returns to the trigger. Used by the trust sheet and the check-in form.
 */
export function BottomSheet({
  title,
  subtitle,
  onClose,
  children,
  className = "",
}: {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const titleId = useId();
  const previousFocus = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    previousFocus.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    const focusable = panel?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
      'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
    );
    focusable?.focus();

    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.stopPropagation();
        onClose();
        return;
      }
      if (event.key !== "Tab" || !panel) return;
      const items = Array.from(
        panel.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
          'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
        ),
      ).filter((element) => element.offsetParent !== null);
      if (items.length === 0) return;
      const first = items[0];
      const last = items[items.length - 1];
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
      previousFocus.current?.focus();
    };
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div className="sheet-backdrop" onClick={onClose}>
      <div
        className={`sheet ${className}`}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        ref={panelRef}
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <header className="sheet-header">
          <div>
            <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>
            {subtitle && <p>{subtitle}</p>}
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="icon-button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close"
          >
            <X aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </header>
        <div className="sheet-body">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildConcerns } from "@/lib/concerns/build";
import type { CheckIn, Observation } from "@/lib/types";

const now = new Date("2026-11-14T12:00:00.000Z");
const hoursAgo = (hours: number) =>
  new Date(now.getTime() - hours * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();

function observation(
  id: string,
  metric: Observation["metric"],
  value: number,
  hours: number,
  method: string,
): Observation {
  return {
    id,
    crewId: "alex-morgan",
    metric,
    value,
    unit: metric === "sleep_minutes" ? "min" : "bpm",
    observedAt: hoursAgo(hours),
    enteredAt: hoursAgo(hours),
    lane: "manual",
    method,
    context: "rest",
    quality: "ok",
    qualityReason: null,
    environment: "mars_transit",
    procedureVersion: "demo-procedures-1",
  };
}

function checkIn(overrides: Partial<CheckIn> = {}): CheckIn {
  return {
    id: "ci-1",
    crewId: "alex-morgan",
    observedAt: hoursAgo(5),
    enteredAt: hoursAgo(5),
    sleepMinutes: 300,
    mood: 3,
    stress: 3,
    rested: 3,
    connected: 4,
    symptoms: [],
    details: "",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("concern builder", () => {
  it("groups co-occurring signals without assigning a cause", () => {
    const observations: Observation[] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < 5; index += 1) {
      observations.push(
        observation(`hr-${index}`, "heart_rate", 64 + index, 48 - index, "ppg_finger"),
        observation(`sl-${index}`, "sleep_minutes", 420, 50 - index, "self_report"),
      );
    }
    observations.push(
      observation("hr-latest", "heart_rate", 92, 2, "ppg_finger"),
      observation("sl-latest", "sleep_minutes", 250, 3, "self_report"),
    );

    const concerns = buildConcerns(observations, [], now);
    expect(concerns).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(concerns[0].summary).toContain("does not establish a cause");
    expect(concerns[0].signals.length).toBe(2);
    expect(concerns[0].hazards.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(JSON.stringify(concerns[0])).not.toMatch(/healthy|diagnos/i);
  });

  it("creates a concern from a worsening symptom with no numbers at all", () => {
    const concerns = buildConcerns(
      [],
      [
        checkIn({
          symptoms: [
            { id: "s1", name: "Dizziness", onset: "today", trend: "worse", urgent: false },
          ],
        }),
      ],
      now,
    );
    expect(concerns).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(concerns[0].title).toContain("symptoms");
  });

  it("ignores signals older than 72 hours", () => {
    const observations: Observation[] = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < 5; index += 1) {
      observations.push(
        observation(`hr-${index}`, "heart_rate", 64 + index, 200 + index, "ppg_finger"),
      );
    }
    observations.push(observation("hr-latest", "heart_rate", 92, 120, "ppg_finger"));
    expect(buildConcerns(observations, [], now)).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

 FILE: frontend/lib/concerns/build.ts
 lines: 143

import type {
  CheckIn,
  Concern,
  HazardTag,
  Metric,
  Observation,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { gate } from "@/lib/quality/gate";
import { comparableHistory } from "@/lib/status/compare";
import { metricMeta } from "@/lib/metrics";

const WINDOW_HOURS = 72;

const hazardsForMetric: Partial<Record<Metric, HazardTag[]>> = {
  heart_rate: ["gravity", "distance"],
  bp_systolic: ["gravity", "distance"],
  bp_diastolic: ["gravity", "distance"],
  sleep_minutes: ["isolation", "environment"],
  mood: ["isolation"],
  stress: ["isolation", "environment"],
  rested: ["isolation", "environment"],
  connected: ["isolation", "distance"],
  skin_temp: ["environment", "radiation"],
  glucose: ["distance"],
  motion: ["gravity"],
};

function withinWindow(iso: string, now: Date): boolean {
  const time = new Date(iso).getTime();
  if (!Number.isFinite(time)) return false;
  return now.getTime() - time <= WINDOW_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}

/** A value is flagged only against this person's own comparable history. */
function differsFromOwnRange(
  observation: Observation,
  history: Observation[],
  now: Date,
): boolean {
  // Historical trend work asks "was this value usable when recorded?", not
  // "is it a current reading?", so staleness is ignored here.
  if (!gate(observation, now, { ignoreStaleness: true }).accepted) return false;
  const comparable = comparableHistory(observation, history);
  if (comparable.length < 5) return false;
  const values = comparable.map((entry) => entry.value as number);
  const tolerance = { heart_rate: 6, sleep_minutes: 60, stress: 1 } as Record<
    Metric,
    number
  >;
  const low = Math.min(...values) - tolerance[observation.metric];
  const high = Math.max(...values) + tolerance[observation.metric];
  return (observation.value as number) < low || (observation.value as number) > high;
}

function latestPerMetric(observations: Observation[], metric: Metric): Observation[] {
  return [...observations]
    .filter((entry) => entry.metric === metric)
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        new Date(b.observedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.observedAt).getTime(),
    );
}

export function buildConcerns(
  observations: Observation[],
  checkIns: CheckIn[],
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Concern[] {
  const concerns: Concern[] = [];
  const inWindow = observations.filter((entry) => withinWindow(entry.observedAt, now));

  const signals: Array<{ observation: Observation; text: string }> = [];
  for (const metric of ["heart_rate", "sleep_minutes", "stress"] as Metric[]) {
    const candidates = latestPerMetric(inWindow, metric);
    const latest = candidates[0];
    if (!latest) continue;
    if (differsFromOwnRange(latest, inWindow, now)) {
      signals.push({
        observation: latest,
        text: `${metricMeta[metric].label} differs from your recent readings`,
      });
    }
  }

  // A symptom with a worsening trend is its own concern, never a reassuring number.
  const symptomSignals = checkIns
    .filter((checkIn) => withinWindow(checkIn.observedAt, now))
    .flatMap((checkIn) =>
      checkIn.symptoms
        .filter((symptom) => symptom.trend === "worse" || symptom.urgent)
        .map((symptom) => ({ checkIn, symptom })),
    );

  if (signals.length >= 2) {
    const metrics = signals.map((signal) => signal.observation.metric);
    const hazardSet = new Set<HazardTag>();
    for (const metric of metrics) {
      for (const hazard of hazardsForMetric[metric] ?? []) hazardSet.add(hazard);
    }
    concerns.push({
      id: `concern-${metrics.sort().join("-")}`,
      crewId: signals[0].observation.crewId,
      createdAt: now.toISOString(),
      title: "These signals changed together",
      summary: "These changed in the same 72 hours. This does not establish a cause.",
      signals: signals.map((signal) => ({
        metric: signal.observation.metric,
        text: signal.text,
        observedAt: signal.observation.observedAt,
      })),
      hazards: [...hazardSet],
      windowHours: WINDOW_HOURS,
    });
  }

  if (symptomSignals.length > 0) {
    const names = [...new Set(symptomSignals.map((entry) => entry.symptom.name))];
    const hazardSet = new Set<HazardTag>(["isolation", "environment"]);
    if (names.some((name) => /dizzi|vision|chest/i.test(name))) {
      hazardSet.add("gravity");
      hazardSet.add("distance");
    }
    concerns.push({
      id: `concern-symptom-${names.sort().join("-").toLowerCase().replace(/\W+/g, "-")}`,
      crewId: symptomSignals[0].checkIn.crewId,
      createdAt: now.toISOString(),
      title: "Reported symptoms need assessment",
      summary:
        "A reported symptom outranks a reassuring number. This does not establish a cause.",
      signals: symptomSignals.map(({ checkIn, symptom }) => ({
        metric: "symptom",
        text: `${symptom.name} — ${
          symptom.onset === "several_days" ? "several days" : symptom.onset === "today" ? "today" : "just now"
        }, ${symptom.trend}`,
        observedAt: checkIn.observedAt,
      })),
      hazards: [...hazardSet],
      windowHours: WINDOW_HOURS,
    });
  }

  return concerns;
}

import { missionConfig } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import type { Observation } from "@/lib/types";

/**
 * Demo history so personal comparisons have something honest to compare with.
 * Every seeded row carries a lane. These are scenario values, not real vitals.
 */
function base(crewId: string, metric: Observation["metric"], unit: string) {
  return {
    crewId,
    metric,
    unit,
    lane: "live" as const,
    quality: "ok" as const,
    qualityReason: null,
    environment: "mars_transit",
    procedureVersion: "demo-seed-1",
  };
}

export function seedObservations(now: Date = new Date()): Observation[] {
  const rows: Observation[] = [];

  for (const member of missionConfig.crew) {
    const drift = member.id === "alex-morgan";
    const seedSleepDrop = member.id === "samira-patel";

    for (let day = 13; day >= 0; day -= 1) {
      const date = new Date(now);
      date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() - day);
      date.setUTCHours(7, 30, 0, 0);
      const iso = date.toISOString();
      const wave = Math.sin((day / 13) * Math.PI * 2) * 2;
      const rising = drift && day <= 2 ? 8 - day : 0;

      const heartRate = Math.round(member.restingHeartRate + wave + rising);
      const sleepBase = seedSleepDrop && day <= 2 ? 290 - (2 - day) * 15 : 430 + wave * 8;
      const stress = drift && day <= 2 ? 4 : 2 + (day % 3 === 0 ? 1 : 0);
      const moodValue = drift && day <= 2 ? 2 : 4;
      const skinTemp = Number((33.9 + wave * 0.02).toFixed(1));
      const systolic = Math.round(116 + wave);
      const diastolic = Math.round(75 + wave / 2);

      rows.push(
        {
          ...base(member.id, "heart_rate", "bpm"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-hr-${day}`,
          value: heartRate,
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          method: "ppg_wrist",
          context: "rest",
        },
        {
          ...base(member.id, "skin_temp", "°C"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-temp-${day}`,
          value: skinTemp,
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          method: "thermometer_skin",
          context: "rest",
        },
        {
          ...base(member.id, "sleep_minutes", "min"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-sleep-${day}`,
          value: Math.round(sleepBase),
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "self_report",
          context: "unknown",
        },
        {
          ...base(member.id, "stress", "/5"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-stress-${day}`,
          value: stress,
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "self_report",
          context: "unknown",
        },
        {
          ...base(member.id, "mood", "/5"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-mood-${day}`,
          value: moodValue,
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "self_report",
          context: "unknown",
        },
      );

      // Cuff and glucose readings exist only for some days, so history is
      // genuinely uneven and the app has to say "insufficient" sometimes.
      if (day % 2 === 0) {
        rows.push({
          ...base(member.id, "bp_systolic", "mmHg"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-sys-${day}`,
          value: systolic,
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "cuff_upper_arm",
          context: "rest",
        });
        rows.push({
          ...base(member.id, "bp_diastolic", "mmHg"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-dia-${day}`,
          value: diastolic,
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "cuff_upper_arm",
          context: "rest",
        });
      }
      if (day % 3 === 0) {
        rows.push({
          ...base(member.id, "glucose", "mmol/L"),
          id: `seed-${member.id}-glucose-${day}`,
          value: Number((5.2 + wave * 0.1).toFixed(1)),
          observedAt: iso,
          enteredAt: iso,
          lane: "manual",
          method: "glucometer",
          context: "post_meal",
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return rows;
}

import type { Encounter, Observation, SymptomEntry } from "@/lib/types";
import type { RunnerState } from "@/lib/pathways/runner";

export function buildEncounter(
  state: RunnerState,
  crewId: string,
  symptoms: SymptomEntry[],
  endedAt: string = new Date().toISOString(),
): Encounter {
  return {
    id: `enc-${state.pathwayId}-${Date.parse(state.startedAt) || Date.now()}`,
    crewId,
    kind: state.pathwayId.startsWith("demo-") ? "emergency" : "assessment",
    pathwayId: state.pathwayId,
    startedAt: state.startedAt,
    endedAt: state.mode === "done" || state.mode === "emergency" ? endedAt : null,
    symptoms,
    answers: state.answers,
    measurements: state.measurements.filter((entry) => entry.value !== null),
    actions: state.actions,
    unknowns: state.unknowns,
    outcome: state.outcome,
    illustrative: true,
  };
}

export function emptyEncounterObservations(encounter: Encounter): Observation[] {
  return encounter.measurements;
}

 FILE: frontend/lib/handoff/build.ts
 lines: 111

import type {
  CheckIn,
  Encounter,
  Handoff,
  Metric,
  Observation,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { metricMeta } from "@/lib/metrics";
import { statusFor } from "@/lib/observations/derived";

const privateMetrics: Metric[] = ["mood", "stress", "rested", "connected"];

export const publicMetrics: Metric[] = [
  "heart_rate",
  "bp_systolic",
  "bp_diastolic",
  "skin_temp",
  "glucose",
  "sleep_minutes",
  "motion",
];

export function buildHandoff(input: {
  crewId: string;
  encounter: Encounter | null;
  checkIn: CheckIn | null;
  observations: Observation[];
  includePrivate: boolean;
  notes: string;
  now?: Date;
}): Handoff {
  const now = input.now ?? new Date();
  const crewObservations = input.observations.filter(
    (entry) => entry.crewId === input.crewId,
  );
  const allowed = input.includePrivate
    ? [...publicMetrics, ...privateMetrics]
    : publicMetrics;

  const recent = crewObservations.filter((entry) => {
    const time = new Date(entry.observedAt).getTime();
    return (
      allowed.includes(entry.metric) &&
      Number.isFinite(time) &&
      now.getTime() - time <= 72 * 60 * 60 * 1000
    );
  });

  const trends = publicMetrics
    .filter((metric) => allowed.includes(metric))
    .map((metric) => {
      const comparison = statusFor(crewObservations, input.crewId, metric, now);
      if (comparison.comparableCount === 0) return null;
      return {
        metric,
        text: `${metricMeta[metric].label}: ${comparison.label.toLowerCase()}. ${comparison.detail}`,
      };
    })
    .filter((entry): entry is { metric: Metric; text: string } => entry !== null);

  return {
    id: `handoff-${input.crewId}-${Date.now()}`,
    crewId: input.crewId,
    createdAt: now.toISOString(),
    encounterId: input.encounter?.id ?? null,
    symptoms: input.encounter?.symptoms ?? input.checkIn?.symptoms ?? [],
    observations: recent,
    trends,
    actions: input.encounter?.actions ?? [],
    unknowns: input.encounter?.unknowns ?? [],
    includePrivate: input.includePrivate,
    notes: input.notes,
    recipients: [
      "Flight surgeon, Earth",
      "Crew medical officer, on board",
    ],
  };
}

/** Summary derived from the data — never static prose. */
export function summariseHandoff(handoff: Handoff): string[] {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push(
    `${handoff.observations.length} observations in the last 72 hours across ${new Set(
      handoff.observations.map((entry) => entry.metric),
    ).size} readings.`,
  );
  if (handoff.symptoms.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      `Reported symptoms: ${handoff.symptoms
        .map((symptom) => `${symptom.name} (${symptom.onset}, ${symptom.trend})`)
        .join("; ")}.`,
    );
  } else {
    lines.push("No symptoms were reported in this packet.");
  }
  if (handoff.unknowns.length > 0) {
    lines.push(`${handoff.unknowns.length} items could not be assessed or measured.`);
  }
  const unreviewed = handoff.observations.filter((entry) => entry.quality !== "ok");
  if (unreviewed.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      `${unreviewed.length} readings carry a quality flag and are not used in comparisons.`,
    );
  }
  if (handoff.trends.length > 0) {
    lines.push(handoff.trends[0].text);
  }
  lines.push("No cause is assigned and no diagnosis is included in this packet.");
  return lines;
}

import type { Metric } from "@/lib/types";

export const metricMeta: Record<
  Metric,
  { label: string; unit: string; precision: number; private: boolean }
> = {
  heart_rate: { label: "Heart rate", unit: "bpm", precision: 0, private: false },
  skin_temp: { label: "Skin temperature", unit: "°C", precision: 1, private: false },
  bp_systolic: { label: "Systolic pressure", unit: "mmHg", precision: 0, private: false },
  bp_diastolic: { label: "Diastolic pressure", unit: "mmHg", precision: 0, private: false },
  glucose: { label: "Blood sugar", unit: "mmol/L", precision: 1, private: false },
  sleep_minutes: { label: "Sleep", unit: "min", precision: 0, private: false },
  mood: { label: "Mood", unit: "/5", precision: 0, private: true },
  stress: { label: "Stress", unit: "/5", precision: 0, private: true },
  rested: { label: "Rested", unit: "/5", precision: 0, private: true },
  connected: { label: "Connected", unit: "/5", precision: 0, private: true },
  motion: { label: "Motion", unit: "g", precision: 2, private: false },
};

  post_exercise: "After exercise",

  unknown: "Context not recorded",
};

export function formatValue(metric: Metric, value: number | null): string {
  if (value === null || Number.isNaN(value)) return "—";
  const meta = metricMeta[metric];
  return `${value.toFixed(meta.precision)} ${meta.unit}`;
}

import type {
  BodyArea,
  CheckIn,
  Comparison,
  Metric,
  Observation,
  Quality,
  StatusState,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { compareObservation } from "@/lib/status/compare";
import { gate } from "@/lib/quality/gate";
import { metricMeta } from "@/lib/metrics";

export const areaMetrics: Partial<Record<BodyArea, Metric[]>> = {
  Head: ["mood", "sleep_minutes"],
  Heart: ["heart_rate"],
  Arm: ["bp_systolic", "bp_diastolic"],
  Skin: ["skin_temp"],
  Abdomen: ["glucose"],
  Legs: ["motion"],
  Radiation: [],
};

const metricAreas: Record<Metric, BodyArea> = {
  heart_rate: "Heart",
  skin_temp: "Skin",
  bp_systolic: "Arm",
  bp_diastolic: "Arm",
  glucose: "Abdomen",
  sleep_minutes: "Head",
  mood: "Head",
  stress: "Head",
  rested: "Head",
  connected: "Head",
  motion: "Legs",
};

export function areaForMetric(metric: Metric): BodyArea {
  return metricAreas[metric];
}

export function forCrew(
  observations: Observation[],
  crewId: string,
  metric?: Metric,
): Observation[] {
  return observations
    .filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId && (!metric || entry.metric === metric))
    .sort(
      (a, b) => new Date(a.observedAt).getTime() - new Date(b.observedAt).getTime(),
    );
}

export function latest(observations: Observation[], metric?: Metric): Observation | null {
  const filtered = metric
    ? observations.filter((entry) => entry.metric === metric)
    : [...observations];
  filtered.sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.observedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.observedAt).getTime(),
  );
  return filtered[0] ?? null;
}

export function statusFor(
  observations: Observation[],
  crewId: string,
  metric: Metric,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Comparison {
  const history = forCrew(observations, crewId, metric);
  const newest = latest(history);
  if (!newest) {
    return {
      state: "no_valid_reading",
      label: "No valid reading",
      detail: "Nothing recorded for this reading yet.",
      comparableCount: 0,
      range: null,
    };
  }
  return compareObservation(newest, history, now);
}

export function statusByArea(
  observations: Observation[],
  crewId: string,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Partial<Record<BodyArea, StatusState>> {
  const result: Partial<Record<BodyArea, StatusState>> = {};
  for (const [area, metrics] of Object.entries(areaMetrics) as Array<
    [BodyArea, Metric[]]
  >) {
    if (metrics.length === 0) continue;
    const states = metrics.map((metric) => statusFor(observations, crewId, metric, now).state);
    const priority: StatusState[] = [
      "attention",
      "no_valid_reading",
      "insufficient_history",
      "within_range",
      "logged",
    ];
    result[area] = priority.find((state) => states.includes(state)) ?? "insufficient_history";
  }
  return result;
}

export function lastRecordedText(observation: Observation | null): string {
  if (!observation) return "Nothing recorded yet.";
  const meta = metricMeta[observation.metric];
  const time = new Date(observation.observedAt).toLocaleTimeString("en-GB", {
    timeZone: "UTC",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
  const value =
    observation.value === null ? "not measured" : `${observation.value} ${meta.unit}`;
  return `Last recorded: ${value}, ${time} UTC. Not a current measurement.`;
}

export function chartPoints(observations: Observation[]) {
  return observations.map((entry) => ({
    value: entry.value,
    at: entry.observedAt,
    lane: entry.lane,
    quality: entry.quality,
  }));
}

export function dailyTrend(observations: Observation[], metric: Metric) {
  const byDay = new Map<string, Observation>();
  for (const entry of observations) {
    if (entry.metric !== metric) continue;
    const day = entry.observedAt.slice(0, 10);
    const existing = byDay.get(day);
    if (!existing || entry.observedAt > existing.observedAt) byDay.set(day, entry);
  }
  return [...byDay.entries()]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : 1))
    .map(([, entry]) => ({
      value: entry.value,
      at: entry.observedAt,
      lane: entry.lane,
      quality: entry.quality,
    }));
}

let sequence = 0;

export function makeObservation(input: {
  crewId: string;
  metric: Metric;
  value: number | null;
  observedAt: string;
  enteredAt?: string;
  lane: Observation["lane"];
  method: string;
  context: Observation["context"];
  unit?: string;
  artifact?: "motion" | null;
  quality?: Quality;
  environment?: string;
}): Observation {
  const draft: Observation = {
    id: `obs-${Date.now()}-${(sequence += 1)}`,
    crewId: input.crewId,
    metric: input.metric,
    value: input.value,
    unit: input.unit ?? metricMeta[input.metric].unit,
    observedAt: input.observedAt,
    enteredAt: input.enteredAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
    lane: input.lane,
    method: input.method,
    context: input.context,
    quality: input.quality ?? "ok",
    qualityReason: null,
    environment: input.environment ?? "mars_transit",
    procedureVersion: "demo-procedures-1",
    artifact: input.artifact ?? null,
  };
  const result = gate(draft);
  return { ...draft, quality: result.quality, qualityReason: result.qualityReason };
}

/** Check-in answers become manual observations, one per metric. */
export function observationsFromCheckIn(checkIn: CheckIn): Observation[] {
  const rows: Observation[] = [];
  const push = (
    metric: Metric,
    value: number | null,
    method = "self_report",
    context: Observation["context"] = "unknown",
  ) => {
    if (value === null || Number.isNaN(value)) return;
    rows.push(
      makeObservation({
        crewId: checkIn.crewId,
        metric,
        value,
        observedAt: checkIn.observedAt,
        enteredAt: checkIn.enteredAt,
        lane: "manual",
        method,
        context,
      }),
    );
  };

  push("sleep_minutes", checkIn.sleepMinutes);
  push("mood", checkIn.mood);
  push("stress", checkIn.stress);
  push("rested", checkIn.rested);
  push("connected", checkIn.connected);
  return rows;
}

 FILE: frontend/lib/outbox/http-transport.ts

import type {
  EnvironmentKey,
  LinkState,
  Observation,
  OutboxItem,
  OutboxState,
} from "@/lib/types";
import type { SensorAdapter } from "@/lib/sensors/adapter";

/**
 * Optional HTTP transport. When NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL is unset every export here
 * is inert and the app keeps behaving exactly as the offline demo.
 */
export const apiBase = (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
export const apiConfigured = apiBase.length > 0;

const crewKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CREW_KEY ?? "";
const groundKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GROUND_KEY ?? "";

export type ServerPacket = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  kind: string;
  title: string;
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  private: boolean;
  includePrivate: boolean;
  state: OutboxState;
  createdAt: string;
  sentAt: string | null;
  visibleAt: string | null;
  deliveredAt: string | null;
  ackAt: string | null;
  ackVisibleAt: string | null;
  delaySeconds: number;
  history: Array<{ state: OutboxState; at: string; note: string }>;
};

export type ApiFailure = {
  ok: false;
  code: string;
  message: string;
  status: number;
};

export type ApiResult<T> = ({ ok: true } & T) | ApiFailure;

async function request<T>(
  path: string,
  init: RequestInit = {},
  key: string = crewKey,
): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
  if (!apiConfigured) {
    return { ok: false, code: "NOT_CONFIGURED", message: "No API URL configured.", status: 0 };
  }
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}${path}`, {
      ...init,
      headers: {
        authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,
        "content-type": "application/json",
        ...(init.headers ?? {}),
      },
    });
    const text = await response.text();
    const body: unknown = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
    if (!response.ok) {
      const error = (body as { error?: { code?: string; message?: string } }).error;
      return {
        ok: false,
        code: error?.code ?? "HTTP_ERROR",
        message: error?.message ?? `Request failed with ${response.status}.`,
        status: response.status,
      };
    }
    return { ok: true, ...(body as T) };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      code: "UNREACHABLE",
      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Server unreachable.",
      status: 0,
    };
  }
}

export function packetToOutbox(packet: ServerPacket): OutboxItem {
  return {
    id: packet.id,
    crewId: packet.crewId,
    kind: packet.kind as OutboxItem["kind"],
    createdAt: packet.createdAt,
    sentAt: packet.sentAt,
    acknowledgedAt: packet.ackAt,
    state: packet.state,
    delaySeconds: packet.delaySeconds,
    title: packet.title,
    payload: packet.payload as OutboxItem["payload"],
    private: packet.private,
    history: packet.history.map((entry) => ({
      state: entry.state,
      at: entry.at,
      note: entry.note,
    })),
  };
}

export async function pingServer() {
  return request<{ ok: boolean; time: string; link: LinkView }>("/health", {}, "");
}

export type LinkView = {
  state: LinkState;
  environment: EnvironmentKey;
  scenarioDelaySeconds: number;
  effectiveDelaySeconds: number;
  delayScale: number;
};

export async function fetchLink() {
  return request<{ link: LinkView; serverTime: string }>("/v1/link");
}

export async function sendLink(state: LinkState, environment: EnvironmentKey) {
  return request<{ link: LinkView; serverTime: string }>("/v1/link", {
    method: "PUT",
    body: JSON.stringify({ state, environment }),
  });
}

export async function postPacket(item: OutboxItem) {
  const payload = item.payload as { includePrivate?: boolean } | null;
  return request<{ packet: ServerPacket; serverTime: string; created: boolean }>("/v1/packets", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({
      ...item,
      includePrivate: payload?.includePrivate ?? false,
    }),
  });
}

export async function fetchPackets(crewId: string) {
  return request<{ packets: ServerPacket[]; serverTime: string }>(
    `/v1/packets?crewId=${encodeURIComponent(crewId)}`,
  );
}

export async function fetchPacket(id: string, crewId: string) {
  return request<{ packet: ServerPacket; serverTime: string }>(
    `/v1/packets/${encodeURIComponent(id)}?crewId=${encodeURIComponent(crewId)}`,
  );
}

export async function fetchSurgeonInbox() {
  return request<{ packets: ServerPacket[]; serverTime: string }>(
    "/v1/surgeon/inbox",
    {},
    groundKey,
  );
}

export async function acknowledgeOnServer(id: string) {
  return request<{ packet: ServerPacket; serverTime: string }>(
    `/v1/surgeon/packets/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/ack`,
    { method: "POST", body: "{}" },
    groundKey,
  );
}

export type StreamHandlers = {
  onDelivered?: (packet: ServerPacket) => void;
  onAcknowledged?: (packet: ServerPacket) => void;
  onSample?: (observation: Observation) => void;
  onLink?: (link: LinkView) => void;
  onError?: () => void;
};

/** Same-origin replay of the server stream. Returns a disconnect function. */
export function connectStream(handlers: StreamHandlers): () => void {
  if (!apiConfigured || typeof EventSource === "undefined") return () => undefined;
  const source = new EventSource(
    `${apiBase}/v1/stream?token=${encodeURIComponent(crewKey)}`,
  );
  const parse = (event: MessageEvent) => JSON.parse(event.data) as unknown;

  source.addEventListener("packet.delivered", (event) => {
    handlers.onDelivered?.(parse(event as MessageEvent) as ServerPacket);
  });
  source.addEventListener("packet.acknowledged", (event) => {
    handlers.onAcknowledged?.(parse(event as MessageEvent) as ServerPacket);
  });
  source.addEventListener("sample", (event) => {
    handlers.onSample?.(parse(event as MessageEvent) as Observation);
  });
  source.addEventListener("link.changed", (event) => {
    handlers.onLink?.(parse(event as MessageEvent) as LinkView);
  });
  source.onerror = () => handlers.onError?.();
  return () => source.close();
}

/**
 * Device stream behind the same SensorAdapter interface. The server decides
 * quality; this adapter only forwards samples.
 */
export class RemoteSensorAdapter implements SensorAdapter {
  readonly name = "Remote sensor (server SSE)";
  readonly available = apiConfigured;
  private disconnectStream: (() => void) | null = null;
  private sampleListeners = new Set<(sample: Observation) => void>();
  private qualityListeners = new Set<(quality: Observation["quality"]) => void>();

  async connect() {
    if (!apiConfigured) {
      throw new Error("Set NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL to use the remote sensor.");
    }
    this.disconnectStream = connectStream({
      onSample: (sample) => {
        for (const listener of this.sampleListeners) listener(sample);
        for (const listener of this.qualityListeners) listener(sample.quality);
      },
      onError: () => {
        for (const listener of this.qualityListeners) listener("no_contact");
      },
    });
  }

  disconnect() {
    this.disconnectStream?.();
    this.disconnectStream = null;
    this.sampleListeners.clear();
    this.qualityListeners.clear();
  }

  onSample(listener: (sample: Observation) => void) {
    this.sampleListeners.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this.sampleListeners.delete(listener);
    };
  }

  onQuality(listener: (quality: Observation["quality"]) => void) {
    this.qualityListeners.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this.qualityListeners.delete(listener);
    };
  }
}

import lightheaded from "@/lib/pathways/lightheaded-v1.json";
import responsive from "@/lib/pathways/demo-responsive.json";
import unresponsive from "@/lib/pathways/demo-unresponsive.json";
import unsure from "@/lib/pathways/demo-unsure.json";
import type { Pathway } from "@/lib/types";

export const pathways = [
  lightheaded,
  responsive,
  unresponsive,
  unsure,
] as unknown as Pathway[];

export function pathwayById(id: string): Pathway | null {
  return pathways.find((pathway) => pathway.id === id) ?? null;
}

export function emergencyPathway(answer: "Yes" | "No" | "Unsure"): Pathway {
  if (answer === "Yes") return pathwayById("demo-responsive")!;
  if (answer === "No") return pathwayById("demo-unresponsive")!;
  return pathwayById("demo-unsure")!;
}

/** Choose an assessment pathway from reported symptoms, else a generic one. */
export function pathwayForSymptoms(symptoms: string[]): Pathway {
  const has = (value: string) => symptoms.some((name) => name.toLowerCase().includes(value));
  if (has("dizz") || has("tired") || has("nausea")) {
    return pathwayById("lightheaded-v1")!;
  }
  if (has("chest") || has("vision") || has("fainting")) {
    return pathwayById("demo-responsive")!;
  }
  return pathwayById("lightheaded-v1")!;
}
