import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { advance, optionsFor, reassessRemainingMs, startPathway } from "@/lib/pathways/runner";
import { pathwayById } from "@/lib/pathways";
import type { Observation } from "@/lib/types";
import { buildEncounter } from "@/lib/encounters/build";

const at = "2026-11-14T12:00:00.000Z";
const pathway = pathwayById("lightheaded-v1")!;

function measure(id: string): Observation {
  return {
    id,
    crewId: "alex-morgan",
    metric: "heart_rate",
    value: 72,
    unit: "bpm",
    observedAt: at,
    enteredAt: at,
    lane: "manual",
    method: "manual_entry",
    context: "rest",
    quality: "ok",
    qualityReason: null,
    environment: "mars_transit",
    procedureVersion: "demo-procedures-1",
  };
}

describe("pathway runner", () => {
  it("starts on the first step and offers an escape hatch on every question", () => {
    const state = startPathway(pathway, at);
    expect(state.stepId).toBe("s1");
    const step = pathway.steps[0];
    expect(optionsFor(step)).toContain("Unable to assess");
  });

  it("moves from a question to the urgent check", () => {
    const state = startPathway(pathway, at);
    const next = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "Position change", at });
    expect(next.stepId).toBe("s2");
    expect(next.answers[0].answer).toBe("Position change");
  });

  it("routes Yes on the urgent check straight to emergency", () => {
    let state = startPathway(pathway, at);
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "None of these", at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "Yes", at });
    expect(state.mode).toBe("emergency");
    expect(state.outcome).toBe("emergency");
  });

  it("treats 'Unable to assess' on the urgent check as unsafe", () => {
    let state = startPathway(pathway, at);
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "None of these", at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "Unable to assess", at });
    expect(state.mode).toBe("emergency");
    expect(state.incomplete).toBe(true);
  });

  it("routes to the alternative step when the cuff is unavailable", () => {
    let state = startPathway(pathway, at);
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "Meal", at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "No", at });
    expect(state.stepId).toBe("s3");
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "equipment_unavailable", at });
    expect(state.stepId).toBe("s3b");
    expect(state.incomplete).toBe(true);
    expect(state.unknowns[0]).toContain("equipment unavailable");
  });

  it("records a measurement and continues", () => {
    let state = startPathway(pathway, at);
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "Exercise", at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "No", at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "measurement", observation: measure("m1"), at });
    expect(state.stepId).toBe("s4");
    expect(state.measurements).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("schedules reassessment after the action timer", () => {
    let state = startPathway(pathway, at);
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "Exercise", at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "answer", option: "No", at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "measurement", observation: measure("m1"), at });
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "measurement", observation: measure("m2"), at });
    expect(state.stepId).toBe("s5");
    state = advance(state, pathway, { type: "action_complete", at });
    expect(state.mode).toBe("reassess");
    expect(state.reassessDueAt).toBe("2026-11-14T12:15:00.000Z");
    expect(reassessRemainingMs(state, new Date("2026-11-14T12:10:00.000Z"))).toBe(
      5 * 60 * 1000,
    );
  });

  it("closes on Better and escalates on Worse", () => {
    const reassess = {
      ...startPathway(pathway, at),
      mode: "reassess" as const,
      stepId: null,
      reassessDueAt: at,
    };
    expect(advance(reassess, pathway, { type: "reassess", option: "Better", at }).outcome).toBe(
      "closed",
    );
    expect(advance(reassess, pathway, { type: "reassess", option: "Worse", at }).outcome).toBe(
      "emergency",
    );
  });

  it("builds an encounter marked illustrative", () => {
    const encounter = buildEncounter(startPathway(pathway, at), "alex-morgan", [], at);
    expect(encounter.illustrative).toBe(true);
    expect(encounter.outcome).toBe("open");
  });
});

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/lib/pathways/runner.ts
 lines: 262
================================================================
import type {
  Encounter,
  EquipmentKey,
  Observation,
  Pathway,
  PathwayStep,
} from "@/lib/types";

/**
 * Generic, data-driven pathway runner. Content lives in JSON; this file only
 * moves between steps. Nothing here evaluates health or names a cause.
 */

export type RunnerMode = "step" | "reassess" | "done" | "emergency";

export type RunnerAnswer = {
  stepId: string;
  question: string;
  answer: string;
  at: string;
};

export type RunnerState = {
  pathwayId: string;
  mode: RunnerMode;
  stepId: string | null;
  answers: RunnerAnswer[];
  measurements: Observation[];
  actions: Array<{ text: string; at: string; timerMinutes?: number }>;
  unknowns: string[];
  incomplete: boolean;
  outcome: Encounter["outcome"];
  startedAt: string;
  reassessDueAt: string | null;
};

export type RunnerEvent =
  | { type: "answer"; option: string; at: string }
  | { type: "measurement"; observation: Observation | null; at: string }
  | { type: "equipment_unavailable"; at: string }
  | { type: "action_complete"; at: string }
  | { type: "reassess"; option: string; at: string };

export function pathwayStep(pathway: Pathway, id: string | null): PathwayStep | null {
  if (!id) return null;
  return pathway.steps.find((step) => step.id === id) ?? null;
}

function addMinutes(iso: string, minutes: number): string {
  return new Date(new Date(iso).getTime() + minutes * 60_000).toISOString();
}

function resolveTarget(
  map: Record<string, string> | undefined,
  option: string,
): string {
  if (!map) return "DONE";
  return map[option] ?? map.default ?? "DONE";
}

function moveTo(
  state: RunnerState,
  pathway: Pathway,
  target: string,
  at: string,
): RunnerState {
  switch (target) {
    case "EMERGENCY":
      return { ...state, mode: "emergency", outcome: "emergency", stepId: null };
    case "close":
    case "CLOSE":
      return { ...state, mode: "done", outcome: "closed", stepId: null };
    case "handoff":
    case "HANDOFF":
      return { ...state, mode: "done", outcome: "handoff", stepId: null };
    case "DONE":
      return {
        ...state,
        mode: "done",
        outcome: state.incomplete ? "incomplete" : "handoff",
        stepId: null,
      };
    case "reassess":
    case "REASSESS": {
      const minutes = pathway.reassess?.after_minutes ?? 15;
      return {
        ...state,
        mode: "reassess",
        stepId: null,
        reassessDueAt: addMinutes(at, minutes),
      };
    }
    default:
      return { ...state, mode: "step", stepId: target };
  }
}

export function startPathway(
  pathway: Pathway,
  at: string = new Date().toISOString(),
): RunnerState {
  const first = pathway.steps[0]?.id ?? null;
  return {
    pathwayId: pathway.id,
    mode: first ? "step" : "done",
    stepId: first,
    answers: [],
    measurements: [],
    actions: [],
    unknowns: [],
    incomplete: false,
    outcome: "open",
    startedAt: at,
    reassessDueAt: null,
  };
}

export function advance(
  state: RunnerState,
  pathway: Pathway,
  event: RunnerEvent,
): RunnerState {
  if (state.mode === "emergency" || state.mode === "done") return state;

  if (event.type === "reassess") {
    if (state.mode !== "reassess" || !pathway.reassess) return state;
    const target = resolveTarget(pathway.reassess.next, event.option);
    const next = moveTo(
      {
        ...state,
        answers: [
          ...state.answers,
          {
            stepId: "reassess",
            question: pathway.reassess.question,
            answer: event.option,
            at: event.at,
          },
        ],
      },
      pathway,
      target,
      event.at,
    );
    return next;
  }

  if (state.mode === "reassess") return state;

  const step = pathwayStep(pathway, state.stepId);
  if (!step) return state;

  if (event.type === "answer") {
    // "Unable to assess" can never replace the urgent screen, so it is
    // treated as "Unsure" there and as a recorded unknown elsewhere.
    const option =
      event.option === "Unable to assess" && step.type === "urgent_check"
        ? "Unsure"
        : event.option;
    const target =
      step.type === "action" ? "DONE" : resolveTarget(step.next, option);
    const withAnswer: RunnerState = {
      ...state,
      answers: [
        ...state.answers,
        {
          stepId: step.id,
          question: step.text,
          answer: event.option,
          at: event.at,
        },
      ],
      unknowns:
        event.option === "Unable to assess"
          ? [...state.unknowns, `${step.text} — unable to assess`]
          : state.unknowns,
      incomplete: state.incomplete || event.option === "Unable to assess",
    };
    return moveTo(withAnswer, pathway, target, event.at);
  }

  if (event.type === "measurement") {
    const nextState: RunnerState = {
      ...state,
      measurements: event.observation
        ? [...state.measurements, event.observation]
        : state.measurements,
      unknowns:
        event.observation === null
          ? [...state.unknowns, `${step.text} — not measured`]
          : state.unknowns,
      incomplete: state.incomplete || event.observation === null,
    };
    const target = event.observation
      ? resolveTarget(step.next, "default")
      : step.type === "measure" && step.alt
        ? step.alt
        : resolveTarget(step.next, "default");
    return moveTo(nextState, pathway, target, event.at);
  }

  if (event.type === "equipment_unavailable") {
    if (step.type !== "measure" || !step.needs) return state;
    const nextState: RunnerState = {
      ...state,
      incomplete: true,
      unknowns: [
        ...state.unknowns,
        `${step.text} — equipment unavailable (${step.needs})`,
      ],
    };
    const target = step.alt ?? resolveTarget(step.next, "default");
    return moveTo(nextState, pathway, target, event.at);
  }

  if (event.type === "action_complete") {
    const timerMinutes = step.type === "action" ? step.timer_minutes : undefined;
    const nextState: RunnerState = {
      ...state,
      actions: [
        ...state.actions,
        { text: step.text, at: event.at, timerMinutes },
      ],
    };
    if (pathway.reassess && step.type === "action" && timerMinutes) {
      return moveTo(nextState, pathway, "reassess", event.at);
    }
    return moveTo(nextState, pathway, resolveTarget(step.next, "default"), event.at);
  }

  return state;
}

/** Every question offers an escape hatch; never invent an answer. */
export function optionsFor(step: PathwayStep): string[] {
  if (step.type === "question") {
    return step.options.includes("Unable to assess")
      ? step.options
      : [...step.options, "Unable to assess"];
  }
  if (step.type === "urgent_check") {
    return step.options.includes("Unable to assess")
      ? step.options
      : [...step.options, "Unable to assess"];
  }
  return [];
}

export function equipmentMissing(
  step: PathwayStep,
  equipment: EquipmentKey[],
): boolean {
  return step.type === "measure" && step.needs !== null && !equipment.includes(step.needs);
}

export function reassessRemainingMs(
  state: RunnerState,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): number {
  if (!state.reassessDueAt) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, new Date(state.reassessDueAt).getTime() - now.getTime());
}

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/lib/privacy.ts
 lines: 10
================================================================
/** Local PIN gate for private wellbeing data. Not encryption: a lock on the UI. */

export async function hashPin(pin: string): Promise<string> {
  if (typeof crypto === "undefined" || !crypto.subtle) return `plain:${pin}`;
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(`crewsignal:${pin}`);
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", bytes);
  return [...new Uint8Array(digest)]
    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join("");
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { gate, isLateEntry, STALE_AFTER_MS } from "@/lib/quality/gate";
import type { Observation } from "@/lib/types";

const now = new Date("2026-11-14T12:00:00.000Z");

function observation(overrides: Partial<Observation> = {}): Observation {
  return {
    id: "obs-1",
    crewId: "alex-morgan",
    metric: "heart_rate",
    value: 72,
    unit: "bpm",
    observedAt: now.toISOString(),
    enteredAt: now.toISOString(),
    lane: "live",
    method: "ppg_finger",
    context: "rest",
    quality: "ok",
    qualityReason: null,
    environment: "mars_transit",
    procedureVersion: "demo-procedures-1",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("quality gate", () => {
  it("accepts a fresh live value inside the plausible range", () => {
    const result = gate(observation(), now);
    expect(result.quality).toBe("ok");
    expect(result.accepted).toBe(true);
  });

  it("flags a live value older than 60 minutes as stale", () => {
    const old = new Date(now.getTime() - STALE_AFTER_MS - 1000).toISOString();
    const result = gate(observation({ observedAt: old }), now);
    expect(result.quality).toBe("stale");
    expect(result.accepted).toBe(false);
  });

  it("never marks replay data stale, however old it is", () => {
    const old = new Date(now.getTime() - 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    const result = gate(observation({ lane: "replay", observedAt: old }), now);
    expect(result.quality).toBe("ok");
    expect(result.accepted).toBe(true);
  });

  it("never marks synthetic data stale", () => {
    const old = new Date(now.getTime() - 10 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    expect(gate(observation({ lane: "synthetic", observedAt: old }), now).accepted).toBe(
      true,
    );
  });

  it("judges a late manual entry by observedAt, not enteredAt", () => {
    const observed = new Date(now.getTime() - 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    const entry = observation({
      lane: "manual",
      method: "cuff_upper_arm",
      observedAt: observed,
      enteredAt: now.toISOString(),
    });
    const result = gate(entry, now);
    expect(result.quality).toBe("ok");
    expect(result.accepted).toBe(true);
    expect(isLateEntry(entry)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("still flags a manual entry whose observed time is old", () => {
    const observed = new Date(now.getTime() - 3 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    const result = gate(
      observation({
        lane: "manual",
        observedAt: observed,
        enteredAt: now.toISOString(),
      }),
      now,
    );
    expect(result.quality).toBe("stale");
  });

  it("rejects values outside the plausible sensor range", () => {
    const result = gate(observation({ value: 300 }), now);
    expect(result.quality).toBe("out_of_range");
    expect(result.accepted).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects missing measurements", () => {
    const result = gate(observation({ value: null }), now);
    expect(result.quality).toBe("not_measured");
  });

  it("keeps no-contact values out of comparisons", () => {
    expect(gate(observation({ quality: "no_contact" }), now).quality).toBe(
      "no_contact",
    );
  });

  it("flags motion artifacts", () => {
    expect(gate(observation({ artifact: "motion" }), now).quality).toBe("motion");
  });
});

 FILE: frontend/lib/quality/gate.ts
 lines: 93

import rulesFile from "@/lib/rules/demo-rules.json";
import type { GateResult, Metric, Observation, Quality } from "@/lib/types";

/**
 * Quality gate. Pure: same inputs, same output.
 *
 * Plausibility ranges are sensor-sanity limits from `demo-rules.json`, never
 * clinical limits. A flagged observation stays in the log but must not enter
 * charts, baselines or comparisons.
 */

export const STALE_AFTER_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;

const ranges: Partial<Record<Metric, { low: number; high: number }>> = {};
for (const rule of rulesFile.rules) {
  ranges[rule.metric as Metric] = { low: rule.low, high: rule.high };
}
// The same five-point scale applies to every self report.
for (const metric of ["stress", "rested", "connected"] as Metric[]) {
  ranges[metric] = ranges.mood;
}

export function plausibilityRange(metric: Metric) {
  return ranges[metric] ?? null;
}

const plain: Record<Quality, string> = {
  ok: "Sensor reported a usable value.",
  no_contact: "The sensor was not touching the skin, so the value is not usable.",
  stale: "This value is older than 60 minutes. It is history, not a current reading.",
  out_of_range: "The value is outside the plausible range for this sensor. Repeat the measurement.",
  motion: "Movement during the measurement made this value unreliable.",
  not_measured: "Nothing was measured for this reading.",
};

export function gate(
  observation: Observation,
  now: Date = new Date(),
  options: { ignoreStaleness?: boolean } = {},
): GateResult {
  const rejected = (quality: Quality, reason?: string): GateResult => ({
    quality,
    qualityReason: reason ?? plain[quality],
    accepted: false,
  });

  if (observation.quality === "not_measured" || observation.value === null) {
    return rejected("not_measured");
  }

  if (observation.artifact === "motion") {
    return rejected("motion");
  }

  if (observation.quality === "no_contact") {
    return rejected("no_contact");
  }

  const range = plausibilityRange(observation.metric);
  if (range && (observation.value < range.low || observation.value > range.high)) {
    return rejected(
      "out_of_range",
      `Value is outside the plausible sensor range (${range.low}–${range.high}). Repeat the measurement.`,
    );
  }

  // Staleness applies only to manual and live lanes. Replay and synthetic data
  // are historical by definition, so they are never "stale".
  if (
    !options.ignoreStaleness &&
    (observation.lane === "manual" || observation.lane === "live")
  ) {
    const observed = new Date(observation.observedAt).getTime();
    if (Number.isFinite(observed) && now.getTime() - observed > STALE_AFTER_MS) {
      return {
        quality: "stale",
        qualityReason: plain.stale,
        accepted: false,
      };
    }
  }

  return { quality: "ok", qualityReason: null, accepted: true };
}

/** Late manual entry: taken earlier, entered now. Judged by observed time. */
export function isLateEntry(observation: Observation): boolean {
  if (observation.lane !== "manual") return false;
  const observed = new Date(observation.observedAt).getTime();
  const entered = new Date(observation.enteredAt).getTime();
  if (!Number.isFinite(observed) || !Number.isFinite(entered)) return false;
  return entered - observed > 5 * 60 * 1000;
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseBedRestCsv, parseDuration } from "@/lib/replay/csv";

const csv = `Subject,Day,Phase,Sleep Duration,Heart Rate,Activity
P01,1,pre,7:44:00,62,0.21
P01,2,bedrest,No Data,58,0.04
P02,1,bedrest,6:40:00,60,0.03`;

describe("bed-rest replay parser", () => {
  it("reads 7:44:00 as 464 minutes, not 7.44 hours", () => {
    expect(parseDuration("7:44:00")).toBeCloseTo(464);
    expect(parseDuration("6:40:00")).toBeCloseTo(400);
  });

  it("keeps 'No Data' as missing", () => {
    expect(parseDuration("No Data")).toBeNull();
    const { rows } = parseBedRestCsv(csv);
    const missing = rows.find((row) => row.metric === "sleep_minutes" && row.day === 2);
    expect(missing?.value).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps phase and bed-rest day and three participants", () => {
    const { rows } = parseBedRestCsv(csv);
    expect(rows.some((row) => row.phase === "bedrest")).toBe(true);
    expect(rows.find((row) => row.metric === "heart_rate" && row.day === 1)?.value).toBe(62);
    expect(new Set(rows.map((row) => row.participant)).size).toBe(2);
  });
});

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/lib/replay/csv.ts
 lines: 117

import type { Metric, Observation } from "@/lib/types";
import { makeObservation } from "@/lib/observations/derived";

/**
 * NASA bed-rest actigraphy is a ground analog, not this crew's data.
 * Durations arrive as `7:44:00` (hours:minutes:seconds) and must not be read
 * as decimal hours. "No Data" stays missing.
 */
export type ReplayRow = {
  participant: string;
  day: number;
  phase: string;
  metric: Metric;
  value: number | null;
  enteredAt: string;
};

const columnMetrics: Array<{ test: RegExp; metric: Metric }> = [
  { test: /sleep/i, metric: "sleep_minutes" },
  { test: /heart|^hr$/i, metric: "heart_rate" },
  { test: /activity|motion/i, metric: "motion" },
  { test: /temp/i, metric: "skin_temp" },
];

export function parseDuration(value: string): number | null {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed || /no\s*data|n\/a|null/i.test(trimmed)) return null;
  const clock = trimmed.match(/^(\d+):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?$/);
  if (clock) {
    const hours = Number(clock[1]);
    const minutes = Number(clock[2]);
    const seconds = Number(clock[3] ?? 0);
    return hours * 60 + minutes + seconds / 60;
  }
  const numeric = Number(trimmed);
  return Number.isFinite(numeric) ? numeric : null;
}

function splitCsvLine(line: string): string[] {
  return line.split(",").map((cell) => cell.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ""));
}

export function parseBedRestCsv(text: string): { rows: ReplayRow[]; skipped: number } {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).filter((line) => line.trim() !== "");
  if (lines.length < 2) return { rows: [], skipped: 0 };

  const header = splitCsvLine(lines[0]);
  const find = (pattern: RegExp) => header.findIndex((cell) => pattern.test(cell));
  const subjectIndex = find(/subject|participant|id/i);
  const dayIndex = find(/^day|bed\s*rest\s*day/i);
  const phaseIndex = find(/phase|condition/i);
  const dateIndex = find(/date|timestamp/i);

  const metricColumns = header
    .map((cell, index) => {
      const match = columnMetrics.find((entry) => entry.test.test(cell));
      return match ? { index, metric: match.metric, isDuration: /sleep/i.test(cell) } : null;
    })
    .filter((entry): entry is { index: number; metric: Metric; isDuration: boolean } => !!entry);

  if (metricColumns.length === 0) return { rows: [], skipped: lines.length - 1 };

  const rows: ReplayRow[] = [];
  let skipped = 0;

  for (const line of lines.slice(1)) {
    const cells = splitCsvLine(line);
    const participant = subjectIndex >= 0 ? cells[subjectIndex] : "unknown";
    const day = dayIndex >= 0 ? Number(cells[dayIndex]) : Number.NaN;
    const phase = phaseIndex >= 0 ? cells[phaseIndex] : "unknown";
    if (!participant && !Number.isFinite(day)) {
      skipped += 1;
      continue;
    }
    for (const column of metricColumns) {
      const raw = cells[column.index] ?? "";
      const parsed = column.isDuration
        ? parseDuration(raw)
        : raw.trim() === "" || /no\s*data/i.test(raw)
          ? null
          : Number(raw);
      rows.push({
        participant,
        day: Number.isFinite(day) ? day : 0,
        phase,
        metric: column.metric,
        value: Number.isFinite(parsed as number) ? parsed : null,
        enteredAt:
          dateIndex >= 0 && cells[dateIndex]
            ? new Date(cells[dateIndex]).toISOString()
            : new Date().toISOString(),
      });
    }
  }

  return { rows, skipped };
}

export function replayObservations(
  rows: ReplayRow[],
  crewId: string,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Observation[] {
  return rows.map((row) =>
    makeObservation({
      crewId,
      metric: row.metric,
      value: row.value,
      observedAt: new Date(now.getTime() - (row.day + 1) * 86_400_000).toISOString(),
      enteredAt: row.enteredAt,
      lane: "replay",
      method: "actigraphy",
      context: "unknown",
      quality: row.value === null ? "not_measured" : "ok",
    }),
  );
}

import { LocalRepository, type Repository } from "@/lib/repo/repository";
import { SyncedRepository } from "@/lib/repo/synced-repository";
import { apiConfigured } from "@/lib/outbox/http-transport";

/**
 * One repository for the UI. With no API URL it is purely local; with one, the
 * synced wrapper writes locally first.
 */
const local = new LocalRepository();

export const repository: Repository = apiConfigured
  ? new SyncedRepository(local)
  : local;

export const syncedRepository =
  repository instanceof SyncedRepository ? repository : null;

 FILE: frontend/lib/repo/repository.ts
 lines: 222
================================================================
import { openDB, type DBSchema, type IDBPDatabase } from "idb";
import type {
  CheckIn,
  Encounter,
  Observation,
  OutboxItem,
} from "@/lib/types";

/**
 * The UI only talks to this interface. Swapping in a network-backed
 * implementation later must not require UI changes.
 */
export interface Repository {
  listObservations(crewId?: string): Promise<Observation[]>;
  putObservations(items: Observation[]): Promise<void>;
  listCheckIns(crewId?: string): Promise<CheckIn[]>;
  putCheckIn(checkIn: CheckIn): Promise<void>;
  listEncounters(crewId?: string): Promise<Encounter[]>;
  putEncounter(encounter: Encounter): Promise<void>;
  listOutbox(): Promise<OutboxItem[]>;
  putOutboxItem(item: OutboxItem): Promise<void>;
  deleteOutboxItem(id: string): Promise<void>;
  getConfig<T>(key: string): Promise<T | null>;
  setConfig<T>(key: string, value: T): Promise<void>;
}

interface CrewSignalDB extends DBSchema {
  observations: {
    key: string;
    value: Observation;
    indexes: { crewId: string };
  };
  checkIns: { key: string; value: CheckIn; indexes: { crewId: string } };
  encounters: { key: string; value: Encounter; indexes: { crewId: string } };
  outbox: { key: string; value: OutboxItem };
  config: { key: string; value: unknown };
}

const DB_NAME = "crewsignal";
const DB_VERSION = 1;
const LS_PREFIX = "crewsignal:repo:";

type StoreName = "observations" | "checkIns" | "encounters" | "outbox";

function hasIndexedDb(): boolean {
  return typeof indexedDB !== "undefined";
}

/** localStorage fallback keeps the same contract for private-mode browsers. */
class LocalStorageShim {
  read<T>(store: StoreName): T[] {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return [];
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${LS_PREFIX}${store}`);
      if (!raw) return [];
      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
      return Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as T[]) : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }

  write<T>(store: StoreName, rows: T[]): void {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
    localStorage.setItem(`${LS_PREFIX}${store}`, JSON.stringify(rows));
  }
}

export class LocalRepository implements Repository {
  private dbPromise: Promise<IDBPDatabase<CrewSignalDB> | null> | null = null;
  private shim = new LocalStorageShim();

  private db() {
    if (!hasIndexedDb()) return Promise.resolve(null);
    this.dbPromise ??= openDB<CrewSignalDB>(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION, {
      upgrade(db) {
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains("observations")) {
          const store = db.createObjectStore("observations", { keyPath: "id" });
          store.createIndex("crewId", "crewId");
        }
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains("checkIns")) {
          const store = db.createObjectStore("checkIns", { keyPath: "id" });
          store.createIndex("crewId", "crewId");
        }
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains("encounters")) {
          const store = db.createObjectStore("encounters", { keyPath: "id" });
          store.createIndex("crewId", "crewId");
        }
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains("outbox")) {
          db.createObjectStore("outbox", { keyPath: "id" });
        }
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains("config")) {
          db.createObjectStore("config");
        }
      },
    }).catch(() => null);
    return this.dbPromise;
  }

  private async all<T>(store: StoreName): Promise<T[]> {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) return this.shim.read<T>(store as never);
    return (await db.getAll(store)) as T[];
  }

  private async byCrew<T>(
    store: "observations" | "checkIns" | "encounters",
    crewId?: string,
  ): Promise<T[]> {
    if (!crewId) return this.all<T>(store);
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      return (this.shim.read<T>(store) as Array<{ crewId?: string }>).filter(
        (row) => row.crewId === crewId,
      ) as T[];
    }
    return (await db.getAllFromIndex(store, "crewId", crewId)) as T[];
  }

  async listObservations(crewId?: string) {
    return this.byCrew<Observation>("observations", crewId);
  }

  async putObservations(items: Observation[]) {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      const existing = this.shim.read<Observation>("observations");
      const map = new Map(existing.map((row) => [row.id, row]));
      for (const item of items) map.set(item.id, item);
      this.shim.write("observations", [...map.values()]);
      return;
    }
    const tx = db.transaction("observations", "readwrite");
    await Promise.all([...items.map((item) => tx.store.put(item)), tx.done]);
  }

  async listCheckIns(crewId?: string) {
    return this.byCrew<CheckIn>("checkIns", crewId);
  }

  async putCheckIn(checkIn: CheckIn) {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      const existing = this.shim.read<CheckIn>("checkIns");
      this.shim.write("checkIns", [
        ...existing.filter((row) => row.id !== checkIn.id),
        checkIn,
      ]);
      return;
    }
    await db.put("checkIns", checkIn);
  }

  async listEncounters(crewId?: string) {
    return this.byCrew<Encounter>("encounters", crewId);
  }

  async putEncounter(encounter: Encounter) {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      const existing = this.shim.read<Encounter>("encounters");
      this.shim.write("encounters", [
        ...existing.filter((row) => row.id !== encounter.id),
        encounter,
      ]);
      return;
    }
    await db.put("encounters", encounter);
  }

  async listOutbox() {
    return this.all<OutboxItem>("outbox");
  }

  async putOutboxItem(item: OutboxItem) {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      const existing = this.shim.read<OutboxItem>("outbox");
      this.shim.write("outbox", [
        ...existing.filter((row) => row.id !== item.id),
        item,
      ]);
      return;
    }
    await db.put("outbox", item);
  }

  async deleteOutboxItem(id: string) {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      this.shim.write(
        "outbox",
        this.shim.read<OutboxItem>("outbox").filter((row) => row.id !== id),
      );
      return;
    }
    await db.delete("outbox", id);
  }

  async getConfig<T>(key: string): Promise<T | null> {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return null;
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${LS_PREFIX}config:${key}`);
      return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as T) : null;
    }
    const value = await db.get("config", key);
    return (value as T) ?? null;
  }

  async setConfig<T>(key: string, value: T): Promise<void> {
    const db = await this.db();
    if (!db) {
      if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
      localStorage.setItem(`${LS_PREFIX}config:${key}`, JSON.stringify(value));
      return;
    }
    await db.put("config", value, key);
  }
}

export const repository: Repository = new LocalRepository();

================================================================
 FILE: frontend/lib/repo/synced-repository.ts
 lines: 152
================================================================
import type {
  CheckIn,
  Encounter,
  Observation,
  OutboxItem,
} from "@/lib/types";
import type { Repository } from "@/lib/repo/repository";
import { apiConfigured } from "@/lib/outbox/http-transport";

const SYNC_CURSOR_KEY = "syncCursor";
const BATCH = 200;

type Cursor = Record<string, number>;

/**
 * Local-first wrapper: every write lands in IndexedDB first, then goes to the
 * server in the background. A failed push never loses the local copy and never
 * invents a server state.
 */
export class SyncedRepository implements Repository {
  private local: Repository;
  private pushing = false;
  private pendingObservations: Observation[] = [];
  private pendingCheckIns: CheckIn[] = [];
  private pendingEncounters: Encounter[] = [];

  constructor(local: Repository) {
    this.local = local;
  }

  listObservations(crewId?: string) {
    return this.local.listObservations(crewId);
  }
  listCheckIns(crewId?: string) {
    return this.local.listCheckIns(crewId);
  }
  listEncounters(crewId?: string) {
    return this.local.listEncounters(crewId);
  }
  listOutbox() {
    return this.local.listOutbox();
  }
  putOutboxItem(item: OutboxItem) {
    return this.local.putOutboxItem(item);
  }
  deleteOutboxItem(id: string) {
    return this.local.deleteOutboxItem(id);
  }
  getConfig<T>(key: string) {
    return this.local.getConfig<T>(key);
  }
  setConfig<T>(key: string, value: T) {
    return this.local.setConfig<T>(key, value);
  }

  async putObservations(items: Observation[]) {
    await this.local.putObservations(items);
    if (!apiConfigured) return;
    this.pendingObservations.push(...items);
    void this.flush();
  }

  async putCheckIn(checkIn: CheckIn) {
    await this.local.putCheckIn(checkIn);
    if (!apiConfigured) return;
    this.pendingCheckIns.push(checkIn);
    void this.flush();
  }

  async putEncounter(encounter: Encounter) {
    await this.local.putEncounter(encounter);
    if (!apiConfigured) return;
    this.pendingEncounters.push(encounter);
    void this.flush();
  }

  private async flush() {
    if (this.pushing || !apiConfigured) return;
    this.pushing = true;
    try {
      await this.push("observations", this.pendingObservations.splice(0, BATCH));
      await this.push("checkins", this.pendingCheckIns.splice(0, BATCH));
      await this.push("encounters", this.pendingEncounters.splice(0, BATCH));
    } finally {
      this.pushing = false;
    }
  }

  private async push(collection: string, items: unknown[]) {
    if (items.length === 0) return;
    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `${(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "").replace(/\/+$/, "")}/v1/sync/${collection}`,
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            authorization: `Bearer ${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CREW_KEY ?? ""}`,
            "content-type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(items),
        },
      );
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`sync ${collection} failed: ${response.status}`);
    } catch {
      // Keep the items for the next flush instead of dropping them.
      if (collection === "observations") this.pendingObservations.push(...(items as Observation[]));
      if (collection === "checkins") this.pendingCheckIns.push(...(items as CheckIn[]));
      if (collection === "encounters") this.pendingEncounters.push(...(items as Encounter[]));
    }
  }

  /** Pull everything newer than the stored cursor. Returns how many rows landed. */
  async pull(): Promise<number> {
    if (!apiConfigured) return 0;
    const cursor = (await this.getConfig<Cursor>(SYNC_CURSOR_KEY)) ?? {
      observations: 0,
      checkins: 0,
      encounters: 0,
    };
    let landed = 0;
    for (const collection of ["observations", "checkins", "encounters"] as const) {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          `${(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? "").replace(/\/+$/, "")}/v1/sync/${collection}?since=${cursor[collection] ?? 0}`,
          {
            headers: {
              authorization: `Bearer ${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CREW_KEY ?? ""}`,
            },
          },
        );
        if (!response.ok) continue;
        const body = (await response.json()) as {
          items: Array<Observation | CheckIn | Encounter>;
          cursor: number;
        };
        if (collection === "observations") {
          await this.local.putObservations(body.items as Observation[]);
        } else if (collection === "checkins") {
          for (const item of body.items as CheckIn[]) await this.local.putCheckIn(item);
        } else {
          for (const item of body.items as Encounter[]) await this.local.putEncounter(item);
        }
        cursor[collection] = body.cursor;
        landed += body.items.length;
      } catch {
        // Offline: keep the cursor and try again next start.
      }
    }
    await this.setConfig(SYNC_CURSOR_KEY, cursor);
    return landed;
  }
}

import type { Observation, Quality } from "@/lib/types";

/**
 * Live-sensor seam. A backend or Bluetooth device can replace the simulator
 * without touching the UI: implement this interface and pass it in.
 */
export interface SensorAdapter {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly available: boolean;
  connect(): Promise<void>;
  disconnect(): void;
  onSample(listener: (sample: Observation) => void): () => void;
  onQuality(listener: (quality: Quality) => void): () => void;
}

export class SimulatedAdapter implements SensorAdapter {
  readonly name = "Simulated sensor";
  readonly available = true;
  private samples = new Set<(sample: Observation) => void>();
  private quality = new Set<(quality: Quality) => void>();
  private timer: number | null = null;

  async connect() {
    if (this.timer !== null) return;
    this.timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      for (const listener of this.quality) listener("ok");
    }, 10_000);
  }

  disconnect() {
    if (this.timer !== null) window.clearInterval(this.timer);
    this.timer = null;
    this.samples.clear();
    this.quality.clear();
  }

  onSample(listener: (sample: Observation) => void) {
    this.samples.add(listener);
    return () => this.samples.delete(listener);
  }

  onQuality(listener: (quality: Quality) => void) {
    this.quality.add(listener);
    return () => this.quality.delete(listener);
  }
}

/** Web Serial pulse sensor placeholder. Kept honest: it does nothing yet. */
export class WebSerialAdapter implements SensorAdapter {
  readonly name = "Web Serial pulse sensor";
  readonly available =
    typeof navigator !== "undefined" && "serial" in (navigator as Navigator);

  async connect() {
    if (!this.available) {
      throw new Error(
        "Web Serial is not available in this browser. Use the simulated sensor for the demo.",
      );
    }
    throw new Error(
      "Web Serial support is a stub in this demo. No device protocol is implemented yet.",
    );
  }

  disconnect() {}

  onSample() {
    return () => undefined;
  }

  onQuality() {
    return () => undefined;
  }
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { compareObservation } from "@/lib/status/compare";
import type { Observation } from "@/lib/types";

const now = new Date("2026-11-14T12:00:00.000Z");

function obs(id: string, value: number, overrides: Partial<Observation> = {}): Observation {
  return {
    id,
    crewId: "alex-morgan",
    metric: "heart_rate",
    value,
    unit: "bpm",
    observedAt: new Date(now.getTime() - 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    enteredAt: new Date(now.getTime() - 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    lane: "live",
    method: "ppg_finger",
    context: "rest",
    quality: "ok",
    qualityReason: null,
    environment: "mars_transit",
    procedureVersion: "demo-procedures-1",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("compare with personal history", () => {
  it("does not turn missing history into a green state", () => {
    const result = compareObservation(obs("new", 70), [obs("a", 68), obs("b", 69)], now);
    expect(result.state).toBe("insufficient_history");
    expect(result.range).toBeNull();
  });

  it("requires five comparable observations before within-range", () => {
    const history = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => obs(`h${n}`, 68 + n));
    const result = compareObservation(obs("new", 70), history, now);
    expect(result.state).toBe("within_range");
    expect(result.comparableCount).toBe(5);
  });

  it("suggests attention when a value sits outside the personal range", () => {
    const history = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => obs(`h${n}`, 60 + n));
    const result = compareObservation(obs("new", 95), history, now);
    expect(result.state).toBe("attention");
    expect(result.detail).toContain("No cause assigned");
  });

  it("never reports green for a bad reading", () => {
    const history = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => obs(`h${n}`, 70));
    const result = compareObservation(obs("new", 70, { quality: "no_contact" }), history, now);
    expect(result.state).toBe("no_valid_reading");
  });

  it("only compares the same metric, method and context", () => {
    const history = [
      ...([1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => obs(`rest${n}`, 70))),
      ...([6, 7, 8, 9, 10].map((n) => obs(`ex${n}`, 140, { context: "post_exercise" }))),
    ];
    const result = compareObservation(obs("new", 72), history, now);
    expect(result.comparableCount).toBe(5);
    expect(result.state).toBe("within_range");
  });
});

 FILE: frontend/lib/status/compare.ts

import type { Comparison, Metric, Observation, StatusState } from "@/lib/types";
import { gate } from "@/lib/quality/gate";
import { metricMeta } from "@/lib/metrics";

/** How far from the personal range a value must sit before it is flagged. */
const tolerance: Record<Metric, number> = {
  heart_rate: 6,
  bp_systolic: 8,
  bp_diastolic: 6,
  skin_temp: 0.3,
  glucose: 1.2,
  sleep_minutes: 60,
  mood: 1,
  stress: 1,
  rested: 1,
  connected: 1,
  motion: 0.15,
};

export const MIN_COMPARABLE = 5;
const MAX_COMPARABLE = 7;

export function isComparable(candidate: Observation, observation: Observation): boolean {
  return (
    candidate.metric === observation.metric &&
    candidate.method === observation.method &&
    candidate.context === observation.context &&
    candidate.lane === observation.lane &&
    candidate.id !== observation.id &&
    candidate.quality === "ok" &&
    candidate.value !== null
  );
}

export function comparableHistory(
  observation: Observation,
  history: Observation[],
): Observation[] {
  return history
    .filter((candidate) => isComparable(candidate, observation))
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        new Date(b.observedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.observedAt).getTime(),
    )
    .slice(0, MAX_COMPARABLE);
}

export const stateLabels: Record<StatusState, string> = {
  logged: "Logged",
  attention: "Attention suggested",
  no_valid_reading: "No valid reading",
  within_range: "Within your recent range",
  insufficient_history: "Insufficient comparable history",
};

export function compareObservation(
  observation: Observation,
  history: Observation[],
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Comparison {
  const result = gate(observation, now);
  if (!result.accepted) {
    return {
      state: "no_valid_reading",
      label: stateLabels.no_valid_reading,
      detail: result.qualityReason ?? "This reading cannot be used.",
      comparableCount: 0,
      range: null,
    };
  }

  // Replay and synthetic lanes are context, not this person's current status.
  if (observation.lane === "replay" || observation.lane === "synthetic") {
    return {
      state: "logged",
      label: stateLabels.logged,
      detail: "Reference data from another source. Not used as your status.",
      comparableCount: 0,
      range: null,
    };
  }

  const comparable = comparableHistory(observation, history);
  const count = comparable.length;
  if (count < MIN_COMPARABLE) {
    return {
      state: "insufficient_history",
      label: stateLabels.insufficient_history,
      detail: `Needs ${MIN_COMPARABLE} comparable observations. ${count} available.`,
      comparableCount: count,
      range: null,
    };
  }

  const values = comparable.map((entry) => entry.value as number);
  const low = Math.min(...values);
  const high = Math.max(...values);
  const value = observation.value as number;
  const delta = tolerance[observation.metric];
  const within = value >= low - delta && value <= high + delta;
  const meta = metricMeta[observation.metric];

  return {
    state: within ? "within_range" : "attention",
    label: within ? stateLabels.within_range : stateLabels.attention,
    detail: within
      ? `Compared with ${count} of your recent ${meta.label.toLowerCase()} readings.`
      : `Differs from your recent ${meta.label.toLowerCase()} readings. No cause assigned.`,
    comparableCount: count,
    range: { low, high },
  };
}

export type StatusSummary = {
  attention: number;
  noValid: number;
  within: number;
  insufficient: number;
  worst: StatusState;
  urgentSymptoms: string[];
};

export function summariseStatus(
  entries: Array<{ comparison: Comparison; observation?: Observation; urgent?: boolean }>,
): StatusSummary {
  const summary: StatusSummary = {
    attention: 0,
    noValid: 0,
    within: 0,
    insufficient: 0,
    worst: "logged",
    urgentSymptoms: [],
  };

  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.comparison.state === "attention") summary.attention += 1;
    if (entry.comparison.state === "no_valid_reading") summary.noValid += 1;
    if (entry.comparison.state === "within_range") summary.within += 1;
    if (entry.comparison.state === "insufficient_history") summary.insufficient += 1;
    if (entry.comparison.state === "attention") summary.worst = "attention";
  }

  return summary;
}

 FILE: frontend/lib/store.ts

"use client";

import { create } from "zustand";
import { repository, syncedRepository } from "@/lib/repo/client";
import type { Repository } from "@/lib/repo/repository";
import {
  apiConfigured,
  fetchPackets,
  packetToOutbox,
  postPacket,
  sendLink,
  type ServerPacket,
} from "@/lib/outbox/http-transport";
import { environmentFor, missionConfig } from "@/lib/mission-config";
import { hashPin } from "@/lib/privacy";
import { seedObservations } from "@/lib/demo/seed";
import type {
  CheckIn,
  Encounter,
  EnvironmentKey,
  LinkState,
  Observation,
  OutboxItem,
  OutboxState,
} from "@/lib/types";

type Toast = { message: string; tone: "neutral" | "attention" | "ok" };

type State = {
  hydrated: boolean;
  storageError: string | null;
  theme: "dark" | "light";
  largeText: boolean;
  nightWatch: boolean;
  environment: EnvironmentKey;
  link: LinkState;
  observations: Observation[];
  checkIns: CheckIn[];
  encounters: Encounter[];
  outbox: OutboxItem[];
  toast: Toast | null;
  pinHash: string | null;
  privateUnlocked: boolean;
  serverConfigured: boolean;
  serverReachable: boolean | null;
  serverDelayScale: number;
};

type Actions = {
  hydrate: (repo?: Repository) => Promise<void>;
  setTheme: (theme: "dark" | "light") => void;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
  setLargeText: (value: boolean) => void;
  setNightWatch: (value: boolean) => void;
  setEnvironment: (value: EnvironmentKey) => Promise<void>;
  setLink: (value: LinkState) => Promise<void>;
  addObservations: (items: Observation[]) => Promise<void>;
  saveCheckIn: (checkIn: CheckIn) => Promise<void>;
  saveEncounter: (encounter: Encounter) => Promise<void>;
  enqueue: (item: OutboxItem) => Promise<void>;
  cancelOutbox: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  acknowledge: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  advanceOutbox: (now?: Date) => Promise<void>;
  refreshOutbox: () => Promise<void>;
  setPin: (pin: string) => Promise<void>;
  unlockPrivate: (pin: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  lockPrivate: () => void;
  notify: (message: string, tone?: Toast["tone"]) => void;
  clearToast: () => void;
  setServerStatus: (reachable: boolean, delayScale?: number) => void;
  applyServerPacket: (packet: ServerPacket) => Promise<void>;
  reconcileOutbox: (crewId: string) => Promise<void>;
};

const THEME_KEY = "crewsignal-theme";
/** In-flight packet ids, so the 2 s tick cannot post the same packet twice. */
const posting = new Set<string>();

function readTheme(): "dark" | "light" {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return "dark";
  const stored = localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY);
  if (stored === "dark" || stored === "light") return stored;
  if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.matchMedia) {
    return window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: light)").matches
      ? "light"
      : "dark";
  }
  return "dark";
}

/** Pure, so the outbox timeline is testable and never fakes a receipt. */
export function nextOutboxState(
  item: OutboxItem,
  link: LinkState,
  now: Date,
): OutboxState | null {
  if (item.state !== "queued") return null;
  if (link === "blackout") return null;
  if (link === "live") return "sent";
  const created = new Date(item.createdAt).getTime();
  return now.getTime() - created >= item.delaySeconds * 1000 ? "sent" : null;
}

export const useStore = create<State & Actions>((set, get) => ({
  hydrated: false,
  storageError: null,
  theme: "dark",
  largeText: false,
  nightWatch: false,
  environment: missionConfig.environment,
  link: "live",
  observations: [],
  checkIns: [],
  encounters: [],
  outbox: [],
  toast: null,
  pinHash: null,
  privateUnlocked: false,
  serverConfigured: apiConfigured,
  serverReachable: apiConfigured ? null : false,
  serverDelayScale: 1,

  hydrate: async (repo = repository) => {
    if (get().hydrated) return;
    set({ theme: readTheme() });
    try {
      let [observations, checkIns, encounters, outbox, prefs] =
        await Promise.all([
          repo.listObservations(),
          repo.listCheckIns(),
          repo.listEncounters(),
          repo.listOutbox(),
          repo.getConfig<{ environment?: EnvironmentKey; link?: LinkState }>(
            "prefs",
          ),
        ]);
      const pinHash = await repo.getConfig<string>("pin");
      if (observations.length === 0) {
        observations = seedObservations();
        await repo.putObservations(observations);
      }

      set({
        observations,
        checkIns,
        encounters,
        outbox,
        environment: prefs?.environment ?? missionConfig.environment,
        link: prefs?.link ?? "live",
        pinHash: pinHash ?? null,
        hydrated: true,
      });

      if (syncedRepository) {
        void syncedRepository
          .pull()
          .then(async (count) => {
            if (count === 0) return;
            const [nextObservations, nextCheckIns, nextEncounters] = await Promise.all([
              repo.listObservations(),
              repo.listCheckIns(),
              repo.listEncounters(),
            ]);
            set({
              observations: nextObservations,
              checkIns: nextCheckIns,
              encounters: nextEncounters,
            });
          })
          .catch(() => undefined);
      }
    } catch {
      set({
        hydrated: true,
        storageError:
          "Saved data could not be loaded from this device. New entries will stay in this session only.",
      });
    }
  },

  setTheme: (theme) => {
    set({ theme });
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, theme);
    } catch {
      get().notify("Theme preference could not be saved on this device.");
    }
  },

  toggleTheme: () => get().setTheme(get().theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"),
  setLargeText: (largeText) => set({ largeText }),
  setNightWatch: (nightWatch) => set({ nightWatch }),

  setEnvironment: async (environment) => {
    set({ environment });
    await repository.setConfig("prefs", { environment, link: get().link });
  },

  setLink: async (link) => {
    const environment = get().environment;
    set({ link });
    await repository.setConfig("prefs", { environment, link });
    if (apiConfigured) {
      const result = await sendLink(link, environment);
      if (result.ok) {
        set({ serverReachable: true, serverDelayScale: result.link.delayScale });
      } else {
        set({ serverReachable: false });
      }
    }
    await get().advanceOutbox();
  },

  addObservations: async (items) => {
    if (items.length === 0) return;
    set({ observations: [...get().observations, ...items] });
    await repository.putObservations(items);
  },

  saveCheckIn: async (checkIn) => {
    set({
      checkIns: [
        ...get().checkIns.filter((entry) => entry.id !== checkIn.id),
        checkIn,
      ],
    });
    await repository.putCheckIn(checkIn);
  },

  saveEncounter: async (encounter) => {
    set({
      encounters: [
        ...get().encounters.filter((entry) => entry.id !== encounter.id),
        encounter,
      ],
    });
    await repository.putEncounter(encounter);
  },

  enqueue: async (item) => {
    set({ outbox: [...get().outbox.filter((row) => row.id !== item.id), item] });
    await repository.putOutboxItem(item);
    await get().advanceOutbox();
  },

  cancelOutbox: async (id) => {
    set({ outbox: get().outbox.filter((row) => row.id !== id) });
    await repository.deleteOutboxItem(id);
  },

  acknowledge: async (id) => {
    // With a ground server, only the server can say a packet was acknowledged.
    if (apiConfigured) return;
    const item = get().outbox.find((row) => row.id === id);
    if (!item || item.state === "acknowledged") return;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const updated: OutboxItem = {
      ...item,
      state: "acknowledged",
      acknowledgedAt: now,
      history: [
        ...item.history,
        { state: "acknowledged", at: now, note: "Flight surgeon acknowledged the packet." },
      ],
    };
    set({
      outbox: get().outbox.map((row) => (row.id === id ? updated : row)),
    });
    await repository.putOutboxItem(updated);
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel !== "undefined") {
      const channel = new BroadcastChannel("crewsignal-outbox");
      channel.postMessage({ type: "acknowledged", id });
      channel.close();
    }
  },

  advanceOutbox: async (now = new Date()) => {
    const { outbox, link } = get();

    if (apiConfigured) {
      if (get().serverReachable === false) return;
      for (const item of outbox.filter((row) => row.state === "queued")) {
        if (posting.has(item.id)) continue;
        posting.add(item.id);
        try {
          const result = await postPacket(item);
          if (result.ok) {
            set({ serverReachable: true });
            await get().applyServerPacket(result.packet);
          } else {
            set({ serverReachable: result.status === 0 ? false : true });
          }
        } finally {
          posting.delete(item.id);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    const updates: OutboxItem[] = [];
    for (const item of outbox) {
      const next = nextOutboxState(item, link, now);
      if (next === "sent") {
        updates.push({
          ...item,
          state: "sent",
          sentAt: now.toISOString(),
          history: [
            ...item.history,
            {
              state: "sent",
              at: now.toISOString(),
              note:
                link === "live"
                  ? "Link is live. Packet left the spacecraft."
                  : `Configured one-way delay (${environmentFor(get().environment).label}) elapsed. Packet left the spacecraft.`,
            },
          ],
        });
      }
    }
    if (updates.length === 0) return;
    set({
      outbox: get().outbox.map(
        (row) => updates.find((update) => update.id === row.id) ?? row,
      ),
    });
    for (const update of updates) await repository.putOutboxItem(update);
    if (typeof BroadcastChannel !== "undefined") {
      const channel = new BroadcastChannel("crewsignal-outbox");
      channel.postMessage({ type: "sent" });
      channel.close();
    }
  },

  refreshOutbox: async () => {
    set({ outbox: await repository.listOutbox() });
  },

  setPin: async (pin) => {
    const hash = await hashPin(pin);
    await repository.setConfig("pin", hash);
    set({ pinHash: hash, privateUnlocked: true });
  },

  unlockPrivate: async (pin) => {
    const hash = await hashPin(pin);
    if (hash !== get().pinHash) return false;
    set({ privateUnlocked: true });
    return true;
  },

  lockPrivate: () => set({ privateUnlocked: false }),

  notify: (message, tone = "neutral") => set({ toast: { message, tone } }),
  clearToast: () => set({ toast: null }),

  setServerStatus: (reachable, delayScale) =>
    set({
      serverReachable: reachable,
      ...(delayScale !== undefined ? { serverDelayScale: delayScale } : {}),
    }),

  applyServerPacket: async (packet) => {
    const item = packetToOutbox(packet);
    set({
      outbox: get().outbox.some((row) => row.id === item.id)
        ? get().outbox.map((row) => (row.id === item.id ? item : row))
        : [...get().outbox, item],
    });
    await repository.putOutboxItem(item);
  },

  reconcileOutbox: async (crewId) => {
    const result = await fetchPackets(crewId);
    if (!result.ok) {
      if (result.status === 0) set({ serverReachable: false });
      return;
    }
    set({ serverReachable: true });
    for (const packet of result.packets) await get().applyServerPacket(packet);
  },
}));

export function useCrew(id: string) {
  return missionConfig.crew.find((member) => member.id === id) ?? null;
}

import type { SymptomOnset, SymptomTrend } from "@/lib/types";

/** Demo symptom list. `urgent` ones must never wait for the form to be saved. */
export const symptomCatalogue: Array<{ name: string; urgent: boolean }> = [
  { name: "Headache", urgent: false },
  { name: "Dizziness", urgent: false },
  { name: "Fainting", urgent: true },
  { name: "Nausea", urgent: false },
  { name: "Tired", urgent: false },
  { name: "Trouble sleeping", urgent: false },
  { name: "Fever feeling", urgent: false },
  { name: "Muscle or joint pain", urgent: false },
  { name: "Vision change", urgent: true },
  { name: "Chest discomfort", urgent: true },
];

export const onsetLabels: Record<SymptomOnset, string> = {
  just_now: "Just now",
  today: "Today",
  several_days: "Several days",
};

export const trendLabels: Record<SymptomTrend, string> = {
  better: "Better",
  same: "Same",
  worse: "Worse",
};

export function isUrgentSymptom(name: string): boolean {
  return symptomCatalogue.find((entry) => entry.name === name)?.urgent ?? false;
}

import type { CheckIn } from "@/lib/types";

export type TaskState = "done" | "due" | "overdue" | "upcoming";

export type MissionTask = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  detail: string;
  dueHourUtc: number;
  state: TaskState;
};

const schedule = [
  { id: "sleep", label: "Sleep log", detail: "Record last night's sleep.", dueHourUtc: 7 },
  { id: "measure", label: "Morning readings", detail: "Heart rate and temperature.", dueHourUtc: 9 },
  { id: "checkin", label: "Daily check-in", detail: "Symptoms, mood and stress.", dueHourUtc: 18 },
];

export function tasksFor(
  crewId: string,
  checkIns: CheckIn[],
  now: Date = new Date(),
): MissionTask[] {
  const today = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const todays = checkIns.filter(
    (entry) => entry.crewId === crewId && entry.observedAt.slice(0, 10) === today,
  );
  return schedule.map((task) => {
    const done = todays.some((entry) => entry.id.startsWith(`${task.id}-`));
    const hour = now.getUTCHours() + now.getUTCMinutes() / 60;
    const state: TaskState = done
      ? "done"
      : hour > task.dueHourUtc + 2
        ? "overdue"
        : hour >= task.dueHourUtc - 1
          ? "due"
          : "upcoming";
    return { ...task, state };
  });
}

/** Consecutive days with at least one check-in, counting back from today. */
export function checkInStreak(crewId: string, checkIns: CheckIn[], now = new Date()): number {
  const days = new Set(
    checkIns
      .filter((entry) => entry.crewId === crewId)
      .map((entry) => entry.observedAt.slice(0, 10)),
  );
  let streak = 0;
  const cursor = new Date(now);
  for (let index = 0; index < 400; index += 1) {
    const day = cursor.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (!days.has(day)) {
      if (index === 0) {
        cursor.setUTCDate(cursor.getUTCDate() - 1);
        continue;
      }
      break;
    }
    streak += 1;
    cursor.setUTCDate(cursor.getUTCDate() - 1);
  }
  return streak;
}

/**
 * Shared domain types. UI must not import from feature components for types.
 * Nothing in this file has side effects.
 */

export type CrewMember = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  role: string;
  initials: string;
  color: string;
  restingHeartRate: number;
};

export type BodyArea =
  | "Head"
  | "Heart"
  | "Arm"
  | "Skin"
  | "Abdomen"
  | "Legs"
  | "Radiation";

export type Metric =
  | "heart_rate"
  | "skin_temp"
  | "bp_systolic"
  | "bp_diastolic"
  | "glucose"
  | "sleep_minutes"
  | "mood"
  | "stress"
  | "rested"
  | "connected"
  | "motion";

/** Every number shows which lane it came from. Lanes are never merged. */
export type Lane = "manual" | "live" | "replay" | "synthetic";

export type ObservationContext =
  | "rest"
  | "post_exercise"
  | "post_meal"
  | "unknown";

export type Quality =
  | "ok"
  | "no_contact"
  | "stale"
  | "out_of_range"
  | "motion"
  | "not_measured";

export type Observation = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  metric: Metric;
  value: number | null;
  unit: string;
  /** When the body was actually measured. */
  observedAt: string;
  /** When the number reached the app. */
  enteredAt: string;
  lane: Lane;
  method: string;
  context: ObservationContext;
  rawValue?: string;
  quality: Quality;
  qualityReason: string | null;
  environment: string;
  procedureVersion: string;
  /** Optional sensor artifact hint used by the quality gate. */
  artifact?: "motion" | null;
};

export type GateResult = {
  quality: Quality;
  qualityReason: string | null;
  /** False when the value must stay out of charts, baselines and comparisons. */
  accepted: boolean;
};

export type StatusState =
  | "logged"
  | "attention"
  | "no_valid_reading"
  | "within_range"
  | "insufficient_history";

export type Comparison = {
  state: StatusState;
  label: string;
  detail: string;
  comparableCount: number;
  range: { low: number; high: number } | null;
};

export type SymptomOnset = "just_now" | "today" | "several_days";
export type SymptomTrend = "better" | "same" | "worse";

export type SymptomEntry = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  onset: SymptomOnset;
  trend: SymptomTrend;
  /** True only for the demo list of symptoms that must never wait for a save. */
  urgent: boolean;
};

export type CheckIn = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  observedAt: string;
  enteredAt: string;
  sleepMinutes: number | null;
  mood: number | null;
  stress: number | null;
  rested: number | null;
  connected: number | null;
  symptoms: SymptomEntry[];
  details: string;
};

export type Concern = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  title: string;
  /** Plain, non-diagnostic sentence: "These changed together. No cause assigned." */
  summary: string;
  signals: Array<{
    metric: Metric | "symptom";
    text: string;
    observedAt: string;
  }>;
  hazards: HazardTag[];
  windowHours: number;
};

export type HazardTag =
  | "isolation"
  | "gravity"
  | "distance"
  | "radiation"
  | "environment";

export type EncounterKind = "assessment" | "emergency" | "daily";

export type Encounter = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  kind: EncounterKind;
  pathwayId: string;
  startedAt: string;
  endedAt: string | null;
  symptoms: SymptomEntry[];
  answers: Array<{
    stepId: string;
    question: string;
    answer: string;
    at: string;
  }>;
  measurements: Observation[];
  actions: Array<{ text: string; at: string; timerMinutes?: number }>;
  unknowns: string[];
  outcome: "open" | "closed" | "handoff" | "emergency" | "incomplete";
  illustrative: true;
};

export type Handoff = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  encounterId: string | null;
  symptoms: SymptomEntry[];
  observations: Observation[];
  trends: Array<{ metric: Metric; text: string }>;
  actions: Array<{ text: string; at: string }>;
  unknowns: string[];
  includePrivate: boolean;
  notes: string;
  recipients: string[];
};

export type LinkState = "live" | "delayed" | "blackout";

export type EnvironmentKey =
  | "mars_transit"
  | "station"
  | "lunar_base"
  | "rover"
  | "remote_clinic";

export type OutboxState =
  | "queued"
  | "sent"
  | "delivered"
  | "acknowledged";

export type OutboxItem = {
  id: string;
  crewId: string;
  kind: "handoff" | "support" | "report";
  createdAt: string;
  sentAt: string | null;
  acknowledgedAt: string | null;
  state: OutboxState;
  /** One-way estimate in seconds for the environment selected at send time. */
  delaySeconds: number;
  title: string;
  payload: Handoff | Record<string, unknown>;
  private: boolean;
  history: Array<{ state: OutboxState; at: string; note: string }>;
};

export type EquipmentKey =
  | "bp_cuff"
  | "thermometer"
  | "pulse_oximeter"
  | "glucometer"
  | "ecg"
  | "ultrasound";

export type MissionConfig = {
  missionName: string;
  missionDay: number;
  environment: EnvironmentKey;
  crew: CrewMember[];
  equipment: EquipmentKey[];
  procedureVersion: string;
};

export type PathwayOption = "Yes" | "No" | "Unsure" | "Unable to assess";

export type PathwayStep =
  | {
      id: string;
      type: "question";
      text: string;
      options: string[];
      next?: Record<string, string>;
    }
  | {
      id: string;
      type: "urgent_check";
      text: string;
      options: string[];
      next: Record<string, string>;
    }
  | {
      id: string;
      type: "measure";
      metric: Metric;
      needs: EquipmentKey | null;
      alt?: string;
      text: string;
      next: Record<string, string>;
    }
  | {
      id: string;
      type: "action";
      text: string;
      timer_minutes?: number;
      next?: Record<string, string>;
    }
  | {
      id: string;
      type: "note";
      text: string;
      next: Record<string, string>;
    };

export type Pathway = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  title: string;
  category?: "assessment" | "emergency";
  triggers: string[];
  steps: PathwayStep[];
  reassess?: {
    after_minutes: number;
    question: string;
    options: string[];
    next: Record<string, string>;
  };
};

export type DemoRule = {
  id: string;
  metric: Metric;
  why: string;
  source: string;
  low: number;
  high: number;
};

/** Legacy shape kept for the seeded sensor charts. */
export type LiveVitals = {
  heartRate: number;
  skinTemperature: number;
  motion: number;
  systolic: number;
  diastolic: number;
  updatedAt: Record<"heartRate" | "skinTemperature" | "bloodPressure" | "motion", string>;
};

export type SensorHistories = Record<
  "heartRate" | "skinTemperature" | "bloodPressure" | "motion",
  Array<number | null>
>;

export type HistoricalDay = {
  dayOffset: number;
  date: string;
  sleepMinutes: number | null;
  restingHeartRate: number;
  skinTemperature: number;
  systolic: number | null;
  diastolic: number | null;
  bloodSugar: number | null;
};

export type HistoricalCheckIn = {
  dayOffset: number;
  date: string;
  mood: number;
  stress: number;
  rested: number;
  connected: number;
};

export type TimelineEvent = {
  label: string;
  time: string;
  kind: "exercise" | "meal" | "check-in" | "symptoms" | "measurement";
};

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {

}

 FILE: frontend/public/sw.js
 lines: 51
================================================================
/* CrewSignal app-shell cache. Offline-first: the shell and emergency route
   must open without a network. Nothing here sends health data anywhere. */
const CACHE = "crewsignal-shell-v1";
const SHELL = ["/", "/emergency", "/manifest.webmanifest"];

self.addEventListener("install", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE).then((cache) => cache.addAll(SHELL)).catch(() => undefined),
  );
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches
      .keys()
      .then((keys) =>
        Promise.all(keys.filter((key) => key !== CACHE).map((key) => caches.delete(key))),
      ),
  );
  self.clients.claim();
});

self.addEventListener("fetch", (event) => {
  const request = event.request;
  if (request.method !== "GET" || !request.url.startsWith(self.location.origin)) return;

  if (request.mode === "navigate") {
    event.respondWith(
      fetch(request).catch(async () => {
        const cache = await caches.open(CACHE);
        return (await cache.match(request)) || (await cache.match("/emergency")) || Response.error();
      }),
    );
    return;
  }

  event.respondWith(
    caches.match(request).then(
      (cached) =>
        cached ||
        fetch(request)
          .then((response) => {
            const copy = response.clone();
            caches.open(CACHE).then((cache) => cache.put(request, copy)).catch(() => undefined);
            return response;
          })
          .catch(() => Response.error()),
    ),
  );
});
